\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{m-1}n^{2}(m+n)|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(m+n)}+\sum_{n=1}^{m-1}n^{2}(m-n)|b_{-n}|^{2}b^{2(m-n)}+8m^{3}|b_{m}|^{2}b^{4m}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4m}\right)

\displaystyle+m^{2}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}(m+n)|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(m+n)}+m^{2}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}(n-m)|b_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-m)}}\leq\frac{1}{8\pi}\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}u\right)^{2}dx.

(2.63)

Notice that we could control all
b_{n}
frequencies except for
b_{-m}
and
b_{0}
.

Step 5.
Estimation of the
L^{2}
norm of
\mathfrak{L}_{m}u
.

Then, consider the following quantity

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}|z|^{2-2m}\left|\partial_{z}^{2}v\right|^{2}|dz|^{2}.

If
v=|z|^{m-1}u=(z\overline{z})^{\frac{m-1}{2}}u
, we get

\displaystyle\partial_{z}v

\displaystyle=|z|^{m-1}\partial_{z}u+\frac{m-1}{2}\overline{z}|z|^{m-3}u

\displaystyle\partial_{z}^{2}v

\displaystyle=|z|^{m-1}\partial_{z}^{2}u+(m-1)\overline{z}|z|^{m-3}u+\frac{(m-1)(m-3)}{4}\overline{z}^{2}|z|^{m-5}u

\displaystyle=|z|^{m-1}\left(\partial_{z}^{2}u+\frac{m-1}{z}\partial_{z}u+\frac{(m-1)(m-3)}{4z^{2}}u\right).

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}|z|^{2-2m}\left|\partial_{z}^{2}v\right|^{2}|dz|^{2}=\int_{\Omega}\left|\partial_{z}^{2}u+\frac{m-1}{z}\partial_{z}u+\frac{(m-1)(m-3)}{4z^{2}}u\right|^{2}|dz|^{2},

which justifies the above choice in the inequality. We therefore define the complex elliptic operator (acting on real functions) with regular singularities

\displaystyle\mathfrak{L}_{m}=\partial_{z}^{2}+\frac{m-1}{z}\partial_{z}+\frac{(m-1)(m-3)}{4z^{2}}.

(2.64)

Recall that

\displaystyle u(z)

\displaystyle=|z|^{1-m}\left(\alpha_{1}\log|z|+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}a_{n}z^{n}\right)\right)+|z|^{m+1}\left(\alpha_{2}\log|z|+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}b_{n}z^{n}\right)\right).

We get

\displaystyle\partial_{z}u

\displaystyle=\frac{1-m}{2}\overline{z}|z|^{-m-1}\left(\alpha_{1}\log|z|+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}a_{n}z^{n}\right)\right)+|z|^{1-m}\left(\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2z}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}n\,a_{n}z^{n-1}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{m+1}{2}\overline{z}|z|^{m-1}\left(\alpha_{2}\log|z|+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}b_{n}z^{n}\right)\right)+|z|^{m+1}\left(\frac{\alpha_{2}}{2z}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}n\,b_{n}z^{n-1}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1-m}{2}|z|^{1-m}\frac{1}{z}\left(\alpha_{1}\log|z|+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}a_{n}z^{n}\right)\right)+|z|^{1-m}\left(\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2z}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}n\,a_{n}z^{n-1}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{m+1}{2}|z|^{m+1}\frac{1}{z}\left(\alpha_{2}\log|z|+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}b_{n}z^{n}\right)\right)+|z|^{m+1}\left(\frac{\alpha_{2}}{2z}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}n\,b_{n}z^{n-1}\right)

and

\displaystyle\partial_{z}^{2}u

\displaystyle=\frac{m^{2}-1}{4}\overline{z}^{2}|z|^{-m-3}\left(\alpha_{1}\log|z|+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}a_{n}z^{n}\right)\right)+(1-m)\overline{z}|z|^{-m-1}\left(\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2z}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}n\,a_{n}z^{n-1}\right)

\displaystyle+|z|^{1-m}\left(-\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2z^{2}}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}n(n-1)a_{n}z^{n-2}\right)+\frac{m^{2}-1}{4}\overline{z}^{2}|z|^{m-3}\left(\alpha_{2}\log|z|+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}b_{n}z^{n}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+(m+1)\overline{z}|z|^{m-1}\left(\frac{\alpha_{2}}{2z}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}n\,b_{n}z^{n-1}\right)+|z|^{m+1}\left(-\frac{\alpha_{2}}{2z^{2}}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}n(n-1)b_{n}\,z^{n-2}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{m^{2}-1}{4}|z|^{1-m}\frac{1}{z^{2}}\left(\alpha_{1}\log|z|+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}a_{n}z^{n}\right)\right)+(1-m)|z|^{1-m}\left(\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2z^{2}}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}n\,a_{n}z^{n-2}\right)

\displaystyle+|z|^{1-m}\left(-\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2z^{2}}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}n(n-1)a_{n}z^{n-2}\right)+\frac{m^{2}-1}{4}|z|^{m+1}\frac{1}{z^{2}}\left(\alpha_{2}\log|z|+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}b_{n}z^{n}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+(m+1)|z|^{m+1}\left(\frac{\alpha_{2}}{2z^{2}}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}n\,b_{n}z^{n-2}\right)+|z|^{m+1}\left(-\frac{\alpha_{2}}{2z^{2}}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}n(n-1)b_{n}z^{n-2}\right).

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\mathfrak{L}_{m}u

\displaystyle=\partial_{z}^{2}u+\frac{m-1}{z}\partial_{z}u+\frac{(m-1)(m-3)}{4z^{2}}u

\displaystyle=\frac{m^{2}-1}{4}|z|^{1-m}\frac{1}{z^{2}}\left(\alpha_{1}\log|z|+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}a_{n}z^{n}\right)\right)+\cancel{(1-m)|z|^{1-m}\left(\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2z^{2}}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}n\,a_{n}z^{n-2}\right)}

\displaystyle+|z|^{1-m}\left(-\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2z^{2}}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}n(n-1)a_{n}z^{n-2}\right)+\frac{m^{2}-1}{4}|z|^{m+1}\frac{1}{z^{2}}\left(\alpha_{2}\log|z|+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}b_{n}z^{n}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+(m+1)|z|^{m+1}\left(\frac{\alpha_{2}}{2z^{2}}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}n\,b_{n}z^{n-2}\right)+|z|^{m+1}\left(-\frac{\alpha_{2}}{2z^{2}}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}n(n-1)b_{n}z^{n-2}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{m^{2}-1}{2}|z|^{1-m}\frac{1}{z^{2}}\left(\alpha_{1}\log|z|+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}a_{n}z^{n}\right)\right)+\cancel{(m-1)|z|^{1-m}\left(\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2z^{2}}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}n\,a_{n}z^{n-2}\right)}

\displaystyle+\frac{m^{2}-1}{2}|z|^{m+1}\frac{1}{z^{2}}\left(\alpha_{2}\log|z|+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}b_{n}z^{n}\right)\right)+(m-1)|z|^{m+1}\left(\frac{\alpha_{2}}{2z^{2}}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}n\,b_{n}z^{n-2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{m^{2}-4m+3}{4}|z|^{1-m}\frac{1}{z^{2}}\left(\alpha_{1}\log|z|+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}a_{n}z^{n}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{m^{2}-4m+3}{4}|z|^{m+1}\frac{1}{z^{2}}\left(\alpha_{2}\log|z|+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}b_{n}z^{n}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=-(m-1)|z|^{1-m}\frac{1}{z^{2}}\left(\alpha_{1}\log|z|+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}a_{n}z^{n}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+m(m-1)|z|^{m+1}\frac{1}{z^{2}}\left(\alpha_{2}\log|z|+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}b_{n}z^{n}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+|z|^{1-m}\left(-\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2z^{2}}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}n(n-1)a_{n}z^{n-2}\right)+|z|^{m+1}\left(m\frac{\alpha_{2}}{z^{2}}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}n(2m+n-1)b_{n}z^{n-2}\right).

We can rewrite this expression more simply as

\displaystyle\mathfrak{L}_{m}u

\displaystyle=|z|^{1-m}\left(\alpha_{1}\frac{\log|z|}{z^{2}}-\frac{\alpha_{1}}{z^{2}}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\left(n(n-1)-(m-1)\right)a_{n}z^{n-2}-\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}(m-1)\overline{a_{n}}\frac{\overline{z}^{n}}{z^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle+|z|^{m+1}\left(m(m-1)\alpha_{2}\frac{\log|z|}{z^{2}}+m\frac{\alpha_{2}}{z^{2}}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\left(n(2m+n-1)+m(m-1)\right)b_{n}z^{n-2}+m(m-1)\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\overline{b_{n}}\frac{\overline{z}^{n}}{z^{2}}\right).

Therefore, we get

\displaystyle\mathrm{Rad}\left(|\mathfrak{L}_{m}u|^{2}\right)=|z|^{2-2m}\left(\alpha_{1}^{2}\frac{\log^{2}|z|}{|z|^{4}}+\frac{\alpha_{1}^{2}}{|z|^{4}}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\left(\left(n(n-1)-(m-1)\right)^{2}+(m-1)^{2}\right)|a_{n}|^{2}|z|^{2n-4}\right.

\displaystyle\left.-2\alpha_{1}^{2}\frac{\log|z|}{|z|^{4}}-4(m-1)\alpha_{1}a_{0}\frac{\log|z|}{|z|^{4}}+4(m-1)\frac{\alpha_{2}a_{0}}{|z|^{4}}-2(m-1)\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\left(n(n-1)-(m-1)\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{a_{-n}}\right)\frac{1}{|z|^{4}}\right)

\displaystyle+|z|^{2m+2}\left(m^{2}(m-1)^{2}\alpha_{2}^{2}\frac{\log^{2}|z|}{|z|^{4}}+m^{2}\frac{\alpha_{2}^{2}}{|z|^{4}}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\left(\left(n(2m+n-1)+m(m-1)\right)^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}\right)|b_{n}|^{2}|z|^{2n-4}\right.

\displaystyle+2m^{2}(m-1)\alpha_{2}^{2}\frac{\log|z|}{|z|^{4}}+4m^{2}(m-1)^{2}\alpha_{2}b_{0}\frac{\log|z|}{|z|^{4}}+4m^{2}(m-1)\frac{\alpha_{2}b_{0}}{|z|^{4}}

\displaystyle\left.+2m(m-1)\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\left(n(2m+n-1)+m(m-1)\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}\overline{b_{-n}}\right)\frac{1}{|z|^{4}}\right)

\displaystyle+2m(m-1)\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}\frac{\log^{2}|z|}{|z|^{2}}-m(m-3)\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}\frac{\log|z|}{|z|^{2}}-2m\frac{\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}}{|z|^{2}}+\left(4m(m-1)\alpha_{1}b_{0}-4m(m-1)^{2}\alpha_{2}a_{0}\right)\frac{\log|z|}{|z|^{2}}

\displaystyle-\left(4m(m-1)\alpha_{1}b_{0}+4m(m-1)\alpha_{2}a_{0}\right)\frac{1}{|z|^{2}}

\displaystyle+2\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\Big\{\left(n(n-1)-(m-1)\right)\left(n(2m+n-1)+m(m-1)\right)-m(m-1)^{2}\Big\}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)|z|^{2n-2}

\displaystyle+2\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\left(m(m-1)\left(n(n-1)-(m-1)\right)+(m-1)\left(n(n-2m-1)-m(m-1)\right)\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{-n}}\right)\frac{1}{|z|^{2}}.

Then, we have for
\alpha\neq-1

\displaystyle\int r^{\alpha}\log(r)dr

\displaystyle=\frac{r^{\alpha+1}}{\alpha+1}\log(r)-\frac{1}{\alpha+1}\int r^{\alpha}dr=\frac{r^{\alpha+1}}{\alpha+1}\log(r)-\frac{1}{(\alpha+1)^{2}}r^{\alpha+1}

\displaystyle\int r^{\alpha}\log^{2}(r)dr

\displaystyle=\frac{r^{\alpha+1}}{\alpha+1}\log^{2}(r)-\frac{2}{(\alpha+1)^{2}}r^{\alpha+1}\log(r)+\frac{2}{(\alpha+1)^{3}}r^{\alpha+1},

and for all
\beta>0

\displaystyle\int\frac{\log^{\beta}(r)}{r}dr=\frac{1}{\beta+1}\log^{\beta+1}(r).

Therefore, we get

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\left|\mathfrak{L}_{m}u\right|^{2}|dz|^{2}=2\pi\int_{a}^{b}\bigg(\alpha_{1}^{2}r^{-2m-1}\log^{2}(r)+\alpha_{1}^{2}r^{-2m-1}

\displaystyle+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\left(\left(n(n-1)-(m-1)^{2}\right)^{2}+(m-1)^{2}\right)|a_{n}|^{2}r^{2n-2m-1}-2\alpha_{1}^{2}r^{-2m-1}\log(r)-4(m-1)\alpha_{1}a_{0}r^{-2m-1}

\displaystyle-2(m-1)\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\left(n(n-1)-(m-1)\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{a_{-n}}\right)r^{-2m-1}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}\alpha_{2}^{2}r^{2m-1}\log^{2}(r)+m^{2}\alpha^{2}_{2}r^{2m-1}

\displaystyle+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\left(\left(n(2m+n-1)+m(m-1)\right)^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}\right)|b_{n}|^{2}r^{2n+2m-1}

\displaystyle+2m^{2}(m-1)\alpha_{2}^{2}r^{2m-1}\log(r)+4m^{2}(m-1)^{2}\alpha_{2}b_{0}r^{2m-1}\log(r)+4m^{2}(m-1)r^{2m-1}\alpha_{2}b_{0}

\displaystyle+2m(m-1)\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\left(n(2m+n-1)+m(m-1)\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}\overline{b_{-n}}\right)r^{2m-1}

\displaystyle+2m(m-1)\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}r^{-1}\log^{2}(r)-m(m-3)\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}r^{-1}\log(r)-2m\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}r^{-1}

\displaystyle+4m(m-1)\left(\alpha_{1}b_{0}-(m-1)\alpha_{2}a_{0}\right)r^{-1}\log(r)-4m(m-1)\left(\alpha_{1}b_{0}+\alpha_{2}a_{0}\right)r^{-1}

\displaystyle+2\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\Big\{\left(n(n-1)-(m-1)\right)\left(n(2m+n-1)+m(m-1)\right)-m(m-1)^{2}\Big\}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)r^{2n-1}

\displaystyle+2\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\left(m(m-1)\left(n(n-1)-(m-1)\right)+(m-1)\left(n(n-2m-1)-m(m-1)\right)\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{-n}}\right)r^{-1}\bigg)dr

\displaystyle=2\pi\bigg(\frac{1}{2m}\alpha_{1}^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\log^{2}(a)-\frac{1}{m}\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)-\frac{1}{2m^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2m}}-\left(\frac{1}{b^{2m}}\log^{2}(b)+\frac{1}{m}\frac{1}{b^{2m}}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)+\frac{1}{2m^{2}}\frac{1}{b^{2m}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2m}\alpha_{1}^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}\frac{\left(n(n-1)-(m-1)\right)^{2}+(m-1)^{2}}{n-m}|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n-m)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-m)}\right)

\displaystyle+(m-1)^{2}\left((m-1)^{2}+1\right)|a_{m}|^{2}\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{n=1-m}^{\infty}\frac{\left(n(n+1)-(m-1)^{2}\right)^{2}+(m-1)^{2}}{n+m}|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{m}\alpha_{1}^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)+\frac{1}{2m}\frac{1}{a^{2m}}-\left(\frac{1}{b^{2m}}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)+\frac{1}{2m}\frac{1}{b^{2m}}\right)\right)-\frac{2(m-1)}{m}\alpha_{1}a_{0}\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{m-1}{m}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\left(n(n-1)-(m-1)\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{a_{-n}}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{m(m-1)^{2}}{2}\alpha_{2}^{2}\left(b^{2m}\log^{2}(b)+\frac{1}{m}b^{2m}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)+\frac{1}{2m^{2}}b^{2m}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)-\left(a^{2m}\log^{2}(a)+\frac{1}{m}a^{2m}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)+\frac{1}{2m^{2}}a^{2m}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{m}{2}\alpha_{2}^{2}b^{2m}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{n=1-m}^{\infty}\frac{\left(n(2m+n-1)+m(m-1)\right)^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}}{n+m}|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n+m)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m)}\right)

\displaystyle+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}|b_{-m}|^{2}\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}\frac{\left(n(n-2m-1)+m(m-1)\right)^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}}{n-m}|b_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-m)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-m)}\right)

\displaystyle-m(m-1)\alpha_{2}^{2}\left(b^{2m}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)+\frac{1}{2m}b^{2m}-\left(a^{2m}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)+\frac{1}{2m}a^{2m}\right)\right)

\displaystyle-2m(m-1)^{2}\alpha_{2}b_{0}\left(b^{2m}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)+\frac{1}{2m}b^{2m}-\left(a^{2m}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)+\frac{1}{2m}a^{2m}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+2m(m-1)\alpha_{0}b_{0}b^{2m}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)

\displaystyle+(m-1)\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\left(n(2m+n-1)+m(m-1)\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}\overline{b_{-n}}\right)b^{2m}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2m(m-1)}{3}\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}\left(\log^{3}(b)-\log^{3}(a)\right)+\frac{m(m-3)}{2}\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}\left(\log^{2}(b)-\log^{2}(a)\right)-2m\,\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)

\displaystyle-4m(m-1)\left(\alpha_{1}b_{0}-(m-1)\alpha_{2}a_{0}\right)\left(\log^{2}(b)-\log^{2}(a)\right)-4(m-1)\left(\alpha_{1}b_{0}+\alpha_{2}a_{0}\right)\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\frac{\left(n(n-1)-(m-1)\right)\left(n(2m+n-1)+m(m-1)\right)-m(m-1)^{2}}{n}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)b^{2n}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2n}\right)

\displaystyle-4m(m-1)^{2}a_{0}b_{0}\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\frac{\left(n(n+1)-(m-1)\right)\left(n(n-2m-1)+m(m-1)\right)-m(m-1)^{2}}{n}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-n}\overline{b_{-n}}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2n}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2n}\right)

\displaystyle+2\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\Big\{m(m-1)\left(n(n-1)-(m-1)\right)+(m-1)\left(n(n-2m-1)-m(m-1)\right)\Big\}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{-n}}\right)\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\bigg).

(2.65)

Let us first estimate the coefficients involving
a_{n}
since we have already estimated all coefficients involving
b_{n}
in the
L^{2}
norm of
\mathscr{L}_{m}u
.

Step 6. Estimation of the coefficients for
|n|\geq m+1
.

For all
n\geq m+1
, consider the following quantity

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\left(n(n-1)-(m-1)\right)^{2}+(m-1)^{2}}{n-m}|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n-m)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-m)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\frac{\left(n(2m+n-1)+m(m-1)\right)^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}}{n+m}|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n+m)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\left(n(n-1)-(m-1)\right)\left(n(2m+n-1)+m(m-1)\right)-m(m-1)^{2}}{n}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)b^{2n}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2n}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m)}\right)}|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n-m)}\left(\frac{\left(n(n-1)-(m-1)\right)^{2}+(m-1)^{2}}{n-m}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-m)}\right)\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m)}\right)\right.

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{\left(n(n-1)-(m-1)\right)\left(n(2m+n-1)+m(m-1)\right)-m(m-1)^{2}}{n}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\left.\times\frac{n+m}{\left(n(2m+n-1)+m(m-1)\right)^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2n}\right)^{2}\right).

Define

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} X&=n(2m+n-1)+m(m-1)\\
Y&=n(n-1)-(m-1).\end{aligned}\right.

We have

\displaystyle\frac{\left(n(n-1)-(m-1)\right)^{2}+(m-1)^{2}}{n-m}

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{\left(n(n-1)-(m-1)\right)\left(n(2m+n-1)+m(m-1)\right)-m(m-1)^{2}}{n}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\times\frac{n+m}{\left(n(2m+n-1)+m(m-1)\right)^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{Y^{2}+(m-1)^{2}}{n-m}-\frac{(XY-m(m-1)^{2})^{2}}{n^{2}}\frac{n+m}{X^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{2}(n-m)\left(X^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}\right)}\bigg(n^{2}\left(X^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}\right)\left(Y^{2}+(m-1)^{2}\right)-\left(n^{2}-m^{2}\right)\left(XY-m(m-1)^{2}\right)^{2}\bigg),

and

\displaystyle n^{2}\left(X^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}\right)\left(Y^{2}+(m-1)^{2}\right)-\left(n^{2}-m^{2}\right)\left(XY-m(m-1)^{2}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=n^{2}X^{2}Y^{2}+(m-1)^{2}n^{2}X^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}Y^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{4}n^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(n^{2}-m^{2}\right)\left(X^{2}Y^{2}-2m(m-1)XY+m^{2}(m-1)^{4}\right)

\displaystyle=m^{2}X^{2}Y^{2}+(m-1)^{2}n^{2}X^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}Y^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{4}n^{2}+2m(m-1)^{2}\left(n^{2}-m^{2}\right)XY

\displaystyle+m^{4}(m-1)^{4}\geq m^{2}X^{2}Y^{2}.

Now, we have

\displaystyle X=n^{2}+(2m-1)n+m(m-1)\geq n^{2}.

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle Y=n^{2}-n-(m-1),

which implies that
Y\geq\frac{1}{2}n^{2}
if and only if

n^{2}-2n-2(m-1)\geq 0
,
which is equivalent to

\displaystyle n\geq 1+\sqrt{2m-1}.

Since
n\geq m+1
and
m+1\geq 1+\sqrt{2m-1}
, the condition is always verified and we finally get

\displaystyle n^{2}\left(X^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}\right)\left(Y^{2}+(m-1)^{2}\right)-\left(n^{2}-m^{2}\right)\left(XY-m(m-1)\right)^{2}\geq\frac{m^{2}}{4}n^{8}.

Since
n\geq m+1
, we have

\displaystyle X=n(2m+n-1)+m(m-1)\leq n(3n-3)+(n-1)(n-2)\leq 4n^{2},

which implies that

\displaystyle n^{2}(n-m)\left(X^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}\right)\leq n^{3}\left(16n^{4}+(n-1)^{2}(n-2)^{2}\right)\leq 17n^{7},

and finally

\displaystyle\frac{\left(n(n-1)-(m-1)\right)^{2}+(m-1)^{2}}{n-m}

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{\left(n(n-1)-(m-1)\right)\left(n(2m+n-1)+m(m-1)\right)-m(m-1)^{2}}{n}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\times\frac{n+m}{\left(n(2m+n-1)+m(m-1)\right)^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}}\geq\frac{m^{2}}{68}n.

(2.66)

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\left(n(n-1)-(m-1)\right)^{2}+(m-1)^{2}}{n-m}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-m)}\right)\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m)}\right)

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{\left(n(n-1)-(m-1)\right)\left(n(2m+n-1)+m(m-1)\right)-m(m-1)^{2}}{n}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\times\frac{n+m}{\left(n(2m+n-1)+m(m-1)\right)^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2n}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{m^{2}}{68}n-\frac{2\left(\left(n(n-1)-(m-1)\right)^{2}+(m-1)^{2}\right)}{n-m}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-m)}.

We now look for a condition on the conformal class so that

\displaystyle\frac{2\left(\left(n(n-1)-(m-1)\right)^{2}+(m-1)^{2}\right)}{n-m}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-m)}\leq\frac{1}{68}-\frac{1}{100},

or

\displaystyle\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-m)}\leq\frac{1}{425}\frac{n-m}{\left(n(n-1)-(m-1)\right)^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}},

or

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2(n-m)}\log\left(\frac{425\left(\left(n(n-1)-(m-1)\right)^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}\right)}{n-m}\right).

Since the right-hand side converges to
0
as
n\rightarrow\infty
, this quantity is bounded in
n\geq m+1
.
Let

\displaystyle f(x)=\frac{1}{x}\log\left(\frac{425\left(\left(x^{2}+(2m-1)x+(m-1)^{2}\right)^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}\right)^{2}}{x}\right)

for
x\geq 1
. We have

\displaystyle\left(x^{2}+(2m-1)x+(m-1)^{2}\right)^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}\leq(m^{2}+1)^{2}x^{4}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}x^{4},

which yields

\displaystyle f(x)\leq\frac{1}{x}\log\left(425\left((m^{2}+1)^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}\right)x^{3}\right)\leq\frac{1}{3e}+\log\left(425\left((m^{2}+1)^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}\right)^{2}\right),

since

\displaystyle\max_{x\geq 1}\frac{\log(x)}{x}=\frac{1}{e}

and this function is strictly decreasing. Finally, the condition on the conformal class holds provided that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{3e}+\log\left(425\left((m^{2}+1)^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}\right)^{2}\right).

(2.67)

If the condition is satisfied, we get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\left(n(n-1)-(m-1)\right)^{2}+(m-1)^{2}}{n-m}|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n-m)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-m)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\frac{\left(n(2m+n-1)+m(m-1)\right)^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}}{n+m}|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n+m)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\left(n(n-1)-(m-1)\right)\left(n(2m+n-1)+m(m-1)\right)-m(m-1)^{2}}{n}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)b^{2n}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2n}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{m^{2}}{200}n|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n-m)}.

(2.68)

Then, for
n\geq m+1
, consider

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\left(n(n+1)-(m-1)\right)^{2}}{n+m}|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\frac{\left(n(n-2m-1)+m(m-1)\right)^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}}{n-m}|b_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-m)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-m)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\left(n(n+1)-(m-1)\right)\left(n(n-2m-1)+m(m-1)\right)-m(m-1)^{2}}{n}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-n}\overline{b_{-n}}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2n}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2n}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2}\frac{\left(n(n+1)-(m-1)\right)^{2}}{n+m}|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m)}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{\left(n(n+1)-(m-1)\right)\left(n(n-2m-1)+m(m-1)\right)-m(m-1)^{2}}{n}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\times\frac{n-m}{\left(n(n-2m-1)+m(m-1)\right)^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}}|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m)}}\frac{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2n}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-m)}}.

Define as above

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} X&=n(n-2m-1)+m(m-1)\\
Y&=n(n+1)-(m-1).\end{aligned}\right.

Then we have

\displaystyle\frac{\left(n(n+1)-(m-1)\right)^{2}}{n+m}

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{\left(n(n+1)-(m-1)\right)\left(n(n-2m-1)+m(m-1)\right)-m(m-1)^{2}}{n}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\times\frac{n-m}{\left(n(n-2m-1)+m(m-1)\right)^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{Y^{2}}{n+m}-\frac{\left(XY-m(m-1)^{2}\right)^{2}}{n^{2}}\frac{n-m}{X^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{2}(n+m)\left(X^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}\right)}\left(m^{2}X^{2}Y^{2}+(m-1)^{2}n^{2}X^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}Y^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{4}n^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+2m(m-1)^{2}\left(n^{2}-m^{2}\right)XY+m^{4}(m-1)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{m^{2}X^{2}Y^{2}+m^{4}(m-1)^{2}}{n^{2}(n+m)\left(X^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}\right)}.

Notice that
X=0
if

\displaystyle n=m+\frac{1+\sqrt{8m+1}}{2}.

We see that for
n\geq 2m+1
, we have

\displaystyle X\geq m(m-1),

and for
n\geq 3m
, we easily get

\displaystyle X\geq\frac{m-1}{3m}n^{2}.

On the other hand, since
n\geq m+1
, we have
Y\geq n^{2}
. Then, we trivially estimate for all
m+1\leq n\leq 3m-1

\displaystyle m^{4}(m-1)^{2}\geq\frac{m^{4}(m-1)^{2}}{(3m-1)^{7}}n^{7}.

Therefore, we finally get that for all
n\geq m+1
, we have

\displaystyle m^{2}X^{2}Y^{2}+m^{4}(m-1)^{2}\geq\frac{m^{4}(m-1)^{2}}{(3m-1)^{7}}n^{7}.

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle n^{2}(n+m)(X^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2})\leq n^{2}(2n+1)(4n^{2})\leq 8n^{6},

and finally, we get

\displaystyle\frac{\left(n(n+1)-(m-1)\right)^{2}}{n+m}

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{\left(n(n+1)-(m-1)\right)\left(n(n-2m-1)+m(m-1)\right)-m(m-1)^{2}}{n}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\times\frac{n-m}{\left(n(n-2m-1)+m(m-1)\right)^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}}\geq\frac{m^{4}(m-1)^{2}}{8(3m-1)^{7}}n.

This condition implies that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\left(n(n+1)-(m-1)\right)^{2}}{n+m}|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m)}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{\left(n(n+1)-(m-1)\right)\left(n(n-2m-1)+m(m-1)\right)-m(m-1)^{2}}{n}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\times\frac{n-m}{\left(n(n-2m-1)+m(m-1)\right)^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}}|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m)}}\frac{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2n}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-m)}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-m)}\right)}|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m)}}\left(\frac{m^{4}(m-1)^{2}}{8(3m-1)^{7}}n-\frac{2\left((n(n+1)-(m-1)\right)^{2}}{n+m}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-m)}\right).

Therefore, we look for a condition on the conformal class such that

\displaystyle\frac{2\left((n(n+1)-(m-1)\right)^{2}}{n+m}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-m)}\leq\frac{m^{4}(m-1)^{2}}{16(3m-1)^{7}}n,

or that for all
n\geq m+1

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2(n-m)}\log\left(\frac{32(3m-1)^{7}}{m^{4}(m-1)^{2}}\frac{\left(n(n+1)-(m-1)\right)^{2}}{n(n+m)}\right).

Since

\displaystyle\frac{\left(n(n+1)-(m-1)\right)^{2}}{n(n+m)}\leq n^{2},

we deduce that the condition is satisfied for

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{e}+\frac{1}{2}\log\left(\frac{32(3m-1)^{7}}{m^{4}(m-1)^{2}}\right).

(2.69)

If this condition is satisfied, we get for all
n\geq m+1

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\left(n(n+1)-(m-1)\right)^{2}}{n+m}|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\frac{\left(n(n-2m-1)+m(m-1)\right)^{2}+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}}{n-m}|b_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-m)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-m)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\left(n(n+1)-(m-1)\right)\left(n(n-2m-1)+m(m-1)\right)-m(m-1)^{2}}{n}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-n}\overline{b_{-n}}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2n}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2n}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{m^{4}(m-1)^{2}}{32(3m-1)^{7}}\,n|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m)}}.

(2.70)

Thanks to (
2.3.2
), (
2.3.2
), and (
2.3.2
), we deduce that

\displaystyle\frac{m^{2}}{200}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}n|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n-m)}+\frac{m^{4}(m-1)^{2}}{32(3m-1)^{7}}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}n|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m)}}

\displaystyle-\frac{(m-1)}{m}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}\left(n(n-1)-(m-1)\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{a_{-n}}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{(m-1)}{m}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}\left(n(n+1)-(m-1)\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{a_{-n}}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)

\displaystyle+2\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}\Big\{m(m-1)\left(n(n-1)-(m-1)\right)+(m-1)\left(n(n-2m-1)-m(m-1)\right)\Big\}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{-n}}\right)\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)

\displaystyle+2\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}\Big\{m(m-1)\left(n(n+1)-(m-1)\right)+(m-1)\left(n(n+2m+1)-m(m-1)\right)\Big\}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{\Omega}\left|\mathfrak{L}_{m}u\right|^{2}|dz|^{2}.

On the other hand, we have by (
2.3.2
)

\displaystyle 4\,m^{2}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}(m+n)|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n+m)}+4\,m^{2}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}(n-m)|b_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-m)}}\leq\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}u\right)^{2}dx.

We estimate

\displaystyle-\frac{2(m-1)}{m}\left(n^{2}-(m-1)\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{a_{-n}}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\geq-\frac{64(3m-1)^{7}}{m^{6}}n^{3}|a_{n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n)}}-\frac{m^{4}(m-1)^{2}}{64(3m-1)^{7}}\,n|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m)}}.

Therefore, we get

\displaystyle\frac{m^{2}}{200}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}n|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n-m)}+\frac{m^{4}(m-1)^{2}}{32(3m-1)^{7}}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}n|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m)}}

\displaystyle-\frac{(m-1)}{m}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}\left(n^{2}-(m-1)\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{a_{-n}}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{m^{2}}{200}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}n|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n-m)}\left(1-\frac{12800(3m-1)^{7}}{m^{8}}n^{2}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-m)}\right)+\frac{m^{4}(m-1)^{2}}{64(3m-1)^{7}}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}n|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m)}}.

We choose a conformal class such that for all
n\geq m+1
,

\displaystyle\frac{12800(3m-1)^{7}}{m^{7}}n^{2}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-m)}\leq\frac{1}{2},

or

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2(n-m)}\log\left(\frac{25600(3m-1)^{7}}{m^{8}}n^{2}\right).

Therefore, the condition is satisfied if

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{e}+m+\frac{1}{2}\log\left(\frac{25600(3m-1)^{7}}{m^{8}}\right).

(2.71)

Indeed, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\log(n)}{n-m}=\frac{\log(n-m)}{n-m}+\frac{\log\left(1+\frac{m}{n-m}\right)}{n-m}\leq\frac{1}{e}+\frac{m}{(n-m)^{2}}\leq\frac{1}{e}+m,

where we used that

\displaystyle\sup_{x\geq 1}\frac{\log(x)}{x}=\frac{1}{e}\qquad\text{and}\;\,\log(1+x)\leq x\qquad\text{for all}\;\,x\geq 0.

If (
2.71
) is satisfied, we get

\displaystyle\frac{m^{2}}{200}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}n|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n-m)}+\frac{m^{4}(m-1)^{2}}{32(3m-1)^{7}}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}n|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m)}}

\displaystyle-\frac{2(m-1)}{m}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}\left(n^{2}-(m-1)\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{a_{-n}}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{m^{2}}{400}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}n|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n-m)}+\frac{m^{4}(m-1)^{2}}{64(3m-1)^{7}}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}n|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m)}}.

We have

\displaystyle\Big|m(m-1)\left(n(n-1)-(m-1)\right)+(m-1)\left(n(n-2m-1)-m(m-1)\right)\Big|\leq 4m(m-1)n^{2}.

Therefore, we get

\displaystyle 2\Big\{m(m-1)\left(n(n-1)-(m-1)\right)+(m-1)\left(n(n-2m-1)-m(m-1)\right)\Big\}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{-n}}\right)\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{8(m-1)^{2}n^{4}}{n-m}|a_{n}|^{2}a^{2(n-m)}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)-2m^{2}(n-m)|b_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-m)}}.

Now, we have

\displaystyle\frac{m^{2}}{400}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}\left(n|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n-m)}-\frac{8(m-1)^{2}n^{4}}{n-m}|a_{n}|^{2}a^{2(n-m)}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{m^{2}}{400}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}n|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n-m)}\left(1-\frac{3200(m-1)^{2}n^{3}}{n-m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-m)}\right).

Therefore, we look for a conformal class such that for all
n\geq m+1
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{3200(m-1)^{2}n^{3}}{n-m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-m)}\leq\frac{1}{2}.

(2.72)

Consider the function

\displaystyle f(x)=x\log^{2}(x)\qquad 0<x<1.

We have

\displaystyle f(x)=\log^{2}(x)+2\log(x)=\log(x)\left(\log(x)+2\right)=\log\left(\frac{1}{x}\right)\left(-2+\log\left(\frac{1}{x}\right)\right).

We deduce that
f
is strictly increasing on
]0,e^{-2}[
and strictly decreasing
]e^{-2},1[
. Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{0<x<1}f(x)=f(e^{-2})=4\,e^{-2}.

Therefore, we get

\displaystyle\frac{3200(m-1)^{2}n^{3}}{n-m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-m)}\leq\frac{12800(m-1)^{2}n^{3}}{e^{2}(n-m)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-m-\frac{1}{2})},

and (
2.72
) is satisfied provided that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2n-(2m+1)}\log\left(\frac{25600(m-1)^{2}n^{3}}{e^{2}(n-m)}\right).

Finally, we have for all
n\geq m+1

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2n-(2m+1)}

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{25600(m-1)^{2}n^{3}}{e^{2}(n-m)}\right)\leq\log\left(\frac{25600(m-1)^{2}}{e^{2}}\right)+\frac{3}{2n-(2m+1)}\log(n)

\displaystyle\leq\log\left(\frac{25600(m-1)^{2}}{e^{2}}\right)+\frac{3}{2e}+3(2m+1).

Indeed, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\log(n)}{2n-(2m+1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{n-(m+\frac{1}{2})+(m+\frac{1}{2})}{2n-(2m+1)}=\frac{\log(n-(m+\frac{1}{2}))}{2n-(2m+1)}+\frac{\log\left(1+\frac{2m+1}{2n-(2m+1)}\right)}{2n-(2m+1)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2e}+\frac{2m+1}{\left(2n-(2m+1)\right)^{2}}\leq\frac{1}{2e}+2m+1,

Therefore, provided that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\log\left(\frac{25600(m-1)^{2}}{e^{2}}\right)+\frac{3}{2e}+3(2m+1).

(2.73)

Assuming that this estimate holds, we deduce that

\displaystyle\frac{m^{2}}{200}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}n|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n-m)}+\frac{m^{4}(m-1)^{2}}{32(3m-1)^{7}}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}n|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m)}}

\displaystyle+4\,m^{2}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}(n-m)|b_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-m)}}

\displaystyle-\frac{2(m-1)}{m}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}\left(n^{2}-(m-1)\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{a_{-n}}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)

\displaystyle+2\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}\Big\{m(m-1)\left(n(n-1)-(m-1)\right)+(m-1)\left(n(n-2m-1)-m(m-1)\right)\Big\}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{-n}}\right)\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{m^{2}}{800}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}n|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n-m)}+\frac{m^{4}(m-1)^{2}}{64(3m-1)^{7}}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}n|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m)}}+2\,m^{2}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}(n-m)|b_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-m)}}.

Now, we estimate

\displaystyle m(m-1)\left(n(n+1)-(m-1)\right)+(m-1)\left(n(n+2m+1)-m(m-1)\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2m(m-1)n^{2}+4(m-1)n^{2}\leq 6m(m-1)n^{2},

which implies that

\displaystyle 2\Big\{m(m-1)\left(n(n+1)-(m-1)\right)+(m-1)\left(n(n+2m+1)-m(m-1)\right)\Big\}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{m^{4}(m-1)^{2}}{128(3m-1)^{7}}n|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{n+m)}}-\frac{4608(3m-1)^{7}n^{3}}{m^{2}}|b_{n}|^{2}a^{2(n+m)}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right).

We have

\displaystyle 4m^{2}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}(m+n)|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n+m)}-\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}\frac{4608(3m-1)^{7}n^{3}}{m^{2}}|b_{n}|^{2}a^{2(n+m)}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)

\displaystyle=4m^{2}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}(m+n)|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n+m)}\left(1-\frac{1152(3m-1)^{7}n^{3}}{m^{4}(n+m)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m)}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\right).

Therefore, we look for a condition on the conformal class such that

\displaystyle\frac{1152(3m-1)^{7}n^{3}}{m^{4}(n+m)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m)}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\leq\frac{1}{2}.

Using once more the inequality
x\log^{2}(x)\leq 4\,e^{-2}
for all
0<x<1
, the condition is verified if

\displaystyle\frac{1152(3m-1)^{7}n^{3}}{m^{4}(n+m)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m-\frac{1}{2})}\leq\frac{1}{2},

or

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2m+2n-1}\log\left(\frac{2304(3m-1)^{7}n^{3}}{m^{4}(n+m)}\right).

We have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2m+2n-1}\log\left(\frac{n^{3}}{n+m}\right)\leq\frac{1}{2m+2n-1}\log\left((2m+2n-1)^{2}\right)\leq\frac{2}{e},

so the new condition on the conformal class is given by

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{2}{e}+\frac{1}{4m+1}\log\left(\frac{2304(3m-1)^{7}}{m^{4}}\right).

(2.74)

If it is satisfied, we finally get

\displaystyle\frac{m^{2}}{200}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}n|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n-m)}+\frac{m^{4}(m-1)^{2}}{32(3m-1)^{7}}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}n|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m)}}

\displaystyle+4\,m^{2}\sum_{n=m+1}(n+m)|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n+m)}+4\,m^{2}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}(n-m)|b_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-m)}}

\displaystyle-\frac{2(m-1)}{m}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}\left(n^{2}-(m-1)\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{a_{-n}}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)

\displaystyle+2\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}\Big\{m(m-1)\left(n(n-1)-(m-1)\right)+(m-1)\left(n(n-2m-1)-m(m-1)\right)\Big\}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{-n}}\right)\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)

\displaystyle+2\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}\Big\{m(m-1)\left(n(n+1)-(m-1)\right)+(m-1)\left(n(n+2m+1)-m(m-1)\right)\Big\}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)

\displaystyle\geq 2\,m^{2}\sum_{n=m+1}(n+m)|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n+m)}+2\,m^{2}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}(n-m)|b_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-m)}}

\displaystyle+\frac{m^{2}}{800}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}n|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n-m)}+\frac{m^{4}(m-1)^{2}}{128(3m-1)^{7}}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}n|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m)}}.

Using (
2.3.2
) and (
2.3.2
), we finally get

\displaystyle 2\,m^{2}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}(n+m)|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n+m)}+2\,m^{2}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}(n-m)|b_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-m)}}

\displaystyle+\frac{m^{2}}{800}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}n|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n-m)}+\frac{m^{4}(m-1)^{2}}{128(3m-1)^{7}}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}n|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m)}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}u\right)^{2}dx+\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{\Omega}\left|\mathfrak{L}_{m}u\right|^{2}|dz|^{2}.

(2.75)

Step 7.
Now, recall that

\displaystyle u(r,\theta)=\alpha_{1}\,r^{1-m}\log(r)+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}a_{n}r^{1-m+n}e^{in\theta}\right)+\alpha_{2}r^{m+1}\log(r)+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}b_{n}r^{m+1+n}e^{in\theta}\right).

If
v=|x|^{m-1}u

\displaystyle|\nabla v|^{2}|x|^{2-2m}=\left|\nabla u+(m-1)\frac{x}{|x|^{2}}u\right|^{2},

while

\displaystyle|\partial_{r}v|^{2}|x|^{2-2m}=\left|\partial_{r}u+\frac{m-1}{r}u\right|^{2}\qquad\text{and}\qquad\left|\frac{1}{r}\partial_{\theta}v\right|^{2}|x|^{2-2m}=\frac{1}{r^{2}}|\partial_{\theta}u|^{2},

which readily shows that

\displaystyle\left|\nabla u+(m-1)\frac{x}{|x|^{2}}u\right|^{2}=\left|\partial_{r}u+\frac{m-1}{r}u\right|^{2}+\frac{1}{r^{2}}|\partial_{\theta}u|^{2}.

Now, we compute

\displaystyle\partial_{r}u

\displaystyle=(1-m)\alpha_{1}\,r^{-m}\log(r)+\alpha_{1}r^{-m}+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}(1-m+n)a_{n}r^{-m+n}e^{in\theta}\right)

\displaystyle+(m+1)\alpha_{2}\,r^{m}\log(r)+\alpha_{2}r^{m}+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}(m+1+n)b_{n}r^{m+n}e^{in\theta}\right),

which implies that

\displaystyle\partial_{r}u+\frac{m-1}{r}u

\displaystyle=\cancel{(1-m)\alpha_{1}\,r^{-m}\log(r)}+\alpha_{1}r^{-m}+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}(\cancel{(1-m)}+n)a_{n}r^{-m+n}e^{in\theta}\right)

\displaystyle+(m+1)\alpha_{2}\,r^{m}\log(r)+\alpha_{2}r^{m}+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}(m+1+n)b_{n}r^{m+n}e^{in\theta}\right)

\displaystyle+\cancel{(m-1)\alpha_{1}\,r^{-m}\log(r)}+\cancel{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}(m-1)a_{n}\,r^{-m+n}e^{in\theta}\right)}

\displaystyle+(m-1)\alpha_{2}\,r^{m}\log(r)+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}(m-1)b_{n}\,r^{m+n}e^{in\theta}\right)

\displaystyle=\alpha_{1}\,r^{-m}+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}n\,a_{n}\,r^{-m+n}e^{in\theta}\right)

\displaystyle+2m\,\alpha_{2}\,r^{m}\log(r)+\alpha_{2}\,r^{m}+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}(2m+n)b_{n}\,r^{m+n}e^{in\theta}\right).

On the other hand, we trivially have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{r}\partial_{\theta}u=2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}n\,a_{n}\,r^{-m+n}e^{in\theta}\right)+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}n\,b_{n}\,r^{m+n}e^{in\theta}\right).

Using the elementary inequality

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{i=1}^{d}z_{i}\right|^{2}\leq d\sum_{i=1}^{d}|z_{n}|^{2}\qquad\forall(z_{1},\cdots,z_{d})\in\mathbb{C}^{d},

we deduce that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Rad}\left(\left|\nabla u+(m-1)\frac{x}{|x|^{2}}u\right|^{2}\right)=\mathrm{Rad}\left(\left|\partial_{r}u+\frac{m-1}{r}u\right|^{2}+\frac{1}{r^{2}}|\partial_{\theta}u|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 5\alpha_{1}^{2}r^{-2m}+20\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}|n|^{2}|a_{n}|^{2}r^{-2m+2n}+20m^{2}\alpha_{2}^{2}r^{2m}\log^{2}(r)+5\alpha_{2}^{2}r^{2m}+20\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}(2m+n)^{2}|b_{n}|^{2}r^{2m+2n}

\displaystyle+8\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}n^{2}|a_{n}|^{2}r^{-2m+2n}+8\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}n^{2}|b_{n}|^{2}r^{2m+2n}

\displaystyle=5\alpha_{1}^{2}r^{-2m}+28\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}|n|^{2}|a_{n}|^{2}r^{-2m+2n}+20m^{2}\alpha_{2}^{2}r^{2m}\log^{2}(r)+5\alpha_{2}^{2}r^{2m}+4\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\left(5(2m+n)^{2}+2n^{2}\right)|b_{n}|^{2}r^{2m+2n}.

Therefore, we have for all
0<|\gamma|<1

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\frac{1}{|x|^{2}}\left|\nabla u+(m-1)\frac{x}{|x|^{2}}u\right|^{2}|x|^{2\gamma}dx\leq 2\pi\int_{a}^{b}\bigg(5\alpha_{1}^{2}r^{-2m-1+2\gamma}+28\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}|n|^{2}|a_{n}|^{2}r^{2n-2m-1+2\gamma}

\displaystyle+20m^{2}\alpha_{2}^{2}r^{2m-1+2\gamma}\log^{2}(r)+5\alpha_{2}^{2}r^{2m-1+2\gamma}+4\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\left(5(2m+n)^{2}+2n^{2}\right)|b_{n}|^{2}r^{2m+2n-1+2\gamma}\bigg)dr

\displaystyle=2\pi\bigg(\frac{5\alpha_{1}^{2}}{2(m-\gamma)}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-\gamma)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-\gamma)}\right)+14\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\frac{|n|^{2}}{|n-m+\gamma|}|a_{n}|^{2}\left|b^{2(n-m+\gamma)}-a^{2(n-m+\gamma)}\right|

\displaystyle+\frac{10m^{2}}{m+\gamma}\alpha_{2}^{2}\left(b^{2m+2\gamma}\log^{2}(b)+\frac{1}{m+\gamma}b^{2m+2\gamma}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)+\frac{1}{2(m+\gamma)^{2}}b^{2m+2\gamma}\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\left(a^{2m+2\gamma}\log^{2}(a)+\frac{1}{m+\gamma}a^{2m+2\gamma}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)+\frac{1}{2(m+\gamma)^{2}}a^{2m+2\gamma}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{5\alpha_{2}^{2}}{2(m+\gamma)}b^{2(m+\gamma)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+\gamma)}\right)+4\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\frac{5(2m+n)^{2}+2n^{2}}{|m+n+\gamma|}|b_{n}|^{2}\left|b^{2(n+m+\gamma)}-a^{2(n+m+\gamma)}\right|\bigg).

Assuming that
0<\gamma<1
, we deduce that

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\frac{1}{|x|^{2}}\left|\nabla u+(m-1)\frac{x}{|x|^{2}}u\right|^{2}|x|^{2\gamma}dx\leq 2\pi\bigg(\frac{5\alpha_{1}^{2}}{2(m-\gamma)}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-\gamma)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-\gamma)}\right)

\displaystyle+14\sum_{n=m}^{\infty}\frac{n^{2}}{n-m+\gamma}|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n-m+\gamma)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-m+\gamma)}\right)

\displaystyle+14\sum_{n=1-m}^{\infty}\frac{n^{2}}{n+m-\gamma}|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m-\gamma)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m-\gamma)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{10m^{2}}{m+\gamma}\alpha_{2}^{2}\left(b^{2m+2\gamma}\log^{2}(b)+\frac{1}{m+\gamma}b^{2m+2\gamma}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)+\frac{1}{2(m+\gamma)^{2}}b^{2m+2\gamma}\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\left(a^{2m+2\gamma}\log^{2}(a)+\frac{1}{m+\gamma}a^{2m+2\gamma}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)+\frac{1}{2(m+\gamma)^{2}}a^{2m+2\gamma}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{5\alpha_{2}^{2}}{2(m+\gamma)}b^{2(m+\gamma)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+\gamma)}\right)+4\sum_{n=-m}^{\infty}\frac{5(2m+n)^{2}+2n^{2}}{n+m+\gamma}|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n+m+\gamma)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m+\gamma)}\right)

\displaystyle+4\sum_{n=1-m}^{\infty}\frac{5(n-2m)^{2}+2n^{2}}{n-m-\gamma}|b_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-m-\gamma)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-m-\gamma)}\right)\bigg).

Now, recall (
2.3.2
).

\displaystyle\frac{m^{2}}{800}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}n|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n-m)}+\frac{m^{4}(m-1)^{2}}{128(3m-1)^{7}}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}n|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m)}}

\displaystyle+2\,m^{2}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}(n+m)|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n+m)}+2\,m^{2}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}(n-m)|b_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-m)}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}u\right)^{2}dx+\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{\Omega}\left|\mathfrak{L}_{m}u\right|^{2}|dz|^{2}.

(2.76)

We trivially estimate

\displaystyle 28\pi\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}\frac{n^{2}}{n-m+\gamma}b^{2(n-m+\gamma)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-m+\gamma)}\right)\leq 28\pi\,b^{2\gamma}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}n|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n-m)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{11200}{m^{2}}b^{2\gamma}\left(\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}u\right)^{2}dx+\int_{\Omega}|\mathfrak{L}_{m}u|^{2}|dz|^{2}\right).

Likewise, we have

\displaystyle 28\pi\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}\frac{n^{2}}{n+m-\gamma}|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m-\gamma)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m-\gamma)}\right)\leq\frac{1792(3m-1)^{7}}{m^{4}(m-1)^{2}}a^{2\gamma}\left(\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}u\right)^{2}dx+\int_{\Omega}|\mathfrak{L}_{m}u|^{2}|dz|^{2}\right).

Then, we readily obtain for all
n\geq m+1

\displaystyle 5(2m+n)^{2}+2n^{2}\leq 47n^{2},

which shows that

\displaystyle 8\pi\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}\frac{5(2m+n)^{2}+2n^{2}}{n+m+\gamma}|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n+m+\gamma)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m+\gamma)}\right)\leq 376\pi\,b^{2\gamma}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}(n+m)|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n+m)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{188}{m^{2}}b^{2\gamma}\left(\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}u\right)^{2}dx+\int_{\Omega}|\mathfrak{L}_{m}u|^{2}|dz|^{2}\right),

and likewise

\displaystyle 4\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}\frac{5(n-2m)^{2}+2n^{2}}{n-m-\gamma}|b_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-m-\gamma)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-m-\gamma)}\right)\leq\frac{188}{m^{2}}\left(\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}u\right)^{2}dx+\int_{\Omega}|\mathfrak{L}_{m}u|^{2}|dz|^{2}\right).

Therefore, we only have finitely many terms left to estimate in the series, and also the logarithm terms. We first estimate
a_{n}
for
1-m\leq n\leq m-1
.
Since
1<2\beta<2
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{|-m+n+2\beta|}\left|b^{-2m+2n+4\beta}-a^{-2m+2n+4\beta}\right|=\sum_{n=m-1}^{\infty}\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{n-m+2\beta}b^{2(n-m+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-m+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=2-m,n\neq 0}^{\infty}\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{n+m-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m-2\beta)}\right).

Likewise, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\frac{|b_{n}|^{2}}{|m+n+2\beta|}\left|b^{2m+2n+4\beta}-a^{2m+2n+4\beta}\right|=\sum_{n=-m-1,n\neq 0}^{\infty}\frac{|b_{n}|^{2}}{m+n+2\beta}b^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=m+2}^{\infty}\frac{|b_{-n}|^{2}}{n-m-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-m-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m-2\beta)}\right).

Then, we have

\displaystyle 2\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\frac{\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)}{n+2\beta}\left(b^{2n+4\beta}-a^{2n-2+4\beta}\right)=2\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\frac{\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)}{n+2\beta}b^{2(n+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+2\frac{\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-1}b_{-1}\right)}{2\beta-1}b^{2(2\beta-1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2\beta-1)}\right)+2\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\frac{\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-n}\overline{b_{-n}}\right)}{n-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-2\beta)}\right).

First, consider the quantity

\displaystyle\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{m-n-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\right)+\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{|b_{n}|^{2}}{m+n+2\beta}b^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{n+m-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n-2\beta)}\right)+\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{|b_{-n}|^{2}}{m-n+2\beta}b^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{m-2\beta}\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}a_{-n}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2m-4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m-4\beta}\right)+\frac{2}{m+2\beta}\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}b_{-n}\right)b^{2m+4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m+4\beta}\right).

We first have for all
2\leq n\leq m-2

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{m-n-2\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{m+n-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{m-2\beta}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}a_{-n}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2m-4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m-4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{m-n-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\right)-\frac{m+n-2\beta}{(m-2\beta)^{2}}|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n-2\beta)}}\frac{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m-4\beta}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n-2\beta)}}

\displaystyle=\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n-2\beta)}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n-2\beta)}}\left(\frac{1}{m-n-2\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\right)\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n-2\beta)}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\frac{m+n-2\beta}{(m-2\beta)^{2}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m-4\beta}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n-2\beta)}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n-2\beta)}}\left(\frac{n^{2}}{(m-n-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}-\frac{2}{m-n-2\beta}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\right),

where we used that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{m-n-2\beta}-\frac{m+n-2\beta}{(m-2\beta)^{2}}=\frac{(m-\beta)^{2}-\left((m-2\beta)^{2}-n^{2}\right)}{(m-n-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}=\frac{n^{2}}{(m-n-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}.

Therefore, we assume that

\displaystyle\frac{2}{m-n-2\beta}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\leq\frac{n^{2}}{2(m-n-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}},

which is equivalent to

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2(m-n-2\beta)}\log\left(\frac{4(m-2\beta)^{2}}{n^{2}}\right).

Since
2\leq n\leq m-2
, the condition is satisfied provided that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2(1-\beta)}\log\left(m-2\beta\right).

(2.77)

If this estimate is satisfied, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{m-n-2\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{m+n-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{m-2\beta}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}a_{-n}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2m-4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m-4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n^{2}}{2(m-n-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}|a_{n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n-2\beta)}}.

(2.78)

Likewise, we estimate

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{m-n-2\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{m+n-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{m-2\beta}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}a_{-n}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2m-4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m-4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n-2\beta)}}\left(\frac{n^{2}}{(m+n-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}-\frac{2}{m+n-2\beta}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\right).

Therefore, we impose the following condition on the conformal class

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2(m-n-2\beta)}\log\left(\frac{4(m-2\beta)^{2}}{n^{2}}\right),

that is satisfied thanks to (
2.77
). Therefore, we deduce that

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{m-n-2\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{m+n-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{m-2\beta}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}a_{-n}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2m-4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m-4\beta}\right)\geq\frac{n^{2}}{2(m+n-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n-2\beta)}}.

(2.79)

Therefore, combining (
2.3.2
) with (
2.3.2
), we deduce that

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{m-n-2\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{m+n-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{m-2\beta}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}a_{-n}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2m-4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m-4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n^{2}}{4(m-n-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}|a_{n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n-2\beta)}}+\frac{n^{2}}{4(m+n-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n-2\beta)}}.

(2.80)

Now, we estimate the terms involving
b_{n}
.
We have for
1\leq n\leq m

\displaystyle\frac{|b_{n}|^{2}}{m+n+2\beta}b^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|b_{-n}|^{2}}{m-n+2\beta}b^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}b_{-n}\right)}{m+2\beta}b^{2m+4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+4\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|b_{n}|^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n+2\beta)}}b^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\left(\frac{1}{m+n+2\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\right)\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\frac{m-n+2\beta}{(m+2\beta)^{2}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m+4\beta}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|b_{n}|^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n+2\beta)}}b^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\left(\frac{n^{2}}{(m+n+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}-\frac{2}{m+n+2\beta}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\right).

Therefore, we impose the condition

\displaystyle\frac{2}{m+n+2\beta}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\leq\frac{n^{2}}{(m+n+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}},

which yields the condition

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2(m-n+2\beta)}\log\left(\frac{4n^{2}}{(m+2\beta)^{2}}\right),

which is satisfied provided that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2\beta}\log\left(\frac{2m}{m+2\beta}\right).

(2.81)

Therefore, we get

\displaystyle\frac{|b_{n}|^{2}}{m+n+2\beta}b^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|b_{-n}|^{2}}{m-n+2\beta}b^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}b_{-n}\right)}{m+2\beta}b^{2m+4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+4\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n^{2}}{2(m+n+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(m+n+2\beta)}.

(2.82)

The same argument (that holds when the same condition on the conformal class is satisfied) also shows that

\displaystyle\frac{|b_{n}|^{2}}{m+n+2\beta}b^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|b_{-n}|^{2}}{m-n+2\beta}b^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}b_{-n}\right)}{m+2\beta}b^{2m+4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+4\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n^{2}}{2(m-n+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}|b_{-n}|^{2}b^{2(m-n+2\beta)}.

(2.83)

Combining (
2.3.2
) and (
2.3.2
) yields

\displaystyle\frac{|b_{n}|^{2}}{m+n+2\beta}b^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|b_{-n}|^{2}}{m-n+2\beta}b^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}b_{-n}\right)}{m+2\beta}b^{2m+4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+4\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n^{2}}{4(m+n+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(m+n+2\beta)}+\frac{n^{2}}{4(m-n+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}|b_{-n}|^{2}b^{2(m-n+2\beta)}.

(2.84)

Therefore, we get by (
2.3.2
) and (
2.3.2
)

\displaystyle\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{m-n-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\right)+\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{|b_{n}|^{2}}{m+n+2\beta}b^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{n+m-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n-2\beta)}\right)+\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{|b_{-n}|^{2}}{m-n+2\beta}b^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{m-2\beta}\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}a_{-n}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2m-4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m-4\beta}\right)+\frac{2}{m+2\beta}\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}b_{-n}\right)b^{2m+4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m+4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{n^{2}}{4(m-n-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}|a_{n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n-2\beta)}}+\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{n^{2}}{4(m+n-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n-2\beta)}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{n^{2}}{4(m+n+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(m+n+2\beta)}+\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{n^{2}}{4(m-n+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}|b_{-n}|^{2}b^{2(m-n+2\beta)}.

Then, we estimate

\displaystyle 4\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}b_{-n}+a_{-n}b_{n}\right)\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)

\displaystyle\geq-32\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{(m-n+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}{n^{2}}|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n-m+2\beta)}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)-\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{n^{2}}{8(m-n+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}|b_{-n}|^{2}b^{2(m-n+2\beta)}

\displaystyle-32\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{(m+n+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}{n^{2}}|a_{-n}|^{2}b^{2(-m-n+2\beta)}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)-\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{n^{2}}{8(m+n+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(m+n+2\beta)}.

Therefore, we deduce that

\displaystyle\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{m-n-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\right)+\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{|b_{n}|^{2}}{m+n+2\beta}b^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{n+m-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n-2\beta)}\right)+\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{|b_{-n}|^{2}}{m-n+2\beta}b^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{m-2\beta}\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}a_{-n}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2m-4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m-4\beta}\right)+\frac{2}{m+2\beta}\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}b_{-n}\right)b^{2m+4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m+4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle+4\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}b_{-n}+a_{-n}b_{n}\right)\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}|a_{n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n-2\beta)}}\left(\frac{n^{2}}{4(m-n-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}-\frac{32(m-n+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}{n^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n-2\beta)}}\left(\frac{n^{2}}{4(m+n-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}-\frac{32(m+n+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}{n^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n-2\beta)}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{n^{2}}{8(m+n+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(m+n+2\beta)}+\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{n^{2}}{8(m-n+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}|b_{-n}|^{2}b^{2(m-n+2\beta)}.

Therefore, we impose

\displaystyle\frac{32(m-n+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}{n^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\leq\frac{n^{2}}{8(m-n-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}

\displaystyle\frac{32(m+n+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}{n^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n-2\beta)}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\leq\frac{n^{2}}{8(m+n-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}.

For all
\varepsilon>0
, consider the function

\displaystyle f(x)=x^{\varepsilon}\log^{2}(x)\qquad 0<x<1.

We have

\displaystyle f^{\prime}(x)=\varepsilon\,x^{\varepsilon-1}\log^{2}(x)+2\,x^{\varepsilon-1}\log(x)=x^{\varepsilon-1}\log\left(\frac{1}{x}\right)\left(\varepsilon\log\left(\frac{1}{x}\right)-2\right).

Therefore,
f
is strictly increasing on
]0,e^{-\frac{2}{\varepsilon}}[
and strictly decreasing on
]e^{-\frac{2}{\varepsilon}},1[
, which implies that

\displaystyle\sup_{0<x<1}f(x)=f(e^{-\frac{2}{\varepsilon}})=\frac{4\,e^{-2}}{\varepsilon}.

Therefore, if
0<\varepsilon<2(1-\beta)
, the first estimate is implied provided that

\displaystyle\frac{32(m-n+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}{n^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta-\varepsilon)}\leq\frac{e^{2}\varepsilon\,n^{2}}{16(m-n+2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}},

or

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2(m-n-2\beta-\varepsilon)}\log\left(\frac{1024((m-n)^{2}-4\beta^{2})(m^{2}-4\beta^{2})^{2}}{e^{2}\,\varepsilon\,n^{4}}\right).

Choosing
\varepsilon=1-\beta
, the estimate is satisfied provided that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2(1-\beta)}\log\left(\frac{1024(m^{2}-4\beta^{2})^{3}}{e^{2}(1-\beta)}\right).

(2.85)

Likewise, the second condition is satisfied provided that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2(1-\beta)}\log\left(\frac{4048m^{2}(m^{2}-4\beta^{2})^{2}}{e^{2}(1-\beta)}\right).

(2.86)

Therefore, we finally get

\displaystyle\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{m-n-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\right)+\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{|b_{n}|^{2}}{m+n+2\beta}b^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{n+m-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n-2\beta)}\right)+\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{|b_{-n}|^{2}}{m-n+2\beta}b^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{m-2\beta}\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}a_{-n}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2m-4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m-4\beta}\right)+\frac{2}{m+2\beta}\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}b_{-n}\right)b^{2m+4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m+4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle+4\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}b_{-n}+a_{-n}b_{n}\right)\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{n^{2}}{8(m-n-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}|a_{n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n-2\beta)}}+\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{n^{2}}{8(m+n-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n-2\beta)}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{n^{2}}{8(m+n+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(m+n+2\beta)}+\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{n^{2}}{8(m-n+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}|b_{-n}|^{2}b^{2(m-n+2\beta)}.

The remaining cross term is given by

\displaystyle\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)}{n+2\beta}b^{2(n+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+2\beta)}\right)+\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-n}\overline{b_{-n}}\right)}{n-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-2\beta)}\right).

We first have

\displaystyle\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)}{n+2\beta}b^{2(n+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{n^{2}}{16(m+n+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(m+n+2\beta)}

\displaystyle-\frac{16(m+n+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}{n^{2}(n+2\beta)^{2}}|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(-m+n+2\beta)}.

We have

\displaystyle\frac{n^{2}}{8(m-n-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}|a_{n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n-2\beta)}}-\frac{16(m+n+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}{n^{2}(n+2\beta)^{2}}|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(-m+n+2\beta)}

\displaystyle=|a_{n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n-2\beta)}}\left(\frac{n^{2}}{8(m-n-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}-\frac{16(m+n+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}{n^{2}(n+2\beta)^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\right).

Therefore, we impose the condition

\displaystyle\frac{16(m+n+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}{n^{2}(n+2\beta)^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\leq\frac{n^{2}}{16(m-n-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}},

which is equivalent to

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2(m-n-2\beta)}\log\left(\frac{256\left(m^{2}-(n+2\beta)^{2}\right)\left(m^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)^{2}}{n^{4}(n+2\beta)^{2}}\right).

This condition is satisfied if

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{4(1-\beta)}\log\left(\frac{4\left(m^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)^{3}}{(1+\beta)^{2}}\right).

(2.87)

Likewise, we get

\displaystyle\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-n}\overline{b_{-n}}\right)}{n-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{n^{2}}{16(m-n+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}|b_{-n}|^{2}b^{2(m-n+2\beta)}

\displaystyle-\frac{16(m-n+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}{n^{2}(n-2\beta)^{2}}|a_{-n}|^{2}b^{2(-m-n+2\beta)}.

Then, we get

\displaystyle\frac{n^{2}}{8(m+n-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n-2\beta)}}-\frac{16(m-n+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}{n^{2}(m-2\beta)^{2}}|a_{-n}|^{2}b^{2(-m-n+2\beta)}

\displaystyle=|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n-2\beta)}}\left(\frac{n^{2}}{8(m+n-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}-\frac{16(m-n+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}{n^{2}(m-2\beta)^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n-2\beta)}\right).

Therefore, we impose

\displaystyle\frac{16(m-n+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}{n^{2}(n-2\beta)^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n-2\beta)}\leq\frac{n^{2}}{16(m+n-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}},

or

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2(m+n-2\beta)}\log\left(\frac{256\left(m^{2}-(n-2\beta)^{2}\right)\left(m^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)^{2}}{n^{4}(n-2\beta)^{2}}\right),

which holds for
2\leq n\leq m-4
provided that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2(m+2(1-\beta)}\log\left(\frac{4\left(m^{2}-4(1-\beta)^{2}\right)\left(m^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)^{2}}{(1-\beta)^{2}}\right).

(2.88)

Finally, we deduce that

\displaystyle\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{m-n-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\right)+\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{|b_{n}|^{2}}{m+n+2\beta}b^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{n+m-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n-2\beta)}\right)+\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{|b_{-n}|^{2}}{m-n+2\beta}b^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{m-2\beta}\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}a_{-n}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2m-4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m-4\beta}\right)+\frac{2}{m+2\beta}\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}b_{-n}\right)b^{2m+4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m+4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle+4\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}b_{-n}+a_{-n}b_{n}\right)\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)}{n+2\beta}b^{2(n+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+2\beta)}\right)+\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-n}\overline{b_{-n}}\right)}{n-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{n^{2}}{16(m-n-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}|a_{n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n-2\beta)}}+\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{n^{2}}{16(m+n-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n-2\beta)}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{n^{2}}{16(m+n+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(m+n+2\beta)}+\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{n^{2}}{16(m-n+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}|b_{-n}|^{2}b^{2(m-n+2\beta)}.

(2.89)

Thanks to (
2.3.2
) (and (
2.3.2
) that shows that the integrated expression is a sum of squares and that bounding a subset of them gives a control by its left-hand side), we deduce that

\displaystyle\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{n^{2}}{16(m-n-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}|a_{n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n-2\beta)}}+\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{n^{2}}{16(m+n-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n-2\beta)}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{n^{2}}{16(m+n+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(m+n+2\beta)}+\sum_{n=2}^{m-2}\frac{n^{2}}{16(m-n+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}|b_{-n}|^{2}b^{2(m-n+2\beta)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{\Omega}\frac{u^{2}}{|x|^{4}}|x|^{4\beta}dx.

Now, besides the logarithm terms, we need only estimate
a_{n}
and
b_{n}
for
m-1\leq|n|\leq m
and
0\leq|n|\leq 1
. For
n=m
, we find the quantity

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{m}|^{2}}{2\beta}b^{4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)+\frac{|a_{-m}|^{2}}{2(m-\beta)}\frac{1}{a^{4(m-\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m-\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{|b_{m}|^{2}}{2(m+\beta)}b^{4(m+\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m+\beta)}\right)+\frac{|b_{-m}|^{2}}{2\beta}b^{4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{m-2\beta}\mathrm{Re}\,(a_{m}a_{-m})\frac{1}{a^{2m-4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m-4\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{m+2\beta}\mathrm{Re}\,(b_{m}b_{-m})b^{2m+4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m+4\beta}\right)+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{m}\overline{b_{m}}\right)}{m+2\beta}b^{2(m+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-m}\overline{a_{-m}}\right)}{m-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+4\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{m}b_{-m}+a_{-m}b_{m}\right)\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right).

As previously, we estimate

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{m}|^{2}}{2\beta}b^{4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)+\frac{|a_{-m}|^{2}}{2(m-\beta)}\frac{1}{a^{4(m-\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m-\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{m-2\beta}\mathrm{Re}\,(a_{m}a_{-m})\frac{1}{a^{2m-4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m-4\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|a_{m}|^{2}}{2\beta}b^{4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)-\frac{4(m-\beta)}{(m-2\beta)^{2}}|a_{m}|^{2}a^{4\beta}\frac{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m-4\beta}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m-\beta)}}+\frac{|a_{-m}|^{2}}{4(m-\beta)}\frac{1}{a^{4(m-\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m-\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{|a_{m}|^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m-\beta)}}b^{4\beta}\left(\frac{1}{2\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m-\beta)}\right)-\frac{2(m-\beta)}{(m-2\beta)^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m-4\beta}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{|a_{-m}|^{2}}{4(m-\beta)}\frac{1}{a^{4(m-\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m-\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|a_{m}|^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m-\beta)}}b^{4\beta}\left(\frac{1}{2\beta}-\frac{1}{2\beta}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}-\frac{1}{2\beta}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m-\beta)}-\frac{4(m-\beta)}{(m-2\beta)^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{|a_{-m}|^{2}}{4(m-\beta)}\frac{1}{a^{4(m-\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m-\beta)}\right)

We impose the following conditions on the conformal class

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &\frac{1}{2\beta}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\leq\frac{1}{16\beta}\\
&\frac{1}{2\beta}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m-\beta)}\leq\frac{1}{16\beta}\\
&\frac{4(m-\beta)}{(m-2\beta)^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\leq\frac{1}{16\beta}.\end{aligned}\right.

Therefore, the condition is equivalent to

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\max\left\{\frac{1}{4\beta}\log(8),\frac{1}{4(m-\beta)}\log(8),\frac{1}{4\beta}\log\left(\frac{64\beta(m-\beta)}{(m-2\beta)^{2}}\right)\right\}.

(2.90)

If this condition is satisfied, we deduce that

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{m}|^{2}}{2\beta}b^{4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)+\frac{|a_{-m}|^{2}}{2(m-\beta)}\frac{1}{a^{4(m-\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m-\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{m-2\beta}\mathrm{Re}\,(a_{m}a_{-m})\frac{1}{a^{2m-4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m-4\beta)}\right)\geq\frac{1}{4\beta}|a_{m}|^{2}b^{4\beta}+\frac{|a_{-m}|^{2}}{4(m-\beta)}\frac{1}{a^{4(m-\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m-\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{4\beta}|a_{m}|^{2}b^{4\beta}+\frac{|a_{-m}|^{2}}{8(m-\beta)}\frac{1}{a^{4(m-\beta)}}.

(2.91)

Now, for
n=m-1
, the relevant quantity is given by

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{m-1}|^{2}}{2\beta-1}b^{2(2\beta-1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2\beta-1)}\right)+\frac{|a_{1-m}|^{2}}{2m-1-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(2m-1-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2m-1-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{m-1}a_{1-m}\right)}{m-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2m-4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m-4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|a_{m-1}|^{2}}{2\beta-1}b^{2(2\beta-1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2\beta-1)}\right)-\frac{2(2m-1-2\beta)}{(m-2\beta)^{2}}|a_{m-1}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(1-2\beta)}}\frac{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m-4\beta}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2m-1-2\beta)}}

\displaystyle+\frac{|a_{1-m}|^{2}}{2(2m-1-2\beta)}\frac{1}{a^{2(2m-1-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2m-1-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|a_{m-1}|^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2m-1-2\beta)}}b^{2(2\beta-1)}\left(\frac{1}{2\beta-1}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2\beta-1)}\right)\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2m-1-2\beta)}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\frac{2(2m-1-2\beta)}{(m-2\beta)^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2\beta-1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m-4\beta}\right)^{2}\right).

Therefore, we impose the following conditions

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &\frac{1}{2\beta-1}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2\beta-1)}\leq\frac{1}{8(2\beta-1)}\\
&\frac{1}{2\beta-1}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2m-1-2\beta)}\leq\frac{1}{8(2\beta-1)}\\
&\frac{2(2m-1-2\beta)}{(m-2\beta)^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2\beta-1)}\leq\frac{1}{8(2\beta-1)}\end{aligned}\right.,

which yields the condition

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\max\left\{\frac{1}{2(2\beta-1)}\log(8),\frac{1}{2(2m-1-2\beta)}\log(8),\frac{1}{2(2\beta-1)}\log\left(\frac{16(2\beta-1)(2m-1-2\beta)}{(m-2\beta)^{2}}\right)\right\},

(2.92)

and we get

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{m-1}|^{2}}{2\beta-1}b^{2(2\beta-1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2\beta-1)}\right)+\frac{|a_{1-m}|^{2}}{2m-1-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(2m-1-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2m-1-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{m-1}a_{1-m}\right)}{m-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2m-4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m-4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|a_{m-1}|^{2}}{2(2\beta-1)}b^{2(2\beta-1)}+\frac{|a_{1-m}|^{2}}{2(2m-1-2\beta)}\frac{1}{a^{2(2m-1-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2m-1-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|a_{m-1}|^{2}}{2(2\beta-1)}b^{2(2\beta-1)}+\frac{|a_{1-m}|^{2}}{4(2m-1-2\beta)}\frac{1}{a^{2(2m-1-2\beta)}}.

Now, we consider

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{1}|^{2}}{m-1-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-1-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-1-2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|a_{-1}|^{2}}{m+1-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+1-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+1-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{1}a_{-1}\right)}{m-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2m-4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m-4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|a_{1}|^{2}}{m-1-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-1-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-1-2\beta)}\right)-\frac{(m+1-2\beta)}{(m-2\beta)^{2}}|a_{1}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-1-2\beta)}}\frac{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m-4\beta}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+1-2\beta)}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|a_{1}|^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+1-2\beta)}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-1-2\beta)}}\left(\frac{1}{(m-1-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}-\frac{1}{m-1-2\beta}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-1-2\beta)}\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\frac{1}{m-1-2\beta}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+1-2\beta)}\right).

Therefore, we impose the conditions

\displaystyle\frac{1}{m-1-2\beta}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-1-2\beta)}\leq\frac{1}{4(m-1-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{m-1-2\beta}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+1-2\beta)}\leq\frac{1}{4(m-1-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}},

which yields

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\max\left\{\frac{1}{2(m+1-2\beta)}\log(4),\frac{1}{2(m-1-2\beta)}\log(4)\right\}=\frac{1}{2(m-1-2\beta)}\log(4),

(2.93)

and yields

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{1}|^{2}}{m-1-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-1-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-1-2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|a_{-1}|^{2}}{m+1-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+1-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+1-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{1}a_{-1}\right)}{m-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2m-4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m-4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|a_{1}|^{2}}{2(m-1-2\beta)}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-1-2\beta)}}.

Likewise, we have (with the same condition on the conformal class)

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{1}|^{2}}{m-1-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-1-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-1-2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|a_{-1}|^{2}}{m+1-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+1-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+1-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{1}a_{-1}\right)}{m-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2m-4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m-4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|a_{-1}|^{2}}{2(m+1-2\beta)}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+1-2\beta)}},

and finally, we get

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{1}|^{2}}{m-1-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-1-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-1-2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|a_{-1}|^{2}}{m+1-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+1-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+1-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{1}a_{-1}\right)}{m-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2m-4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m-4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|a_{1}|^{2}}{4(m-1-2\beta)}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-1-2\beta)}}+\frac{|a_{-1}|^{2}}{4(m+1-2\beta)}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+1-2\beta)}}.

(2.94)

Now, we estimate the
b_{n}
terms. We consider

\displaystyle\frac{|b_{m}|^{2}}{2(m+\beta)}b^{4(m+\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m+\beta)}\right)+\frac{|b_{-m}|^{2}}{2\beta}b^{4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{m}b_{-m}\right)}{m+2\beta}b^{2m+4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m+4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|b_{m}|^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}}b^{4(m+\beta)}\left(\frac{m^{2}}{2(m+\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}-\frac{1}{2(m+\beta)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m+\beta)}-\frac{1}{2(m+\beta)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right).

Therefore, we impose the conditions

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2(m+\beta)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m+\beta)}\leq\frac{m^{2}}{8(m+\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2(m+\beta)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\leq\frac{m^{2}}{8(m+\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}},

which yields

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\max\left\{\frac{1}{2(m+\beta)}\log\left(\frac{2(m+2\beta)}{m}\right),\frac{1}{2\beta}\log\left(\frac{2(m+2\beta)}{m}\right)\right\}=\frac{1}{2\beta}\log\left(\frac{2(m+2\beta)}{m}\right),

(2.95)

and we get the estimate

\displaystyle\frac{|b_{m}|^{2}}{2(m+\beta)}b^{4(m+\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m+\beta)}\right)+\frac{|b_{-m}|^{2}}{2\beta}b^{4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{m}b_{-m}\right)}{m+2\beta}b^{2m+4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m+4\beta}\right)\geq\frac{m^{2}}{4(m+\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}|b_{m}|^{2}b^{4(m+\beta)}.

Likewise, if

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2\beta}\log\left(\frac{2(m+2\beta)}{m}\right),

(2.96)

we get

\displaystyle\frac{|b_{m}|^{2}}{2(m+\beta)}b^{4(m+\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m+\beta)}\right)+\frac{|b_{-m}|^{2}}{2\beta}b^{4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{m}b_{-m}\right)}{m+2\beta}b^{2m+4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m+4\beta}\right)\geq\frac{m^{2}}{4\beta(m+2\beta)^{2}}|b_{-m}|^{2}b^{4\beta},

and finally, we have

\displaystyle\frac{|b_{m}|^{2}}{2(m+\beta)}b^{4(m+\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m+\beta)}\right)+\frac{|b_{-m}|^{2}}{2\beta}b^{4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{m}b_{-m}\right)}{m+2\beta}b^{2m+4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m+4\beta}\right)\geq\frac{m^{2}}{8(m+\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}|b_{m}|^{2}b^{4(m+\beta)}+\frac{m^{2}}{8\beta(m+2\beta)^{2}}|b_{-m}|^{2}b^{4\beta}.

(2.97)

Then, we have

\displaystyle\frac{|b_{m-1}|^{2}}{2m-1+2\beta}b^{2(2m-1+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-1+2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|b_{1-m}|^{2}}{2\beta+1}b^{2(2\beta+1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2\beta+1)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{m-1}b_{1-m}\right)}{m+2\beta}b^{2m+4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m+4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|b_{m-1}|^{2}}{2m-1+2\beta}b^{2(2m-1+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-1+2\beta)}\right)-\frac{2\beta+1}{(m+2\beta)^{2}}|b_{m-1}|^{2}b^{2(2m-1+2\beta)}\frac{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m+4\beta}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2\beta+1)}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|b_{m-1}|^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2\beta+1)}}b^{2(2m-1+2\beta)}\left(\frac{(m-1)^{2}}{(2m-1+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}-\frac{1}{2m-1+2\beta}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2m-1+2\beta)}\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\frac{1}{2m-1+2\beta}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2\beta+1)}\right).

Therefore, we impose the conditions

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2m-1+2\beta}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2m-1+2\beta)}\leq\frac{(m-1)^{2}}{4(2m-1+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2m-1+2\beta}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2\beta+1)}\leq\frac{(m-1)^{2}}{4(2m-1+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}.

Therefore, the conditions becomes

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\max\left\{\frac{1}{2m-1+2\beta}\log\left(\frac{2(m+2\beta)}{m-1}\right),\frac{1}{2\beta+1}\log\left(\frac{2(m+2\beta)}{m-1}\right)\right\},

(2.98)

and the estimate is given by

\displaystyle\frac{|b_{m-1}|^{2}}{2m-1+2\beta}b^{2(2m-1+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-1+2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|b_{1-m}|^{2}}{2\beta+1}b^{2(2\beta+1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2\beta+1)}\right)
