\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{m-1}b_{1-m}\right)}{m+2\beta}b^{2m+4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m+4\beta}\right)\geq\frac{(m-1)^{2}}{2(2m-1+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}|b_{m-1}|^{2}b^{2(2m-1+2\beta)}.

Likewise, the hypothesis (
2.98
) shows that

\displaystyle\frac{|b_{m-1}|^{2}}{2m-1+2\beta}b^{2(2m-1+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-1+2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|b_{1-m}|^{2}}{2\beta+1}b^{2(2\beta+1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2\beta+1)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{m-1}b_{1-m}\right)}{m+2\beta}b^{2m+4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m+4\beta}\right)\geq\frac{(m-1)^{2}}{2(2\beta+1)(m+2\beta)^{2}}b^{2(2\beta+1)},

and finally, we get

\displaystyle\frac{|b_{m-1}|^{2}}{2m-1+2\beta}b^{2(2m-1+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-1+2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|b_{1-m}|^{2}}{2\beta+1}b^{2(2\beta+1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2\beta+1)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{m-1}b_{1-m}\right)}{m+2\beta}b^{2m+4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m+4\beta}\right)\geq\frac{(m-1)^{2}}{4(2m-1+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}|b_{m-1}|^{2}b^{2(2m-1+2\beta)}

\displaystyle+\frac{(m-1)^{2}}{4(2\beta+1)(m+2\beta)^{2}}b^{2(2\beta+1)}.

(2.99)

Then, we have

\displaystyle\frac{|b_{1}|^{2}}{m+1+2\beta}b^{2(m+1+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+1+2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|b_{-1}|^{2}}{m-1+2\beta}b^{2(m-1+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-1+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{1}b_{-1}\right)}{m+2\beta}b^{2m+4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m+4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|b_{1}|^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-1+2\beta)}}b^{2(m+1+2\beta)}\left(\frac{1}{(m+1+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}-\frac{1}{m+1+2\beta}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+1+2\beta)}-\frac{1}{m+1+2\beta}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-1+2\beta)}\right).

Therefore, we impose the conditions

\displaystyle\frac{1}{m+1+2\beta}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+1+2\beta)}\leq\frac{1}{4(m+1+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{m-1+2\beta}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-1+2\beta)}\leq\frac{1}{4(m+1+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}},

which yields the condition

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\max\left\{\frac{1}{m+1+2\beta}\log\left(2(m+2\beta)\right),\frac{1}{m-1+2\beta}\log\left(2(m+2\beta)\right)\right\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{m-1+2\beta}\log\left(2(m+2\beta)\right),

(2.100)

and we get the estimate

\displaystyle\frac{|b_{1}|^{2}}{m+1+2\beta}b^{2(m+1+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+1+2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|b_{-1}|^{2}}{m-1+2\beta}b^{2(m-1+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-1+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{1}b_{-1}\right)}{m+2\beta}b^{2m+4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m+4\beta}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2(m+1+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}|b_{1}|^{2}b^{2(m+1+2\beta)}.

Likewise, if (
2.3.2
) holds, we get

\displaystyle\frac{|b_{1}|^{2}}{m+1+2\beta}b^{2(m+1+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+1+2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|b_{-1}|^{2}}{m-1+2\beta}b^{2(m-1+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-1+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{1}b_{-1}\right)}{m+2\beta}b^{2m+4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m+4\beta}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2(m-1+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}|b_{-1}|^{2}b^{2(m-1+2\beta)},

and finally, we have

\displaystyle\frac{|b_{1}|^{2}}{m+1+2\beta}b^{2(m+1+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+1+2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|b_{-1}|^{2}}{m-1+2\beta}b^{2(m-1+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-1+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{1}b_{-1}\right)}{m+2\beta}b^{2m+4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m+4\beta}\right)\geq\frac{1}{4(m+1+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}|b_{1}|^{2}b^{2(m+1+2\beta)}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4(m-1+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}|b_{-1}|^{2}b^{2(m-1+2\beta)}.

(2.101)

Then, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4\beta}|a_{m}|^{2}b^{4\beta}+\frac{|a_{-m}|^{2}}{8(m-\beta)}\frac{1}{a^{4(m-\beta)}}+\frac{m^{2}}{8(m+\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}|b_{m}|^{2}b^{4(m+\beta)}+\frac{m^{2}}{8\beta(m+2\beta)^{2}}|b_{-m}|^{2}b^{4\beta}

\displaystyle+4\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{m}b_{-m}+a_{-m}b_{m}\right)\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right).

\displaystyle-\frac{2(m-1)}{m}\left(m^{2}-m+1\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{m}a_{-m}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)

Then, we have

. We have

\displaystyle\geq(m-1)^{2}\left((m-1)^{2}+1\right)|a_{m}|^{2}\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)-\frac{(m-1)^{2}\left(m^{2}-m+1\right)^{2}}{m^{2}\varepsilon}|a_{m}|^{2}\frac{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4m}}

Likewise, we have

\displaystyle-\delta|b_{m}|^{2}b^{4m}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4m}\right),

and

\displaystyle-m(3m^{2}-2m+3)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-m}b_{-m}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)

\displaystyle\geq-\varepsilon|a_{-m}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{4m}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4m}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{m^{2}(3m^{2}-2m+3)^{2}}{\varepsilon}|b_{-m}|^{2}\frac{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4m}}.

Then, for all
\eta>0
and
\xi>0
, we have

\displaystyle m(m-1)\left(m^{2}-4m+1\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{m}b_{-m}\right)\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{\eta}{2}\,m(m-1)\left|m^{2}-4m+1\right||a_{m}|^{2}\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4\xi}m^{2}(m-1)^{2}\left(m^{2}+2m+3\right)^{2}|a_{-m}|^{2}\frac{1}{b^{4m}}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\frac{1}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4m}}.

Finally, we get the estimate

\displaystyle(m-1)^{2}\left((m-1)^{2}+1\right)|a_{m}|^{2}\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)+\frac{m^{4}+3m^{2}-2m+2}{4m}|a_{-m}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{4m}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4m}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{2(m-1)}{m}\left(m^{2}-m+1\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{m}a_{-m}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)+\frac{m(17m^{2}-18m+5)}{4}|b_{m}|^{2}b^{4m}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4m}\right)

\displaystyle+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}|b_{-m}|^{2}\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)+2m(m-1)(2m-1)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{m}b_{-m}\right)b^{2m}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)

\displaystyle+m(m-1)^{2}(4m-3)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{m}\overline{b_{m}}\right)b^{2m}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)

\displaystyle-m(3m^{2}-2m+3)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-m}\overline{b_{-m}}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)

\displaystyle+m(m-1)\left(m^{2}-4m+1\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{m}b_{-m}\right)\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)

\displaystyle+m(m-1)\left(m^{2}+2m+3\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-m}b_{m}\right)\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)

\displaystyle\geq|a_{m}|^{2}\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\left\{(m-1)^{2}\left((m-1)^{2}+1\right)-\frac{\eta}{2}m(m-1)\left|m^{2}-4m+1\right|\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\left(\frac{(m-1)^{2}\left(m^{2}-m+1\right)^{2}}{m^{2}\varepsilon}\frac{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4m}}+\frac{m^{2}(m-1)^{4}(4m-3)^{2}}{2\delta}\right)\frac{1}{\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{|a_{-m}|^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4m}}\frac{1}{a^{4m}}\left(\left(\frac{m^{4}+3m^{2}-2m+2}{4m}-2\varepsilon\right)\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4m}\right)^{2}\right.

To get a non-trivial estimate, we must impose the following conditions

\displaystyle\frac{\eta}{2}m(m-1)|m^{2}-4m+1|<(m-1)^{2}\left((m-1)^{2}+1\right)

Fir

, the first and last conditions are void, and we can easily solve the remaining equations in
\delta,\varepsilon,\xi
. Independently of the value of
m
, we can take

\displaystyle\varepsilon

\displaystyle=\frac{m^{4}+3m^{2}-2m+2}{16m}

(2.102)

\displaystyle\delta

\displaystyle=\frac{m(17m^{2}-18m+5)}{16}

(2.103)

\displaystyle\xi

\displaystyle=\frac{m(17m^{2}-18m+5)}{16}.

(2.104)

Notice that we already control
b_{m}
, but not
b_{-m}
, the first and fourth equation cannot be replaced by milder conditions, even if we take
\delta
arbitrary large. Now, assume that
m\neq 2+\sqrt{3}
. Then, we can find a solution to the first and fourth inequalities provided that

\displaystyle\frac{|m^{2}-4m+1|}{2m(m-1)}<\frac{2(m-1)\left((m-1)^{2}+1\right)}{m\left|m^{2}-4m+1\right|},

which is equivalent to

\displaystyle 4(m-1)^{2}\left((m-1)^{2}+1\right)-\left(m^{2}-4m+1\right)^{2}=3m^{4}-8m^{3}+10m^{2}-16m+7>0.

Let

\displaystyle f(x)=0.

We have

\displaystyle f^{\prime}(x)=12x^{3}-24x^{2}+20x-16

\displaystyle f^{\prime\prime}(x)=36x^{2}-48x+20=4(9x^{2}-12x+5)>0

which implies that
f^{\prime}
is strictly increasing. Furthermore, we have

\displaystyle f^{\prime}(2)=12\cdot 8-24\cdot 4+40-16=24>0,

which shows in particular that
f
is strictly increasing on
[2,\infty[
. However,
f(2)=-1<0
, so we will not get the estimate we need for
m=2
(recall that we are mostly interested in the case
m\in\mathbb{N}\setminus\left\{0,1\right\}
). If
m_{0}=2.039423\cdots
is the largest real root of
f
, we deduce that for all
m>m_{0}
, the above system can be solved in
\eta
and we simply take

\displaystyle\eta=\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{|m^{2}-4m+1|}{2m(m-1)}+\frac{2(m-1)\left((m-1)^{2}+1\right)}{m\left|m^{2}-4m+1\right|}\right).

We have

\displaystyle\frac{\eta}{2}m(m-1)\left|m^{2}-4m+1\right|=\frac{(m^{2}-4m+1)^{2}}{8}+\frac{1}{2}(m-1)^{2}\left((m-1)^{2}+1\right)

Furthermore, since
3x^{2}-2x-1\leq 0
for
0\leq x\leq 1
, we get the estimate

\displaystyle\frac{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4m}}\geq 2.

Therefore, we impose the condition

\displaystyle\left(\frac{2(m-1)^{2}\left(m^{2}-m+1\right)^{2}}{m^{2}\varepsilon}+\frac{m^{2}(m-1)^{4}(4m-3)^{2}}{2\delta}\right)\frac{1}{\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\left\{(m-1)^{2}\left((m-1)^{2}+1\right)-\frac{\eta}{2}m(m-1)\left|m^{2}-4m+1\right|\right\},

or

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{128}{4(m-1)^{2}\left((m-1)^{2}+1\right)-\left(m^{2}-4m+1\right)^{2}}\left(\frac{4(m-1)^{2}\left(m^{2}-m+1\right)^{2}}{m(m^{4}+3m^{2}-2m+2)}+\frac{m(m-1)^{4}(4m-3)^{2}}{17m^{2}-18m+5}\right).

(2.105)

Then, imposing that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{4m}\log\left(\frac{\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{2}-1}\right),

(2.106)

we get

\displaystyle\left(\frac{m^{4}+3m^{2}-2m+2}{4m}-2\varepsilon\right)\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4m}\right)^{2}\geq\frac{m^{4}+3m^{2}-2m+2}{16m}.

Furthermore, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4\xi}m^{2}(m-1)^{2}\left(m^{2}+2m+3\right)^{2}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\leq\frac{4m(m-1)^{2}\left(m^{2}+2m+3\right)^{2}}{e^{2}\left(17m^{2}-18m+5\right)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4m-2}.

Therefore, we impose the condition

\displaystyle\frac{4m(m-1)^{2}\left(m^{2}+2m+3\right)^{2}}{e^{2}\left(17m^{2}-18m+5\right)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4m-2}\leq\frac{m^{4}+3m^{2}-2m+2}{32m},

which yields the condition

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2(2m-1)}\log\left(\frac{64m^{2}(m-1)^{2}\left(m^{2}+2m+3\right)^{2}}{e^{2}(17m^{2}-18m+5)(m^{4}+3m^{2}-2m+2)}\right).

(2.107)

Then, we have

\displaystyle\frac{m^{2}(m-1)^{2}(2m-1)^{2}}{\delta}\frac{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4m}}+\frac{m^{2}(3m^{2}-2m+3)^{2}}{\varepsilon}\frac{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4m}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{32m(m-1)^{2}(2m-1)^{2}}{17m^{2}-18m+5}+\frac{32m^{3}\left(3m^{2}-2m+3\right)^{2}}{m^{4}+3m^{2}-2m+2},

and since

\displaystyle m^{2}(m-1)^{2}-\frac{1}{2\eta}m(m-1)\left|m^{2}-4m+1\right|=\frac{2m^{3}(m-1)\left(m^{2}-4m+1\right)^{2}}{\left(m^{2}-4m+1\right)^{2}+2(m-1)^{2}\left((m-1)^{2}+1\right)}

\displaystyle=m^{2}(m-1)^{2}\frac{4(m-1)^{2}\left((m-1)^{2}+1\right)-\left(m^{2}-4m+1\right)^{2}}{4(m-1)^{2}\left((m-1)^{2}+1\right)+\left(m^{2}-4m+1\right)^{2}},

we also impose the condition

\displaystyle\left(\frac{32m(m-1)^{2}(2m-1)^{2}}{17m^{2}-18m+5}+\frac{32m^{3}\left(3m^{2}-2m+3\right)^{2}}{m^{4}+3m^{2}-2m+2}\right)\frac{1}{\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}m^{2}(m-1)^{2}\frac{4(m-1)^{2}\left((m-1)^{2}+1\right)-\left(m^{2}-4m+1\right)^{2}}{4(m-1)^{2}\left((m-1)^{2}+1\right)+\left(m^{2}-4m+1\right)^{2}},

which yields

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{2}{m^{2}(m-1)^{2}}\frac{4(m-1)^{2}\left((m-1)^{2}+1\right)+\left(m^{2}-4m+1\right)^{2}}{4(m-1)^{2}\left((m-1)^{2}+1\right)-\left(m^{2}-4m+1\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\left(\frac{32m(m-1)^{2}(2m-1)^{2}}{17m^{2}-18m+5}+\frac{32m^{3}\left(3m^{2}-2m+3\right)^{2}}{m^{4}+3m^{2}-2m+2}\right).

(2.108)

Finally, we deduce that

\displaystyle(m-1)^{2}\left((m-1)^{2}+1\right)|a_{m}|^{2}\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)+\frac{m^{4}+3m^{2}-2m+2}{4m}|a_{-m}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{4m}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4m}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{2(m-1)}{m}\left(m^{2}-m+1\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{m}a_{-m}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)+\frac{m(17m^{2}-18m+5)}{4}|b_{m}|^{2}b^{4m}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4m}\right)

\displaystyle+m^{2}(m-1)^{2}|b_{-m}|^{2}\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)+2m(m-1)(2m-1)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{m}b_{-m}\right)b^{2m}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)

\displaystyle+m(m-1)^{2}(4m-3)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{m}\overline{b_{m}}\right)b^{2m}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)

\displaystyle-m(3m^{2}-2m+3)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-m}\overline{b_{-m}}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)

\displaystyle+m(m-1)\left(m^{2}-4m+1\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{m}b_{-m}\right)\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)

\displaystyle+m(m-1)\left(m^{2}+2m+3\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-m}b_{m}\right)\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{16}\left(4(m-1)^{2}\left((m-1)^{2}+1\right)-\left(m^{2}-4m+1\right)^{2}\right)|a_{m}|^{2}\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{m^{4}+3m^{2}-2m+2}{32m}|a_{-m}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{4m}}+\frac{m(17m^{2}-18m+5)}{32}|b_{m}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}m^{2}(m-1)^{2}\frac{4(m-1)^{2}\left((m-1)^{2}+1\right)-\left(m^{2}-4m+1\right)^{2}}{4(m-1)^{2}\left((m-1)^{2}+1\right)+\left(m^{2}-4m+1\right)^{2}}|b_{-m}|^{2}\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right).

(2.109)

Step 8.
The estimate of radial components.

Finally, we need to estimate
b_{0}
and the logarithm terms. Notice that thanks to Parseval’s lemma, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right)^{2}dx\leq\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}u\right)^{2}dx.

Likewise, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\left|\mathfrak{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right|^{2}|dz|^{2}\leq\int_{\Omega}\left|\mathfrak{L}_{m}u\right|^{2}|dz|^{2}.

Now, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Rad}(u)=\alpha_{1}|z|^{1-m}\log|z|+2\,a_{0}|z|^{1-m}+\alpha_{2}|z|^{m+1}\log|z|+2\,b_{0}|z|^{m+1}.

Recall that
\alpha_{1}|z|^{1-m}\log|z|+2\,a_{0}|z|^{1-m}\in\mathrm{Ker}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}\right)
. Then, we have

\displaystyle\partial_{r}\left(r^{m+1}\log(r)\right)=(m+1)r^{m}\log(r)+r^{m}

\displaystyle\partial_{r}^{2}\left(r^{m+1}\log(r)\right)=m(m+1)r^{m-1}\log(r)+(2m+1)r^{m-1}.

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}_{m}

\displaystyle=\Delta+2(m-1)\frac{x}{|x|^{2}}\cdot\nabla+\frac{(m-1)^{2}}{|x|^{2}}

\displaystyle=\partial_{r}^{2}+\frac{2m-1}{r}\partial_{r}+\frac{(m-1)^{2}}{r^{2}}+\frac{1}{r^{2}}\partial_{\theta}^{2}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)

\displaystyle=m(m+1)\alpha_{2}r^{m-1}\log(r)+(2m+1)\alpha_{2}r^{m-1}+(2m-1)(m+1)\alpha_{2}r^{m-1}\log(r)+(2m-1)\alpha_{2}r^{m}

\displaystyle+(m-1)^{2}\alpha_{2}r^{m-1}\log(r)+2m(m+1)b_{0}r^{m-1}+2(2m-1)(m+1)b_{0}r^{m-1}+2(m-1)^{2}b_{0}r^{m-1}

\displaystyle=4m^{2}\alpha_{2}r^{m-1}\log(r)+4m\alpha_{2}r^{m-1}+8m^{2}b_{0}r^{m-1}

\displaystyle=4m^{2}\,r^{m-1}\left(\alpha_{2}\log(r)+\frac{1}{m}\alpha_{2}+2b_{0}\right).

Then, we have

\displaystyle\partial_{z}\mathrm{Rad}(u)

\displaystyle=\frac{1-m}{2}\alpha_{1}\,\overline{z}|z|^{-m-1}\log|z|+\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2}\overline{z}|z|^{-m-1}+(1-m)a_{0}\overline{z}|z|^{-1-m}

\displaystyle+\frac{m+1}{2}\alpha_{2}\overline{z}|z|^{m-1}\log|z|+\frac{\alpha_{2}}{2}\overline{z}|z|^{m-1}+(m+1)b_{0}\overline{z}|z|^{m-1}

\displaystyle\partial_{z}^{2}\mathrm{Rad}(u)

\displaystyle=\frac{m^{2}-1}{4}\alpha_{1}\,\overline{z}^{2}|z|^{-m-3}\log|z|-\frac{m}{2}\alpha_{1}\overline{z}^{2}|z|^{-m-3}+\frac{m^{2}-1}{2}a_{0}\overline{z}^{3}|z|^{-m-3}

\displaystyle+\frac{m^{2}-1}{4}\alpha_{2}\overline{z}^{2}|z|^{m-3}\log|z|+\frac{m}{2}\alpha_{2}\overline{z}^{2}|z|^{m-1}+\frac{m^{2}-1}{2}b_{0}\overline{z}^{2}|z|^{m-3}.

Therefore, we get

\displaystyle\mathfrak{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{z^{2}}\left(|z|^{1-m}\left(\frac{m^{2}-1}{4}\alpha_{1}\log|z|-\frac{m}{2}\alpha_{1}+\frac{m^{2}-1}{2}a_{0}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\frac{m^{2}-1}{2}\alpha_{1}\log|z|+\frac{m-1}{2}\alpha_{1}-(m^{2}-1)a_{0}+\frac{m^{2}-4m+3}{4}\alpha_{1}\log|z|+\frac{m^{2}-4m+3}{2}a_{0}\right)

\displaystyle+|z|^{m+1}\left(\frac{m^{2}-1}{4}\alpha_{2}\log|z|+\frac{m}{2}\alpha_{2}+\frac{m^{2}-1}{4}b_{0}+\frac{m^{2}-1}{2}\alpha_{2}\log|z|+\frac{m-1}{2}\alpha_{2}+(m^{2}-1)b_{0}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.+\frac{m^{2}-4m+3}{4}\alpha_{2}\log|z|+\frac{m^{2}-4m+3}{2}b_{0}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{z^{2}}|z|^{1-m}\left(-(m-1)\alpha_{1}\log|z|-\frac{1}{2}\alpha_{1}-2(m-1)a_{0}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{z^{2}}|z|^{m+1}\left(m(m-1)\alpha_{2}\log|z|+\frac{2m-1}{2}\alpha_{2}+2m(m-1)b_{0}\right).

Then, we have

\displaystyle\partial_{r}u+\frac{(m-1)}{r}u

\displaystyle=(1-m)\alpha_{1}r^{-m}\log(r)+\alpha_{1}r^{-m}+2(1-m)a_{0}r^{-m}+(m+1)\alpha_{2}r^{m}\log(r)+\alpha_{2}r^{m}+2(m+1)b_{0}r^{m}

\displaystyle+(m-1)\alpha_{1}r^{-m}+2(m-1)a_{0}r^{-m}+(m-1)\alpha_{2}r^{m}\log(r)+2(m-1)b_{0}r^{m}

\displaystyle=\alpha_{1}r^{-m}+2m\,r^{m}\left(\alpha_{2}\log(r)+\frac{1}{2m}\alpha_{2}+2b_{0}\right).

Now, we have

\displaystyle 4m\frac{z^{2}}{|z|^{2}}\mathfrak{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)-(m-1)\mathscr{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)=-4m(m-1)r^{-m-1}\left(\alpha_{1}\log(r)+\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2(m-1)}+2a_{0}\right)

\displaystyle+r^{m-1}\left(4m^{2}(m-1)\alpha_{2}\log(r)+2(m-1)(2m-1)\alpha_{2}+8m(m-1)b_{0}\right)

\displaystyle-r^{m-1}\left(4m^{2}(m-1)\alpha_{2}\log(r)+4m(m-1)\alpha_{2}+8m^{2}(m-1)b_{0}\right)

\displaystyle=-4m(m-1)r^{-m-1}\left(\alpha_{1}\log(r)+\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2(m-1)}+2a_{0}\right)-2(m-1)\alpha_{2}r^{m-1}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\mathscr{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)-\frac{2m}{m-1}\frac{z^{2}}{|z|^{2}}\mathfrak{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)=\alpha_{2}r^{m-1}+2m\,r^{-m-1}\left(\alpha_{1}\log(r)+\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2(m-1)}+2a_{0}\right).

Then, we have for
\alpha\neq-1

\displaystyle\int r^{\alpha}\log(r)dr

\displaystyle=\frac{r^{\alpha+1}}{\alpha+1}\log(r)-\frac{1}{\alpha+1}\int r^{\alpha}dr=\frac{r^{\alpha+1}}{\alpha+1}\log(r)-\frac{1}{(\alpha+1)^{2}}r^{\alpha+1}

\displaystyle\int r^{\alpha}\log^{2}(r)dr

\displaystyle=\frac{r^{\alpha+1}}{\alpha+1}\log^{2}(r)-\frac{2}{(\alpha+1)^{2}}r^{\alpha+1}\log(r)+\frac{2}{(\alpha+1)^{3}}r^{\alpha+1},

and for all
\beta>-1

\displaystyle\int\frac{\log^{\beta}(r)}{r}dr=\frac{1}{\beta+1}\log^{\beta+1}(r).

In particular, for
\alpha=2\gamma-1
, we have

\displaystyle\int r^{2\gamma-1}\log(r)dr

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2\gamma}\left(r^{2\gamma}\log\left(\frac{1}{r}\right)+\frac{1}{2\gamma}r^{2\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle\int r^{2\gamma-1}\log^{2}(r)dr

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\gamma}\left(r^{2\gamma}\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{r}\right)+\frac{1}{\gamma}r^{2\gamma}\log\left(\frac{1}{r}\right)+\frac{1}{2\gamma^{2}}r^{2\gamma}\right),

We deduce that

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\left(\frac{1}{2}\mathscr{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)-\frac{2m}{m-1}\frac{z^{2}}{|z|^{2}}\mathfrak{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right)^{2}dx=2\pi\int_{a}^{b}\left(\alpha_{2}^{2}r^{2m-1}+4m^{2}r^{-2m-1}\log^{2}(r)\right.

\displaystyle\left.+4m^{2}\left(\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2(m-1)}+2a_{0}\right)^{2}r^{-2m-1}+8m^{2}\left(\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2(m-1)}+2a_{0}\right)\alpha_{1}\,r^{-2m-1}\log(r)\right.

\displaystyle\left.+4m\,\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}r^{-1}\log(r)+4m\left(\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2(m-1)}+2a_{0}\right)\alpha_{2}r^{-1}\right)dr

\displaystyle=2\pi\left\{\frac{\alpha_{2}^{2}}{2m}b^{2m}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)+2m\,\alpha_{1}^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)-\frac{1}{m}\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)+\frac{1}{2m^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\left(\frac{1}{b^{2m}}\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)-\frac{1}{m}\frac{1}{b^{2m}}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)+\frac{1}{2m^{2}}\frac{1}{b^{2m}}\right)\right)+2m\left(\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2(m-1)}+2a_{0}\right)^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)

\displaystyle-4m\left(\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2(m-1)}+2a_{0}\right)\alpha_{1}\left(\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)-\frac{1}{2m}\frac{1}{a^{2m}}-\left(\frac{1}{b^{2m}}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)-\frac{1}{2m}\frac{1}{b^{2m}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\left.+2m\,\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}\left(\log^{2}(b)-\log^{2}(a)\right)+2m\left(\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2(m-1)}+2a_{0}\right)\alpha_{2}\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\right\}.

Likewise, we get

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right)^{2}dx=32\pi\,m^{2}\int_{a}^{b}\left(\alpha_{2}^{2}r^{2m-1}\log^{2}(r)+\left(\frac{1}{m}\alpha_{2}+2b_{0}\right)^{2}r^{2m-1}+2\left(\frac{1}{m}\alpha_{2}+2b_{0}\right)\alpha_{2}\,r^{2m-1}\log(r)\right)dr

\displaystyle=32\pi\,m^{4}\left\{\frac{1}{2m}\alpha_{2}^{2}\left(b^{2m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)+\frac{1}{m}b^{2m}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)+\frac{1}{2m^{2}}b^{2m}-\left(a^{2m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)+\frac{1}{m}a^{2m}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)+\frac{1}{2m^{2}}a^{2m}\right)\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{1}{2m}\left(\frac{1}{m}\alpha_{2}+2b_{0}\right)^{2}b^{2m}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\frac{1}{m}\left(\frac{1}{m}\alpha_{2}+2b_{0}\right)\alpha_{2}\left(b^{2m}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)+\frac{1}{2m}b^{2m}-\left(a^{2m}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)+\frac{1}{2m}a^{2m}\right)\right)\right\}.

Now, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Rad}(u)^{2}

\displaystyle=\left(\alpha_{1}r^{1-m}\log(r)+2a_{0}r^{1-m}+\alpha_{2}r^{m+1}\log(r)+2b_{0}r^{m+1}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\alpha_{1}^{2}r^{2-2m}\log^{2}(r)+4a_{0}^{2}r^{2-2m}+\alpha_{2}^{2}r^{2m+2}\log^{2}(r)+4b_{0}^{2}r^{2m+2}+4\alpha_{1}a_{0}r^{2-2m}\log(r)+4\alpha_{2}b_{0}r^{2m+2}\log(r)

\displaystyle+2\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}r^{2}\log^{2}(r)+4(\alpha_{1}b_{0}+\alpha_{2}a_{0})r^{2}\log(r)+8a_{0}b_{0}r^{2},

which yields for all
1/2<\beta<m/2

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\frac{\mathrm{Rad}(u)^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{4\beta}+\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{4\beta}\right)dx=\frac{2\pi}{b^{4\beta}}\int_{a}^{b}\left(\alpha_{1}^{2}r^{-2m-1+4\beta}\log^{2}(r)+4a_{0}^{2}r^{-2m-1+4\beta}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\alpha_{2}^{2}r^{2m-1+4\beta}\log^{2}(r)+4b_{0}^{2}r^{2m-1+4\beta}+4\alpha_{1}a_{0}r^{-2m-1+4\beta}\log(r)+2\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}r^{-1+4\beta}\log^{2}(r)\right.

\displaystyle\left.+4(\alpha_{1}b_{0}+\alpha_{2}a_{0})r^{-1+4\beta}\log(r)+8a_{0}b_{0}r^{-1+4\beta}\right)dr

\displaystyle+2\pi\,a^{4\beta}\int_{a}^{b}\left(\alpha_{1}^{2}r^{-2m-1-4\beta}\log^{2}(r)+4a_{0}^{2}r^{-2m-1-4\beta}+\alpha_{2}^{2}r^{2m-1-4\beta}\log^{2}(r)+4b_{0}^{2}r^{2m-1-4\beta}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+4\alpha_{1}a_{0}r^{-2m-1-4\beta}\log(r)+2\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}r^{-1-4\beta}\log(r)+4(\alpha_{1}b_{0}+\alpha_{2}a_{0})r^{-1-4\beta}\log^{2}(r)+8a_{0}b_{0}r^{-1-4\beta}\right)dr

\displaystyle=2\pi\bigg\{\frac{\alpha_{1}^{2}}{2m-4\beta}\left(\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\left(\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)-\frac{1}{m-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)+\frac{1}{2(m-2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\left(\frac{1}{b^{2m}}\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)-\frac{1}{m-2\beta}\frac{1}{b^{2m}}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)+\frac{1}{2(m-2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{b^{2m}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{m-2\beta}a_{0}^{2}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\frac{1}{a^{4m}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4m-2\beta}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\alpha_{2}^{2}}{2m+4\beta}\left(b^{2m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)+\frac{1}{m+2\beta}b^{2m}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)+\frac{1}{2(m+2\beta)^{2}}b^{2m}\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\left(a^{2m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)+\frac{1}{m+2\beta}a^{2m}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)+\frac{1}{2(m+2\beta)}a^{2m}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{m+2\beta}b_{0}^{2}b^{2m}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m+4\beta}\right)-\frac{2\alpha_{1}a_{0}}{m-2\beta}\left(\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\left(\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)-\frac{1}{2m-4\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\left(\frac{1}{b^{2m}}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)-\frac{1}{2m-4\beta}\frac{1}{b^{2m}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{2\alpha_{2}b_{0}}{m+2\beta}\left(b^{2m}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)+\frac{1}{2m+4\beta}b^{2m}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\left(a^{2m}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)+\frac{1}{2m+4\beta}a^{2m}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}}{4\beta}\left(\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)+\frac{1}{2\beta}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)+\frac{1}{8\beta^{2}}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\left(\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)+\frac{1}{2\beta}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)+\frac{1}{8\beta^{2}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{\alpha_{1}b_{0}+\alpha_{2}a_{0}}{\beta}\left(\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)+\frac{1}{8\beta}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\left(\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)+\frac{1}{8\beta}\right)\right)+\frac{2a_{0}b_{0}}{\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\alpha_{1}^{2}}{2m+4\beta}\left(\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)-\frac{1}{m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)+\frac{1}{2(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\left(\frac{1}{b^{2m}}\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)\right)-\frac{1}{m+2\beta}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)+\frac{1}{2(m+2\beta)}\frac{1}{b^{2m}}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{m+2\beta}a_{0}^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m+4\beta}\right)+\frac{\alpha_{2}^{2}}{m-2\beta}\left(\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\left(b^{2m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)+\frac{1}{m-2\beta}b^{2m}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)+\frac{1}{2(m-2\beta)^{2}}b^{2m}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\left(a^{2m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)+\frac{1}{m-2\beta}a^{2m}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)\right)+\frac{1}{2(m-2\beta)^{2}}a^{2m}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{m-2\beta}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}b^{2m}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m-4\beta}\right)-\frac{2\alpha_{1}a_{0}}{m+2\beta}\left(\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)-\frac{1}{2m+4\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\left(\frac{1}{b^{2m}}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)-\frac{1}{2m+4\beta}\frac{1}{b^{2m}}\right)\right)-\frac{\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}}{2\beta}\left(\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)-\frac{1}{4\beta}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\left(\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)-\frac{1}{4\beta}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\alpha_{1}b_{0}+\alpha_{2}a_{0}}{\beta}\left(\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)-\frac{1}{2\beta}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)+\frac{1}{8\beta^{2}}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\left(\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)-\frac{1}{4\beta}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)+\frac{1}{8\beta^{2}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2a_{0}b_{0}}{\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)\bigg\}.

Finally, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{r^{2}}\left|\partial_{r}\mathrm{Rad}(u)+\frac{m-1}{r}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right|^{2}=\alpha_{1}r^{-2m-2}

\displaystyle+4m^{2}\,r^{2m-2}\left(\alpha_{2}^{2}\log^{2}(r)+\left(\frac{1}{2m}\alpha_{2}+2b_{0}\right)^{2}+2\left(\frac{1}{2m}\alpha_{2}+2b_{0}\right)\alpha_{2}\log(r)\right)

\displaystyle+4m\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}r^{-2}\log(r)+4m\alpha_{1}\left(\frac{1}{2m}\alpha_{2}+2b_{0}\right)r^{-2}.

We deduce that

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\frac{1}{|x|^{2}}\left|\partial_{r}\mathrm{Rad}(u)+\frac{m-1}{r}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right|^{2}\left(\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}+\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)dx

\displaystyle=\frac{2\pi}{b^{2\gamma}}\int_{a}^{b}\bigg\{\alpha_{1}^{2}r^{-2m-1+2\gamma}+4m^{2}\alpha_{2}^{2}r^{2m-1+2\gamma}\log^{2}(r)+4m^{2}\left(\frac{1}{2m}\alpha_{2}+2b_{0}\right)^{2}r^{2m-1+2\gamma}

\displaystyle+8m^{2}\left(\frac{1}{2m}\alpha_{2}+2b_{0}\right)\alpha_{2}r^{2m-1+2\gamma}\log(r)+4m\,\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}r^{-1+2\gamma}\log(r)+4m\,\alpha_{1}\left(\frac{1}{2m}\alpha_{2}+2b_{0}\right)r^{-1+2\gamma}\bigg\}dr

\displaystyle+2\pi\,a^{2\gamma}\int_{a}^{b}\bigg\{\alpha_{1}^{2}r^{-2m-1-2\gamma}+4m^{2}\alpha_{2}^{2}r^{2m-1-2\gamma}\log^{2}(r)+4m^{2}\left(\frac{1}{2m}\alpha_{2}+2b_{0}\right)^{2}r^{2m-1-2\gamma}

\displaystyle+8m^{2}\left(\frac{1}{2m}\alpha_{2}+2b_{0}\right)\alpha_{2}\,r^{2m-1-2\gamma}\log(r)+4m\,\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}r^{-1-2\gamma}\log(r)+4m\,\alpha_{1}\left(\frac{1}{2m}\alpha_{2}+2b_{0}\right)r^{-1-2\gamma}\bigg\}dr

\displaystyle=2\pi\bigg\{\frac{\alpha_{1}^{2}}{2(m-\gamma)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-\gamma)}\right)+\frac{2m^{2}}{m+\gamma}\left(b^{2m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)+\frac{1}{m+\gamma}b^{2m}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)+\frac{1}{2(m+\gamma)^{2}}b^{2m}\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\left(a^{2m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)+\frac{1}{m+\gamma}a^{2m}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)+\frac{1}{2(m+\gamma)^{2}}a^{2m}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2m^{2}}{m+\gamma}\left(\frac{1}{m}\alpha_{2}+2b_{0}\right)^{2}b^{2m}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+\gamma)}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{4m^{2}}{m+\gamma}\left(\frac{1}{2m}\alpha_{2}+2b_{0}\right)\alpha_{2}\left(b^{2m}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)-\frac{1}{2(m+\gamma)}b^{2m}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\left(a^{2m}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)-\frac{1}{2(m+\gamma)}a^{2m}\right)\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{2m}{\gamma}\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}\left(\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)-\frac{1}{2\gamma}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\left(\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)-\frac{1}{2\gamma}\right)\right)+\frac{2m}{\gamma}\alpha_{1}\left(\frac{1}{2m}\alpha_{2}+2b_{0}\right)\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\alpha_{1}^{2}}{2(m+\gamma)}\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+\gamma)}\right)+\frac{2m^{2}}{m-\gamma}\left(\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\left(b^{2m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)+\frac{1}{m-\gamma}b^{2m}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)+\frac{1}{2(m-\gamma)^{2}}b^{2m}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\left(a^{2m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)+\frac{1}{m-\gamma}a^{2m}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)+\frac{1}{2(m-\gamma)^{2}}a^{2m}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2m^{2}}{m-\gamma}\left(\frac{1}{2m}\alpha_{2}+2b_{0}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}b^{2m}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-\gamma)}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{4m^{2}}{m-\gamma}\left(\frac{1}{m}\alpha_{2}+2b_{0}\right)\alpha_{2}\left(\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\left(b^{2m}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)-\frac{1}{2(m-\gamma)}b^{2m}\right)-\left(a^{2m}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)-\frac{1}{2(m-\gamma)}a^{2m}\right)\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{2m}{\gamma}\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}\left(\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)-\frac{1}{2\gamma}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\left(\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)-\frac{1}{2\gamma}\right)\right)+\frac{2m}{\gamma}\alpha_{1}\left(\frac{1}{2m}\alpha_{2}+2b_{0}\right)\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)\bigg\}.

Recalling that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Rad}(u)=\alpha_{1}r^{1-m}\log(r)+2a_{0}r^{1-m}+\alpha_{2}r^{m+1}\log(r)+2b_{0}r^{m+1}

and

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)=4m^{2}r^{m-1}\left(\alpha_{2}\log(r)+\dfrac{1}{m}\alpha_{2}+2b_{0}\right),

(2.110)

we apply the previous estimate (
2.47
) to
f_{1}=r^{m-1}\log(r)

f_{2}=\left(\dfrac{1}{m}\alpha_{2}+2b_{0}\right)r^{m-1}
, and
(\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2})=\left(\alpha_{2},\dfrac{1}{m}\alpha_{2}+2b_{0}\right)
. Notice that we can assume without generality (otherwise, the estimate would be trivial as the integral of a sum of positive functions) that

\displaystyle\lambda_{1}\lambda_{2}=\left(\frac{1}{m}\alpha_{2}+2b_{0}\right)\alpha_{2}>0.

Thanks to (
2.3.1
), we have

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}|f_{1}(x)f_{2}(x)-f_{1}(y)f_{2}(y)|^{2}dx\,dy

\displaystyle=2\pi\int_{a}^{b}\int_{a}^{b}r^{2m-1}s^{2m-1}\log^{2}\left(\frac{r}{s}\right)dr\,ds

\displaystyle=\frac{\pi}{4m^{4}}b^{4m}\left\{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)^{2}-4m^{2}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\right\}.

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}|f_{1}|^{2}dx=2\pi\int_{a}^{b}r^{2m-1}\log^{2}(r)dr

\displaystyle=\frac{\pi}{m}\left(b^{2m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)-a^{2m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)+\frac{1}{m}\left(b^{2m}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)-a^{2m}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)\right)+\frac{1}{2m^{2}}b^{2m}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)\right),

while

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}|f_{2}|^{2}dx=2\pi\int_{a}^{b}r^{2m-1}dr=\frac{\pi}{m}b^{2m}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right).

Then, assuming that
b\leq e^{-1}
, we trivially have

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}|f_{1}|^{2}dx\leq\frac{\pi}{m}\left(1+\frac{1}{m}+\frac{1}{2m^{2}}\right)b^{2m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{b}\right),

which yields the estimate

\displaystyle\left(\int_{\Omega}|f_{1}|^{2}dx\right)\left(\int_{\Omega}|f_{2}|^{2}dx\right)\leq\frac{\pi^{2}}{2m^{2}}\left(1+\frac{1}{m}+\frac{1}{2m^{2}}\right)b^{4m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)\leq\frac{2\pi^{2}}{m^{2}}b^{4m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{b}\right).

Therefore, we get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4m^{3}\sqrt{2}}\left(\frac{1}{m}\alpha_{2}+2b_{0}\right)\alpha_{2}\frac{b^{2m}}{\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)}\left\{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)^{2}-4m^{2}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{\Omega}\left(\alpha_{2}\log(r)+\frac{1}{2}\alpha_{2}+2b_{0}\right)r^{2m-1}dr=\frac{1}{16\pi m^{4}}\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right)^{2}dx,

which implies that

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{m}\alpha_{2}+2b_{0}\right)\alpha_{2}\frac{b^{2m}}{\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)}\leq\frac{1}{2m\pi\sqrt{2}}\frac{1}{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)^{2}-4m^{2}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)}\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right)^{2}dx.

Therefore, using the identity

\displaystyle\frac{1}{16\pi m^{3}}\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right)^{2}dx=\alpha_{2}^{2}\left(b^{2m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)-a^{2m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)+\frac{1}{m}\left(b^{2m}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)-a^{2m}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{1}{2m^{2}}b^{2m}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)\right)+\left(\frac{1}{m}\alpha_{2}+2b_{0}\right)^{2}b^{2m}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)

\displaystyle-2\left(\frac{1}{m}\alpha_{2}+2b_{0}\right)\alpha_{2}\left(b^{2m}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)+\frac{1}{2m}b^{2m}-\left(a^{2m}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)+\frac{1}{2m}a^{2m}\right)\right),

we deduce that

\displaystyle\alpha_{2}^{2}\left(b^{2m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)-a^{2m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)+\frac{1}{m}\left(b^{2m}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)-a^{2m}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{1}{2m^{2}}b^{2m}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)\right)+\left(\frac{1}{m}\alpha_{2}+2b_{0}\right)^{2}b^{2m}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{1}{16\pi m^{3}}+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{\pi\,m}\frac{\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)}{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)^{2}-4m^{2}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)}\right)\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right)^{2}dx,

which implies that

\displaystyle\alpha_{2}^{2}b^{2m}\leq\frac{1}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\frac{\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)}{\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)}}\left(\frac{1}{16\pi m^{3}}+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{\pi\,m}\frac{1}{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)^{2}-4m^{2}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)}\right)\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right)^{2}dx.

(2.111)

On can check that using Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, the estimate
cannot
be bootstrapped to recover the
\log^{2}(1/b)
factor. There seems to be no way to get estimates for
a_{0}
and
b_{0}
using the weighted integral of
u^{2}
to get the missing logarithm factors, so we will use another argument and estimate
\alpha_{1}^{2}a^{-2m}
in a similar fashion. First, notice that the identity (
2.3.1
) holds for all
\gamma\in\mathbb{C}
by analytic continuation (one needs to compute the limit when
\gamma\rightarrow 0
, but we are only interested in non-zero values of
\gamma
, and since the left-hand side is non-singular in
\gamma
, we actually get the analytic continuation on
\mathbb{C}
immediately). Notice that for
\gamma=0
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{a}^{b}\int_{a}^{b}\log^{2}\left(\frac{r}{s}\right)\frac{dr}{r}\frac{ds}{s}

\displaystyle=2\left(\int_{a}^{b}\log^{2}(r)\frac{dr}{r}\right)\left(\int_{a}^{b}\frac{ds}{s}\right)-2\left(\int_{a}^{b}\log(t)\frac{dt}{t}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{3}\log^{3}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\times\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)-2\left(\frac{1}{2}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\right)^{2}=\frac{1}{6}\log^{4}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right),

which yields

\displaystyle\lim_{\gamma\rightarrow 0}\frac{1}{8\gamma^{4}}\left\{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)^{2}-4\gamma^{2}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\right\}=\frac{1}{6}\log^{4}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right).

Therefore, thanks to (
2.56
), we deduce that for all
\gamma>0
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{a}^{b}\log^{2}\left(\frac{r}{s}\right)\frac{dr}{r^{2\gamma+1}}\frac{ds}{s^{2\gamma+1}}=\frac{1}{8\gamma^{4}}\frac{1}{a^{4\gamma}}\left\{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)^{2}-4\gamma^{2}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\right\}.

Now, recall that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{\Omega}\left(\frac{1}{2}\mathscr{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)-\frac{2m}{m-1}\frac{z^{2}}{|z|^{2}}\mathfrak{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right)^{2}dx=\frac{\alpha_{2}^{2}}{2m}b^{2m}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)

\displaystyle+2m\,\alpha_{1}^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)-\frac{1}{m}\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)+\frac{1}{2m^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2m}}-\left(\frac{1}{b^{2m}}\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)-\frac{1}{m}\frac{1}{b^{2m}}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)+\frac{1}{2m^{2}}\frac{1}{b^{2m}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+2m\left(\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2(m-1)}+2a_{0}\right)^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)

\displaystyle-4m\left(\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2(m-1)}+2a_{0}\right)\alpha_{1}\left(\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)-\frac{1}{2m}\frac{1}{a^{2m}}-\left(\frac{1}{b^{2m}}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)-\frac{1}{2m}\frac{1}{b^{2m}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+2m\,\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}\left(\log^{2}(b)-\log^{2}(a)\right)+2m\left(\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2(m-1)}+2a_{0}\right)\alpha_{2}\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right).

In fact, there is an easier way to get the needed estimate since we already have a control on
\alpha_{2}
. Also write for simplicity

\displaystyle\mathfrak{D}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)=\frac{1}{2}\mathscr{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)-\frac{2m}{m-1}\frac{z^{2}}{|z|^{2}}\mathfrak{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)=\alpha_{2}\,r^{m-1}+2m\,r^{-m-1}\left(\alpha_{1}\log(r)+\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2(m-1)}+2a_{0}\right).

We have

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathfrak{D}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right)^{2}dx\geq 2m^{2}\int_{a}^{b}r^{-2m-2}\left(\alpha_{1}\log(r)+\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2(m-1)}+2a_{0}\right)^{2}r\,dr-\alpha_{2}^{2}\int_{a}^{b}r^{2m-1}dr

\displaystyle=2m^{2}\int_{a}^{b}r^{-2m-2}\left(\alpha_{1}\log(r)+\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2(m-1)}+2a_{0}\right)^{2}r\,dr-\frac{\alpha_{2}^{2}}{2m}b^{2m}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right).

Therefore, we get the estimate

\displaystyle 2m^{2}\int_{a}^{b}r^{-2m-2}\left(\alpha_{1}\log(r)+\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2(m-1)}+2a_{0}\right)^{2}r\,dr\leq\frac{\alpha_{2}^{2}}{2m}b^{2m}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)+\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathfrak{D}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right)^{2}dx

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\frac{\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)}{\log^{2}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)}}\left(\frac{1}{16\pi m^{3}}+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{\pi\,m}\frac{1}{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)^{2}-4m^{2}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)}\right)\right)\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right)^{2}dx

\displaystyle+\frac{8m^{2}}{(m-1)^{2}}\int_{\Omega}\left|\mathfrak{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right|^{2}|dz|^{2}.

(2.112)

Therefore, we are reduced to the previous analysis. If

\displaystyle f(x)=|x|^{-(m+1)}\left(\alpha_{1}\log(r)+\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2(m-1)}+2a_{0}\right),

we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{\Omega}|f(x)|^{2}dx=\alpha_{1}^{2}\int_{a}^{b}r^{-2m-1}\log^{2}(r)dr+\left(\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2(m-1)}+2a_{0}\right)^{2}\int_{a}^{b}r^{-2m-1}dr

\displaystyle+2\alpha_{1}\left(\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2(m-1)}+2a_{0}\right)\int_{a}^{b}r^{-2m-1}\log(r)dr

\displaystyle=\alpha_{1}^{2}\left[-\frac{1}{2m}r^{-2m}\log^{2}(r)-\frac{1}{4m^{2}}r^{-2m}\log(r)-\frac{1}{8m^{3}}r^{-2m}\right]_{a}^{b}+\left(\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2(m-1)}+2a_{0}\right)^{2}\frac{1}{2m}\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)

\displaystyle+2\alpha_{1}\left(\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2(m-1)}+2a_{0}\right)\left[-\frac{1}{2m}r^{-2m}\log(r)-\frac{1}{4m^{2}}r^{-2m}\right]_{a}^{b}

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha_{1}^{2}}{2m}\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\frac{\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)}{\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)}\right)-\frac{\alpha_{1}^{2}}{4m^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)}{\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\alpha_{1}^{2}}{8m^{3}}\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)-\frac{\alpha_{1}}{m}\left(\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2(m-1)}+2a_{0}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)}{\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2m^{2}}\left(\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2(m-1)}+2a_{0}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right).

As previously, the estimates are trivial if

\displaystyle\alpha_{1}\left(\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2(m-1)}+2a_{0}\right)\leq 0,

so we assume that

\displaystyle\alpha_{1}\left(\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2(m-1)}+2a_{0}\right)>0.

Therefore, we will get an estimate thanks to (
2.47
). We readily estimate

\displaystyle\int_{a}^{b}r^{-2m-1}\log^{2}(r)dr\int_{a}^{b}s^{-2m-1}ds\leq\frac{1}{2m^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{4m}}\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a}\right).

Therefore, we deduce that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4m^{3}\sqrt{2}}\alpha_{1}\left(\frac{\alpha_{1}}{2(m-1)}+2a_{0}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\frac{1}{\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)}\left\{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)^{2}-4m^{2}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{a}^{b}\left(\alpha_{1}\log(r)+\frac{1}{2(m-1)}\alpha_{1}+2a_{0}\right)^{2}r^{-(2m+1)}dr=\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{\Omega}|f(x)|^{2}dx,

and we get

\displaystyle\alpha_{1}\left(\frac{1}{2(m-1)}\alpha_{1}+2a_{0}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\frac{1}{\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)}\leq\frac{2m^{3}\sqrt{2}}{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)^{2}-4m^{2}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)}\int_{\Omega}|f(x)|^{2}dx.

Therefore, we deduce that

\displaystyle\alpha_{1}^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)\left(1-\frac{1}{2m}\frac{1}{\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)}\right)\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\frac{\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)}{\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{m}{\pi}+\frac{4m^{3}\sqrt{2}}{\pi}\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)}{\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)}}{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)^{2}-4m^{2}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)}\right)\int_{\Omega}|f(x)|^{2}dx.

Since
a<b\leq e^{-1}
by hypothesis, we deduce that

\displaystyle\alpha_{1}^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\leq\frac{1}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\frac{\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)}{\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)}}\left(\frac{2m}{\pi}+\frac{8m^{3}\sqrt{2}}{\pi}\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)}{\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)}}{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)^{2}-4m^{2}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)}\right)\int_{\Omega}|f(x)|^{2}dx

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\frac{\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)}{\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)}}\left(\frac{2m}{\pi}+\frac{8m^{3}\sqrt{2}}{\pi}\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)}{\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)}}{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)^{2}-4m^{2}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)}\right)

\displaystyle\times\left\{\left(\frac{1}{4m^{2}}+\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\frac{\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)}{\log^{2}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)}}\left(\frac{1}{32\pi m^{4}}+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2\pi\,m^{2}}\frac{1}{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)^{2}-4m^{2}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)}\right)\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\times\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right)^{2}dx+\frac{2}{(m-1)^{2}}\int_{\Omega}\left|\mathfrak{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right|^{2}|dz|^{2}\right\}.

(2.113)

We can now conclude the proof of the radial estimate. We rewrite and use the elementary estimate

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{i=1}^{d}a_{i}\right)^{2}\leq d\sum_{i=1}^{d}a_{i}^{2}\qquad\forall d\in\mathbb{N},\;\forall\left\{a_{1},\cdots,a_{d}\right\}\subset\mathbb{R},

(2.114)

to show that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{r^{2}}\left|\partial_{r}\mathrm{Rad}(u)+\frac{m-1}{r}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right|^{2}=\frac{1}{r^{2}}\left|\alpha_{1}\,r^{-m}+2m\,r^{m}\left(\alpha_{2}\log(r)+\frac{1}{2m}\alpha_{2}+2b_{0}\right)\right|^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{r^{2}}\left|\alpha_{1}\,r^{-m}-\alpha_{2}\,r^{m}+2m\,r^{m}\left(\alpha_{2}\log(r)+\frac{1}{m}\alpha_{2}+2b_{0}\right)\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3}{r^{2}}\left(\alpha_{1}^{2}r^{-2m}+\alpha_{2}^{2}r^{2m}+4m^{2}r^{2m}\left(\alpha_{2}^{2}\log^{2}(r)+\left(\frac{1}{m}\alpha_{2}+2b_{0}\right)^{2}+2\alpha_{2}\left(\frac{1}{m}\alpha_{2}+2b_{0}\right)\log(r)\right)\right).

Therefore, we have by (
2.110
), (
2.111
), and (
2.3.2
)

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\frac{1}{|x|^{2}}\left|\partial_{r}\mathrm{Rad}(u)+\frac{m-1}{r}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right|^{2}dx\leq 6\pi\int_{a}^{b}\alpha_{1}^{2}\,r^{-(2m+1)}dr+6\pi\int_{a}^{b}\alpha_{2}^{2}r^{2m-1}dr+\frac{1}{4m^{2}}\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right)^{2}dx

\displaystyle=\frac{3\pi}{m}\alpha_{1}^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2m}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)+\frac{3\pi}{m}\alpha_{2}^{2}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)+\frac{1}{4m^{2}}\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right)^{2}dx

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\frac{\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)}{\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)}}\left(6+24m^{2}\sqrt{2}\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\frac{\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)}{\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)}}{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)^{2}-4m^{2}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)}\right)

\displaystyle\times\left\{\left(\frac{1}{4m^{2}}+\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\frac{\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)}{\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)}}\left(\frac{1}{32\pi m^{4}}+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2\pi\,m^{2}}\frac{1}{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)^{2}-4m^{2}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)}\right)\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\times\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right)^{2}dx+\frac{2}{(m-1)^{2}}\int_{\Omega}\left|\mathfrak{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right|^{2}|dz|^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\frac{\log^{2}\left(a\right)}{\log^{2}(b)}}\left(\frac{3}{16m^{4}}+\frac{3\sqrt{2}}{m^{2}}\frac{1}{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)^{2}-4m^{2}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)}\right)\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right)^{2}dx

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4m^{2}}\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right)^{2}dx.

(2.115)

Step 9.
The final estimates.
Therefore, we now need only control the angular part of

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\frac{1}{|x|^{2}}\left|\nabla u+(m-1)\frac{x}{|x|^{2}}u\right|^{2}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2\gamma}dx

to conclude the proof. Write

\displaystyle u_{0}=u-\mathrm{Rad(u)}=2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\left(a_{n}r^{1-m+n}+b_{n}r^{m+1+n}\right)e^{in\theta}\right).

Then, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{r}\partial_{\theta}u_{0}

\displaystyle=2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}n\left(a_{n}r^{-m+n}+b_{n}r^{m+n}\right)e^{in\theta}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}n\left(a_{n}\,r^{-m+n}-\overline{a_{-n}}r^{-m-n}+b_{n}\,r^{m+n}-\overline{b_{-n}}r^{m-n}\right)e^{in\theta}

and

\displaystyle\partial_{r}u_{0}+\frac{m-1}{r}u=2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\left((1-m+n)a_{n}r^{-m+n}+(m+1+n)b_{n}r^{m+n}\right)e^{in\theta}\right)

\displaystyle+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\left((m-1)a_{n}r^{-m+n}+(m-1)b_{n}r^{m+n}\right)e^{in\theta}\right)

\displaystyle=2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\left(n\,a_{n}r^{-m+n}+(2m+n)b_{n}r^{m+n}\right)e^{in\theta}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\left(n\,a_{n}r^{-m+n}-n\,\overline{a_{-n}}r^{-m-n}+(2m+n)b_{n}r^{m+n}+(2m-n)\overline{b_{-n}}r^{m-n}\right)e^{in\theta},

which implies that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Rad}\left(\left|\nabla u_{0}+(m-1)\frac{x}{|x|^{2}}u_{0}\right|^{2}\right)=\mathrm{Rad}\left(\left|\partial_{r}u_{0}+\frac{(m-1)}{r}u_{0}\right|^{2}+\frac{1}{r^{2}}\left|\partial_{\theta}u_{0}\right|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}n^{2}\left|a_{n}r^{-m+n}-\overline{a_{-n}}+b_{n}r^{m+n}-\overline{b_{-n}}r^{m-n}\right|^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\left|n\,a_{n}r^{-m+n}-n\,a_{-n}r^{m-n}+(2m+n)b_{n}r^{m+n}+(2m-n)\overline{b_{-n}}r^{m-n}\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 8\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}n^{2}|a_{n}|^{2}r^{-2m+2n}+8\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}n^{2}|b_{n}|^{2}r^{2m-2n}+8\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}n^{2}|a_{n}|^{2}r^{-2m+2n}+8\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}(2m+n)^{2}|b_{n}|^{2}r^{2m+2n}

\displaystyle=16\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}n^{2}|a_{n}|^{2}r^{-2m+2n}+8\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\left(n^{2}+(2m+n)^{2}\right)|b_{n}|^{2}r^{2m+2n},

where we used the elementary inequality (
2.114
) with
d=4
. Therefore, we deduce that for all
0<\gamma<1
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\frac{1}{|x|^{2}}\left|\nabla u_{0}+(m-1)\frac{x}{|x|^{2}}u_{0}\right|^{2}\frac{1}{|x|^{2\gamma}}dx\leq 32\pi\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\int_{a}^{b}n^{2}|a_{n}|^{2}r^{-2m+2n-1-2\gamma}dr

\displaystyle+16\pi\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\int_{a}^{b}\left(n^{2}+(2m+n)^{2}\right)|b_{n}|^{2}r^{2m+2n-1-2\gamma}dr

\displaystyle=16\pi\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}\frac{n^{2}|a_{n}|^{2}}{n-(m+\gamma)}b^{2(n-(m+\gamma)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-(m+\gamma)}\right)+16\pi\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n=-m\\
n\neq 0\end{subarray}}^{\infty}\frac{n^{2}|a_{-n}|^{2}}{n+m+\gamma}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m+\gamma)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m+\gamma)}\right)

\displaystyle+8\pi\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}n=1-m\\
n\neq 0\end{subarray}}^{\infty}\frac{n^{2}+(2m+n)^{2}}{m+n-\gamma}|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(m+n-\gamma)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m-\gamma)}\right)

\displaystyle+8\pi\sum_{n=m}^{\infty}\frac{n^{2}+(2m-n)^{2}}{n-m+\gamma}|b_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-m+\gamma)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-m+\gamma)}\right).

(2.116)

Now, recall (
2.3.2
) (notice that we can replace
u
by
u_{0}
since the radial part plays no rule in the estimate)

\displaystyle 2\,m^{2}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}(n+m)|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n+m)}+2\,m^{2}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}(n-m)|b_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-m)}}+\frac{m^{2}}{800}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}n|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n-m)}

\displaystyle+\frac{m^{4}(m-1)^{2}}{128(3m-1)^{7}}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}n|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m)}}\leq\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}u_{0}\right)^{2}dx+\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{\Omega}\left|\mathfrak{L}_{m}u_{0}\right|^{2}|dz|^{2}.

(2.117)

Let
\alpha>1
to be determined later such that the following inequality holds for all
n\geq m+1

\displaystyle\frac{n^{2}}{n-(m+\gamma)}\leq\alpha\,n.

This condition is equivalent to

\displaystyle\alpha\geq\frac{n}{n-(m+\gamma)}=1+\frac{m+\gamma}{n-(m+\gamma)}

and the right hand-side is maximal for
n=m+1
, so we get the estimate thanks to (
2.3.2
)

\displaystyle 16\pi\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}\frac{n^{2}|a_{n}|^{2}}{n-(m+1)}b^{2(n-(m+\gamma)}

\displaystyle\leq 16\pi\left(1+\frac{m+\gamma}{1-\gamma}\right)\frac{1}{b^{2\gamma}}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}n|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n-m)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{6400}{m^{2}}\frac{m+1}{1-\gamma}\frac{1}{b^{2\gamma}}\left(\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}u_{0}\right)^{2}dx+\int_{\Omega}\left|\mathfrak{L}_{m}u_{0}\right|^{2}|dz|^{2}\right).

(2.118)

Now, we directly estimate

\displaystyle 16\pi\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}\frac{n^{2}|a_{-n}|^{2}}{n+m+\gamma}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m+\gamma)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m+\gamma)}\right)\leq 16\pi\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}n|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m)}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1024(3m-1)^{7}}{m^{4}(m-1)^{2}}\left(\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}u_{0}\right)^{2}dx+\int_{\Omega}\left|\mathfrak{L}_{m}u_{0}\right|^{2}|dz|^{2}\right).

(2.119)

Now, we look for
\alpha>1
such that

\displaystyle\frac{n^{2}+(2m+n)^{2}}{m+n-\gamma}\leq\alpha(n+m)\quad\text{for all}\;\,n\geq m+1.

Equivalently, we get

\displaystyle(\alpha-2)n^{2}+\left(2(\alpha-2)m-\alpha\gamma\right)n-4m^{2}-\alpha\gamma m\geq 0.

Assuming that
\alpha>2
, we deduce that the condition is given by

\displaystyle n\geq\frac{-2(\alpha-2)m+\alpha\gamma+\sqrt{\left(2\alpha-2)m-\alpha\gamma\right)^{2}+4(\alpha-2)m(4m-\alpha\gamma)}}{2(\alpha-2)}\qquad\text{for all}\;\,n\geq m+1,

which is equivalent to

\displaystyle 4(\alpha-2)^{2}\left(m+1\right)^{2}+4(\alpha-2)(m+1)\left(2(\alpha-2)m-\alpha\gamma\right)\geq 4(\alpha-2)m(4m-\alpha\gamma).

Since
4(\alpha-2)m(4m-\alpha\gamma)\leq 16(\alpha-2)m^{2}
, the condition is satisfied provided that
\alpha\geq 6
and
2(\alpha-2)m-\alpha\gamma\geq 0
, so we can take

\displaystyle\alpha=\max\left\{6,\frac{4m}{2m-\gamma}\right\}=6,

and we deduce that

\displaystyle 8\pi\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}\frac{n^{2}+(2m+n)^{2}}{m+n-\gamma}|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(m+n-\gamma)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m-\gamma)}\right)\leq 48\pi\frac{1}{b^{2\gamma}}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}n|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(m+n)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{24}{m^{2}}\frac{1}{b^{2\gamma}}\left(\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}u_{0}\right)^{2}dx+\int_{\Omega}\left|\mathfrak{L}_{m}\right|^{2}|dz|^{2}\right).

Now, we look for
\alpha>1
such that for all
n\geq m+1
,

\displaystyle\frac{n^{2}+(2m-n)^{2}}{n-m+\gamma}\leq\alpha(n-m).

Writing
x=n-m
, the condition is equivalent to

\displaystyle\alpha\geq\frac{(x+m)^{2}+(x-m)^{2}}{x^{2}+\gamma x}=\frac{2x^{2}+2m^{2}}{x^{2}+\gamma x}=2+\frac{2m^{2}-2\gamma x}{x^{2}+\gamma x}

and since
x\geq 1
, we can take

\displaystyle\alpha=\frac{2\left(m^{2}+1+\gamma\right)}{1+\gamma}

and we get

\displaystyle 8\pi\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}\frac{n^{2}+(2m-n)^{2}}{n-m+\gamma}|b_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-m+\gamma)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-m+\gamma)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{16\pi(m^{2}+1+\gamma)}{1+\gamma}\frac{1}{a^{2\gamma}}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}(n-m)|b_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-m)}}\leq\frac{8(m^{2}+1+\gamma)}{(1+\gamma)m^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2\gamma}}\left(\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}u\right)^{2}+\int_{\Omega}\left|\mathfrak{L}_{m}\right|^{2}|dz|^{2}\right).

Therefore, we need only estimate the remaining terms for
-m\leq n\leq m
, which will be obtained by an elementary argument. We have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Rad}\left(u_{0}^{2}\right)=\mathrm{Rad}\left|\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\left(a_{n}\,r^{1-m+n}+\overline{a_{-n}}r^{1-m-n}+b_{n}\,r^{m+1+n}+\overline{b_{-n}}\,r^{m+1-n}\right)e^{in\theta}\right|^{2}

\displaystyle=2\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}|a_{n}|^{2}r^{2-2m+2n}+2\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}|b_{n}|^{2}r^{2m+2+2n}+2\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}a_{-n}\right)r^{2-2m}+2\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}b_{-n}\right)r^{2m+2}

\displaystyle+4\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)r^{2+2n}+4\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}b_{-n}\right)r^{2}.

Therefore, for all
\frac{1}{2}<\beta<\min\left\{\frac{m}{2},1\right\}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{\Omega}\frac{u_{0}^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\frac{dx}{|x|^{4\beta}}=2\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}|a_{n}|^{2}\int_{a}^{b}r^{-2m+2n-1-4\beta}dr+2\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}|b_{n}|^{2}\int_{a}^{b}r^{2m+2n-1-4\beta}dr

\displaystyle+2\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}a_{-n}\right)\int_{a}^{b}r^{-2m-1-4\beta}dr+2\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\int_{a}^{b}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}b_{-n}\right)r^{2m-1-4\beta}dr

\displaystyle+4\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\int_{a}^{b}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)r^{2n-1-4\beta}dr+4\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{-n}}\right)\int_{a}^{b}r^{-1-4\beta}dr

\displaystyle=\sum_{n=m+2}^{\infty}\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{n-(m+2\beta)}b^{2(n-(m+2\beta))}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-(m+2\beta))}\right)+\sum_{n=-m-1}^{\infty}\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{n+m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=2-m}^{\infty}\frac{|b_{n}|^{2}}{n+m-2\beta}b^{2(n+m-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m-2\beta)}\right)+\sum_{n=m-1}^{\infty}\frac{|b_{-n}|^{2}}{n-m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-m+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{m+2\beta}\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}a_{-n}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2(m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)+\frac{2}{m-2\beta}\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}b_{-n}\right)b^{2(m-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+2\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\frac{\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)}{n-2\beta}b^{2(n-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-2\beta)}\right)+2\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\frac{\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-n}\overline{b_{-n}}\right)}{n+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{1}\overline{b_{1}}\right)}{2\beta-1}\frac{1}{a^{2(2\beta-1)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2\beta-1)}\right)+\frac{1}{\beta}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{-n}}\right)\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right).

Thanks to the previous estimates, we need only estimate the terms involving
a_{n}
and
b_{n}
in (
2.3.2
) for
1\leq|n|\leq m
. First, consider for
1\leq n\leq m
the following quantity

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{-n+m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(-n+m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(-n+m+2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{n+m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}a_{-n}\right)}{m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{-n+m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(-n+m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(-n+m+2\beta)}\right)-\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{(m+2\beta)^{2}}(n+m+2\beta)\frac{1}{a^{2(-n+m+2\beta)}}\frac{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m+2\beta)}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m+2\beta)}}\frac{1}{a^{2(-n+m+2\beta)}}\left(\frac{n^{2}}{(-n+m+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}-\frac{2}{-n+m+2\beta}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(-n+m+2\beta)}\right).

We therefore choose a conformal class such that

\displaystyle\frac{2}{-n+m+2\beta}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(-n+m+2\beta)}\leq\frac{n^{2}}{2(-n+m+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}},

which yields

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2(-n+m+2\beta)}\log\left(\frac{4(m+2\beta)^{2}}{n^{2}}\right)=\frac{1}{-n+m+2\beta}\log\left(\frac{2(m+2\beta)}{n}\right),

and this condition is implied by

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2\beta}\log\left(2(m+2\beta)\right)

(2.120)

and we get

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{-n+m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(-n+m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(-n+m+2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{n+m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}a_{-n}\right)}{m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m+2\beta)}}\frac{1}{a^{2(-n+m+2\beta)}}\frac{n^{2}}{2(-n+m+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}.

Likewise, we have

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{-n+m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(-n+m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(-n+m+2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{n+m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}a_{-n}\right)}{m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{n+m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m+2\beta)}\right)-\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{(m+2\beta)^{2}}(-n+m+2\beta)\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m+2\beta)}}\frac{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(-n+m+2\beta)}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(-n+m+2\beta)}}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m+2\beta)}}\left(\frac{n^{2}}{(n+m+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}-\frac{2}{n+m+2\beta}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(-n+m+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(-n+m+2\beta)}}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m+2\beta)}}\frac{n^{2}}{2(n+m+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}

if (
2.120
) is verified.
Now, consider for
1\leq n\leq m-2
the quantity

\displaystyle\frac{|b_{n}|^{2}}{n+m-2\beta}b^{2(n+m-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m-2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|b_{-n}|^{2}}{-n+m-2\beta}b^{2(-n+m-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(-n+m-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}b_{-n}\right)}{m-2\beta}b^{2(m-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|b_{n}|^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(-n+m-2\beta)}}b^{2(n+m-2\beta)}\left(\frac{n^{2}}{(n+m-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}-2\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(-n+m-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|b_{n}|^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(-n+m-2\beta)}}b^{2(n+m-2\beta)}\frac{n^{2}}{2(n+m-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}

provided that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2(1-\beta)}\log\left(2(m-2\beta)\right),

(2.121)

and the condition also shows that

\displaystyle\frac{|b_{n}|^{2}}{n+m-2\beta}b^{2(n+m-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m-2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|b_{-n}|^{2}}{-n+m-2\beta}b^{2(-n+m-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(-n+m-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}b_{-n}\right)}{m-2\beta}b^{2(m-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|b_{-n}|^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m-2\beta)}}b^{2(-n+m-2\beta)}\frac{n^{2}}{2(-n+m-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}.

Finally, we get the first estimate if (
2.120
) and (
2.121
) are satisfied and
1\leq n\leq m-2
:

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{-n+m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(-n+m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(-n+m+2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{n+m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}a_{-n}\right)}{m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\frac{|b_{n}|^{2}}{n+m-2\beta}b^{2(n+m-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m-2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|b_{-n}|^{2}}{-n+m-2\beta}b^{2(-n+m-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(-n+m-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}b_{-n}\right)}{m-2\beta}b^{2(m-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n^{2}|a_{n}|^{2}}{4(-n+m+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(-n+m+2\beta)}}+\frac{n^{2}|a_{-n}|^{2}}{4(n+m+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m+2\beta)}}

\displaystyle+\frac{n^{2}|b_{n}|^{2}}{4(n+m-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}b^{2(n+m-2\beta)}+\frac{n^{2}|b_{-n}|^{2}}{4(-n+m-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}b^{2(-n+m-2\beta)}.

(2.122)

Now, we have for
2\leq n\leq m-2
(notice that the non-triviality of this range implies that
m\geq 4
)

\displaystyle\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)}{n-2\beta}b^{2(n-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{8(n+m-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}{(n-2\beta)^{2}n^{2}}|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n-m-2\beta)}-\frac{n^{2}|b_{n}|^{2}}{8(n+m-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}b^{2(n+m-2\beta)}.

Therefore, we get

\displaystyle\frac{n^{2}|a_{n}|^{2}}{4(-n+m+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(-n+m+2\beta)}}+\frac{n^{2}|b_{n}|^{2}}{4(n+m-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}b^{2(n+m-2\beta)}

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)}{n-2\beta}b^{2(n-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n^{2}|a_{n}|^{2}}{4(-n+m+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(-n+m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\frac{32\left(m^{2}-(n-2\beta)^{2}\right)\left(m^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)}{(n-2\beta)^{2}n^{4}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(-n+m+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{n^{2}|b_{n}|^{2}}{8(n+m-2\beta)\left(m^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)}b^{2(n+m-2\beta)}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n^{2}|a_{n}|^{2}}{8(-n+m+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(-n+m+2\beta)}}+\frac{n^{2}|b_{n}|^{2}}{8(n+m-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}b^{2(n+m-2\beta)}

provided that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2(-n+m+2\beta)}\log\left(\frac{64\left(m^{2}-(n-2\beta)^{2}\right)\left(m^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)}{(n-2\beta)^{2}n^{4}}\right),

which is satisfied in particular if

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{m-2(1-\beta)}\log\left(\frac{m^{2}}{1-\beta}\right).

(2.123)

Now, we estimate for
1\leq n\leq m-2

\displaystyle\frac{n^{2}|a_{-n}|^{2}}{4(n+m+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m+2\beta)}}+\frac{n^{2}|b_{-n}|^{2}}{4(-n+m-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}b^{2(-n+m-2\beta)}

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-n}\overline{b_{-n}}\right)}{n+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n^{2}|a_{-n}|^{2}}{8(n+m+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m+2\beta)}}+\frac{n^{2}|b_{-n}|^{2}}{4(-n+m-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}b^{2(-n+m-2\beta)}

\displaystyle-\frac{8(n+m+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}{(n+2\beta)^{2}n^{2}}|b_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-m+2\beta)}}

\displaystyle=\frac{n^{2}|a_{-n}|^{2}}{8(n+m+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m+2\beta)}}

\displaystyle+\frac{n^{2}|b_{-n}|^{2}}{4(-n+m-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}b^{2(-n+m-2\beta)}\left(1-\frac{32\left(m^{2}-(n+2\beta)^{2}\right)^{2}\left(m^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)}{(n+2\beta)^{2}n^{4}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(-n+m-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n^{2}|a_{-n}|^{2}}{8(n+m+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m+2\beta)}}+\frac{n^{2}|b_{-n}|^{2}}{8(-n+m-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}b^{2(-n+m-2\beta)}

provided that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2(-n+m-2\beta)}\log\left(\frac{64\left(m^{2}-(n+2\beta)^{2}\right)\left(m^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)}{(n+2\beta)^{2}n^{4}}\right),

which holds in particular if

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2(1-\beta)}\log\left(\frac{m^{2}}{1+\beta}\right).

(2.124)

Finally, we get for all
2\leq n\leq m-2

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{-n+m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(-n+m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(-n+m+2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{n+m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}a_{-n}\right)}{m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\frac{|b_{n}|^{2}}{n+m-2\beta}b^{2(n+m-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m-2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|b_{-n}|^{2}}{-n+m-2\beta}b^{2(-n+m-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(-n+m-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}b_{-n}\right)}{m-2\beta}b^{2(m-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)}{n-2\beta}b^{2(n-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-2\beta)}\right)+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-n}\overline{b_{-n}}\right)}{n+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n^{2}|a_{n}|^{2}}{8(-n+m+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(-n+m+2\beta)}}+\frac{n^{2}|a_{-n}|^{2}}{8(n+m+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m+2\beta)}}

\displaystyle+\frac{n^{2}|b_{n}|^{2}}{8(n+m-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}b^{2(n+m-2\beta)}+\frac{n^{2}|b_{-n}|^{2}}{8(-n+m-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}b^{2(-n+m-2\beta)}.

(2.125)

The final term to estimate for
2\leq n\leq m-2
is given by

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{-n}}\right)}{\beta}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)+\frac{\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)}{\beta}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{n^{2}|a_{n}|^{2}}{16(-n+m+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(-n+m+2\beta)}}-\frac{8(-n+m+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}{\beta^{2}n^{2}}|b_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-m+2\beta)}}

\displaystyle-\frac{n^{2}|a_{-n}|^{2}}{16(n+m+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m+2\beta)}}-\frac{8(n+m+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}{\beta^{2}n^{2}}|b_{n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(-n-m+2\beta)}}.

We deduce that

\displaystyle\frac{n^{2}|a_{n}|^{2}}{8(-n+m+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(-n+m+2\beta)}}+\frac{n^{2}|a_{-n}|^{2}}{8(n+m+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m+2\beta)}}

\displaystyle+\frac{n^{2}|b_{n}|^{2}}{8(n+m-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}b^{2(n+m-2\beta)}+\frac{n^{2}|b_{-n}|^{2}}{8(-n+m-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}b^{2(-n+m-2\beta)}

\displaystyle+\frac{\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{-n}}\right)}{\beta}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)+\frac{\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)}{\beta}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n^{2}|a_{n}|^{2}}{16(-n+m+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(-n+m+2\beta)}}+\frac{n^{2}|a_{-n}|^{2}}{8(n+m+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m+2\beta)}}

\displaystyle+\frac{n^{2}|b_{n}|^{2}}{8(n+m-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}b^{2(n+m-2\beta)}\left(1-\frac{64\left((m+n)^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)\left(m^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)}{\beta^{2}n^{4}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{n^{2}|b_{-n}|^{2}}{8(-n+m-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}b^{2(-n+m-2\beta)}\left(1-\frac{64\left(m^{2}-(n+2\beta)^{2}\right)\left(m^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)}{\beta^{2}n^{4}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(-n+m-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n^{2}|a_{n}|^{2}}{16(-n+m+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(-n+m+2\beta)}}+\frac{n^{2}|a_{-n}|^{2}}{8(n+m+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m+2\beta)}}

\displaystyle+\frac{n^{2}|b_{n}|^{2}}{16(n+m-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}b^{2(n+m-2\beta)}+\frac{n^{2}|b_{-n}|^{2}}{16(-n+m-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}b^{2(-n+m-2\beta)},

provided that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2(n+m-2\beta)}\log\left(\frac{128\left((m+n)^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)\left(m^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)}{\beta^{2}n^{4}}\right)

and

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2(-n+m-2\beta)}\log\left(\frac{128\left(m^{2}-(n+2\beta)^{2}\right)\left(m^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)}{\beta^{2}n^{4}}\right),

which holds if

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\max\left\{\frac{1}{2(m+2(1-\beta))}\log\left(\frac{32m^{4}}{\beta^{2}}\right),\frac{1}{4(1-\beta)}\log\left(\frac{32m^{4}}{\beta^{2}}\right)\right\}.

(2.126)

Therefore, we need only estimate the terms involving
a_{n}
for
|n|\in\left\{1,m-1,m\right\}
to conclude the proof of the theorem! We first estimate. First, the two initial estimates are verified for
n=1
, which yields

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{1}|^{2}}{m-1+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-1+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-1+2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|a_{-1}|^{2}}{m+1+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+1+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+1+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{1}a_{-1}\right)}{m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\frac{|b_{1}|^{2}}{m+1-2\beta}b^{2(m+1-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+1-2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|b_{-1}|^{2}}{m-1-2\beta}b^{2(m-1-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-1-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{1}b_{-1}\right)}{m-2\beta}b^{2(m-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|a_{1}|^{2}}{4(m-1+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-1+2\beta)}}+\frac{|a_{-1}|^{2}}{4(m+1+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+1+2\beta)}}

\displaystyle+\frac{|b_{1}|^{2}}{4(m+1-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}b^{2(m+1-2\beta)}+\frac{|b_{-1}|^{2}}{4(m-1-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}b^{2(m-1-2\beta)}.

Notice that we need to assume that
\beta<\frac{m-1}{2}
, so we make this assumption for now. We therefore need to assume that
m>2
. The case
m=2
will require a new argument, but as we saw earlier with the polynomial issue, we would have had to treat this case separately anyway. Now, we estimate

\displaystyle\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{1}\overline{b_{1}}\right)}{2\beta-1}\frac{1}{a^{2(2\beta-1)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2\beta-1)}\right)\geq-\frac{|a_{1}|^{2}}{8(m-1+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-1+2\beta)}}

\displaystyle-\frac{8(m-1+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}{(2\beta-1)^{2}}|b_{1}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(-m-1+2\beta)}}

and

\displaystyle\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-1}\overline{b_{-1}}\right)}{2\beta+1}\frac{1}{a^{2(2\beta+1)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2\beta+1)}\right)\geq-\frac{|a_{-1}|^{2}}{8(m+1+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+1+2\beta)}}

\displaystyle-\frac{8(m+1+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}{(2\beta+1)^{2}}|b_{-1}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(-m-1+2\beta)}}.

Therefore, we get

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{1}|^{2}}{4(m-1+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-1+2\beta)}}+\frac{|a_{-1}|^{2}}{4(m+1+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+1+2\beta)}}

\displaystyle+\frac{|b_{1}|^{2}}{4(m+1-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}b^{2(m+1-2\beta)}+\frac{|b_{-1}|^{2}}{4(m-1-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}b^{2(m-1-2\beta)}

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{1}\overline{b_{1}}\right)}{2\beta-1}\frac{1}{a^{2(2\beta-1)}}+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-1}\overline{b_{-1}}\right)}{2\beta+1}\frac{1}{a^{2(2\beta+1)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2\beta+1)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|a_{1}|^{2}}{8(m-1+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-1+2\beta)}}+\frac{|a_{-1}|^{2}}{8(m+1+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+1+2\beta)}}

\displaystyle+\frac{|b_{1}|^{2}}{4(m+1-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}b^{2(m+1-2\beta)}\left(1-\frac{32\left((m+2\beta)^{2}-1\right)\left(m^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)}{(2\beta-1)^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+1-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{|b_{-1}|^{2}}{4(m-1-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}b^{2(m-1-2\beta)}\left(1-\frac{32\left(m^{2}-(2\beta+1)^{2}\right)\left(m^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)}{(2\beta+1)^{2}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-1-2\beta)}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|a_{1}|^{2}}{8(m-1+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-1+2\beta)}}+\frac{|a_{-1}|^{2}}{8(m+1+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+1+2\beta)}}

\displaystyle+\frac{|b_{1}|^{2}}{8(m+1-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}b^{2(m+1-2\beta)}+\frac{|b_{-1}|^{2}}{8(m-1-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}b^{2(m-1-2\beta)},

provided that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2(m+1-2\beta)}\log\left(\frac{64\left((m+2\beta)^{2}-1\right)\left(m^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)}{(2\beta-1)^{2}}\right)

(2.127)

and

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2(m-1-2\beta)}\log\left(\frac{64\left(m^{2}-(2\beta+1)^{2}\right)\left(m^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)}{(2\beta+1)^{2}}\right).

(2.128)

Then, we get as before

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{1}\overline{b_{-1}}\right)}{\beta}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)+\frac{\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-1}\overline{b_{1}}\right)}{\beta}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{|a_{1}|^{2}}{16(m-1+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-1+2\beta)}}-\frac{8(m-1+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}{\beta^{2}}|b_{-1}|^{2}a^{2(m-1-2\beta)}

\displaystyle-\frac{|a_{-1}|^{2}}{16(m+1+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{4(m+1+\beta)}}-\frac{8(m+1+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}{\beta^{2}}|b_{1}|^{2}a^{2(m+1-2\beta)},

and we deduce that

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{1}|^{2}}{8(m-1+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-1+2\beta)}}+\frac{|a_{-1}|^{2}}{8(m+1+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+1+2\beta)}}

\displaystyle+\frac{|b_{1}|^{2}}{8(m+1-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}b^{2(m+1-2\beta)}+\frac{|b_{-1}|^{2}}{8(m-1-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}b^{2(m-1-2\beta)}

\displaystyle+\frac{\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{1}\overline{b_{-1}}\right)}{\beta}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)+\frac{\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-1}\overline{b_{1}}\right)}{\beta}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|a_{1}|^{2}}{16(m-1+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-1+2\beta)}}+\frac{|a_{-1}|^{2}}{16(m+1+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+1+2\beta)}}

\displaystyle+\frac{|b_{1}|^{2}}{8(m+1-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}b^{2(m+1-2\beta)}\left(1-\frac{64\left((m+1)^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)\left(m^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)}{\beta^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+1-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{|b_{-1}|^{2}}{8(m-1-2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}b^{2(m-1-2\beta)}\left(1-\frac{64\left((m-1)^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)\left(m^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)}{\beta^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-1-2\beta)}\right).

Therefore, assuming that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2(m+1-2\beta)}\log\left(128\frac{\left((m+1)^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)\left(m^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)}{\beta^{2}}\right)

(2.129)

and

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2(m-1-2\beta)}\log\left(128\frac{\left((m-1)^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)\left(m^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)}{\beta^{2}}\right),

(2.130)

we deduce that

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{1}|^{2}}{4(m-1+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-1+2\beta)}}+\frac{|a_{-1}|^{2}}{4(m+1+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+1+2\beta)}}

\displaystyle+\frac{|b_{1}|^{2}}{4(m+1-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}b^{2(m+1-2\beta)}+\frac{|b_{-1}|^{2}}{4(m-1-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}b^{2(m-1-2\beta)}
