\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{1}\overline{b_{1}}\right)}{2\beta-1}\frac{1}{a^{2(2\beta-1)}}+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-1}\overline{b_{-1}}\right)}{2\beta+1}\frac{1}{a^{2(2\beta+1)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2\beta+1)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{1}\overline{b_{-1}}\right)}{\beta}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)+\frac{\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-1}\overline{b_{1}}\right)}{\beta}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|a_{1}|^{2}}{16(m-1+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-1+2\beta)}}+\frac{|a_{-1}|^{2}}{16(m+1+2\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+1+2\beta)}}

\displaystyle+\frac{|b_{1}|^{2}}{16(m+1-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}b^{2(m+1-2\beta)}+\frac{|b_{-1}|^{2}}{16(m-1-2\beta)(m-2\beta)^{2}}b^{2(m-1-2\beta)}.

(2.131)

Now, let us estimate
a_{n},b_{n}
for
|n|=m
. Then estimates for
a_{m},a_{-m}
are unchanged and we get

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{m}|^{2}}{2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)+\frac{|a_{-m}|^{2}}{2(m+\beta)}\frac{1}{a^{4(m+\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m+\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{m}a_{-m}\right)}{m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{m^{2}|a_{m}|^{2}}{8\beta(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}+\frac{m^{2}|a_{-m}|^{2}}{8(m+\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{4(m+\beta)}}.

In fact, this estimate is too crude, so we need to refine it. We first have

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{m}|^{2}}{2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)+\frac{|a_{-m}|^{2}}{2(m+\beta)}\frac{1}{a^{4(m+\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m+\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{m}a_{-m}\right)}{m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|a_{m}|^{2}}{2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)-\frac{2(m+\beta)|a_{m}|^{2}}{(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\frac{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m+\beta)}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m+\beta)}}\frac{|a_{m}|^{2}}{2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m+\beta)}\right)-\frac{4\beta(m+\beta)}{(m+2\beta)^{2}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m+\beta)}}\frac{|a_{m}|^{2}}{2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(\frac{m^{2}}{(m+2\beta)^{2}}-2\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right).

Likewise, we have

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{m}|^{2}}{2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)+\frac{|a_{-m}|^{2}}{2(m+\beta)}\frac{1}{a^{4(m+\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m+\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{m}a_{-m}\right)}{m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|a_{-m}|^{2}}{2(m+\beta)}\frac{1}{a^{4(m+\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m+\beta)}\right)-\frac{2\beta|a_{-m}|^{2}}{(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{4(m+\beta)}}\frac{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}}\frac{|a_{-m}|^{2}}{2(m+\beta)}\frac{1}{a^{4(m+\beta)}}\left(\frac{m^{2}}{(m+2\beta)^{2}}-2\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right).

Therefore, for all
0<\varepsilon<1
, we deduce that

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{m}|^{2}}{2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)+\frac{|a_{-m}|^{2}}{2(m+\beta)}\frac{1}{a^{4(m+\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m+\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{m}a_{-m}\right)}{m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\varepsilon\,m^{2}|a_{m}|^{2}}{2\beta(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(1-\frac{2(m+2\beta)^{2}}{m^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)+\frac{(1-\varepsilon)m^{2}|a_{-m}|^{2}}{2(m+\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{4(m+\beta)}}\left(1-\frac{2(m+2\beta)^{2}}{m^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right).

We move to the estimate of

\displaystyle\frac{|b_{m}|^{2}}{2(m-\beta)}b^{4(m-\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m-\beta)}\right)+\frac{|b_{-m}|^{2}}{2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{m}b_{-m}\right)}{m-2\beta}b^{2(m-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|b_{m}|^{2}}{4(m-\beta)^{2}}b^{4(m-\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m-\beta)}\right)+\frac{|b_{-m}|^{2}}{2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{4(m-\beta)}{(m-2\beta)^{2}}|b_{-m}|^{2}b^{4\beta}\frac{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2\beta)}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m-\beta)}}

\displaystyle=\frac{|b_{m}|^{2}}{4(m-\beta)^{2}}b^{4(m-\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m-\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m-\beta)}}\frac{|b_{-m}|^{2}}{2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m-\beta)}\right)-\frac{4(m-\beta)}{(m-2\beta)^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2\beta)}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|b_{m}|^{2}}{4(m-\beta)^{2}}b^{4(m-\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m-\beta)}\right)+\frac{|b_{-m}|^{2}}{2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(1-\left(1+\frac{4(m-\beta)}{(m-2\beta)^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m-\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|b_{m}|^{2}}{4(m-\beta)^{2}}b^{4(m-\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m-\beta)}\right)+\frac{|b_{-m}|^{2}}{2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(1-\left(2+\frac{4(m-\beta)}{(m-2\beta)^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)

since we assumed that
\beta<\dfrac{m}{2}
.
Then, we estimate as previously

\displaystyle\frac{\varepsilon\,m^{2}|a_{m}|^{2}}{2\beta(m+2\beta)^{2}}\left(1-\frac{2(m+2\beta)^{2}}{m^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)+\frac{(1-\varepsilon)m^{2}|a_{-m}|^{2}}{2(m+\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{4(m+\beta)}}\left(1-\frac{2(m+2\beta)^{2}}{m^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{|b_{m}|^{2}}{4(m-\beta)^{2}}b^{4(m-\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m-\beta)}\right)+\frac{|b_{-m}|^{2}}{2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(1-\left(2+\frac{4(m-\beta)}{(m-2\beta)^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{m}\overline{b_{m}}\right)}{m-2\beta}b^{2(m-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2\beta)}\right)+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-m}\overline{b_{-m}}\right)}{m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|b_{m}|^{2}}{8(m-\beta)^{2}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m-\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\varepsilon\,m^{2}|a_{m}|^{2}}{2\beta(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(1-\frac{2(m+2\beta)^{2}}{m^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)-\frac{8(m-\beta)^{2}|a_{m}|^{2}}{(m-2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{b^{4\beta}}\frac{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2\beta)}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m-\beta)}}

\displaystyle+\frac{(1-\varepsilon)m^{2}|a_{-m}|^{2}}{2(m+\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{4(m+\beta)}}\left(1-\frac{2(m+2\beta)^{2}}{m^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)-\frac{2\beta|a_{-m}|^{2}}{(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{4(m+\beta)}}\frac{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(2+\frac{4(m-\beta)}{(m-2\beta)^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}}.

We will get a non-trivial estimate provided that

\displaystyle\frac{(1-\varepsilon)m^{2}}{2(m+\beta)}-2\beta=\frac{(1-\varepsilon)m^{2}-4\beta m-4\beta^{2}}{2(m+\beta)}>0,

which is equivalent to

\displaystyle\frac{2\left(1+\sqrt{2-\varepsilon}\right)}{1-\varepsilon}\beta<m,

which yields an admissible value of
\beta
provided that

\displaystyle\frac{m}{2(1+\sqrt{2})}>\frac{1}{2},

or

\displaystyle m>1+\sqrt{2}.

Since we already had to treat separately the case
m=2
, we assume without loss of generality that
m>1+\sqrt{2}
(recall that we are mostly interested in the case
m\in\mathbb{N}
), and for
1/2<\beta<\dfrac{m}{2(1+\sqrt{2})}
, we let

\displaystyle\varepsilon=\varepsilon_{m,\beta}=\frac{1}{2}-\frac{(1+\sqrt{2})\beta}{m}.

Then, we have

\displaystyle\frac{(1-\varepsilon_{m,\beta})m^{2}|a_{-m}|^{2}}{2(m+\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{4(m+\beta)}}\left(1-\frac{2(m+2\beta)^{2}}{m^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)-\frac{2\beta|a_{-m}|^{2}}{(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{4(m+\beta)}}\frac{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(2+\frac{4(m-\beta)}{(m-2\beta)^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{1-\left(2+\frac{4(m-\beta)}{(m-2\beta)^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}}\frac{|a_{-m}|^{2}}{2(m+\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{4(m+\beta)}}\bigg((1-\varepsilon_{m,\beta})m^{2}-4\beta m-4\beta^{2}

\displaystyle-2(1-\varepsilon_{m,\beta})\left((m+2\beta)^{2}+m^{2}+\frac{2m^{2}(m-\beta)}{(m-2\beta)^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\bigg).

Therefore, we impose the conditions

\displaystyle\left(2+\frac{4(m-\beta)}{(m-2\beta)^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\leq\frac{1}{2}

\displaystyle 2(1-\varepsilon_{m,\beta})\left((m+2\beta)^{2}+m^{2}+\frac{2m^{2}(m-\beta)}{(m-2\beta)^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\leq\frac{(1-\varepsilon_{m,\beta})m^{2}-4\beta m-4\beta^{2}}{2},

which holds in particular if

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{4\beta}\max\left\{\log\left(4+\frac{8(m-\beta)}{(m-2\beta)^{2}}\right),\log\left(\frac{4\left(\left(m^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)^{2}+m^{2}(m-2\beta)^{2}+2m^{2}(m-\beta)\right)}{\left((1-\varepsilon_{m,\beta})m^{2}-4\beta m-4\beta^{2}\right)(m-2\beta)^{2}}\right)\right\},

(2.132)

which yield

\displaystyle\frac{(1-\varepsilon_{m,\beta})m^{2}|a_{-m}|^{2}}{2(m+\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{4(m+\beta)}}\left(1-\frac{2(m+2\beta)^{2}}{m^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)-\frac{2\beta|a_{-m}|^{2}}{(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{4(m+\beta)}}\frac{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(2+\frac{4(m-\beta)}{(m-2\beta)^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{(1-\varepsilon_{m,\beta})m^{2}-4\beta m-4\beta^{2}}{2(m+\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}|a_{-m}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{4(m+\beta)}}.

Next, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\varepsilon_{m,\beta}\,m^{2}|a_{m}|^{2}}{2\beta(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(1-\frac{2(m+2\beta)^{2}}{m^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)-\frac{8(m-\beta)^{2}|a_{m}|^{2}}{(m-2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{b^{4\beta}}\frac{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2\beta)}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m-\beta)}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m-\beta)}}\frac{\varepsilon_{m,\beta}m^{2}|a_{m}|^{2}}{2\beta(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{2(m+2\beta)^{2}}{m^{2}}+\frac{16\beta(m+2\beta)^{2}(m-\beta)^{2}}{\varepsilon_{m,\beta}m^{2}(m-2\beta)^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m-\beta)}\right).

Therefore, imposing that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{4(m-\beta)}\log(4)=\frac{\log(2)}{2(m-\beta)}

(2.133)

and

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{4\beta}\log\left(\frac{2(m+2\beta)^{2}}{m^{2}}+\frac{16\beta(m+2\beta)^{2}(m-\beta)^{2}}{\varepsilon_{m,\beta}m^{2}(m-2\beta)^{2}}\right),

(2.134)

we finally obtain

\displaystyle\frac{\varepsilon\,m^{2}|a_{m}|^{2}}{2\beta(m+2\beta)^{2}}\left(1-\frac{2(m+2\beta)^{2}}{m^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)+\frac{(1-\varepsilon)m^{2}|a_{-m}|^{2}}{2(m+\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{4(m+\beta)}}\left(1-\frac{2(m+2\beta)^{2}}{m^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{|b_{m}|^{2}}{4(m-\beta)^{2}}b^{4(m-\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m-\beta)}\right)+\frac{|b_{-m}|^{2}}{2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(1-\left(2+\frac{4(m-\beta)}{(m-2\beta)^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{m}\overline{b_{m}}\right)}{m-2\beta}b^{2(m-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2\beta)}\right)+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-m}\overline{b_{-m}}\right)}{m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|b_{m}|^{2}}{16(m-\beta)^{2}}b^{4(m-\beta)}+\frac{(1-\varepsilon_{m,\beta})m^{2}-4\beta m-4\beta^{2}}{2(m+\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}|a_{-m}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{4(m+\beta)}}+\frac{\varepsilon_{m,\beta}m^{2}|a_{m}|^{2}}{4\beta(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}.

Next, we need to estimate
|b_{-m}|^{2}
. We have

\displaystyle\frac{(1-\varepsilon)m^{2}|a_{-m}|^{2}}{2(m+\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{4(m+\beta)}}\left(1-\frac{2(m+2\beta)^{2}}{m^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)+\frac{|b_{-m}|^{2}}{2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(1-\left(2+\frac{4(m-\beta)}{(m-2\beta)^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-m}\overline{b_{-m}}\right)}{m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|b_{-m}|^{2}}{2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(1-\left(2+\frac{4(m-\beta)}{(m-2\beta)^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)-\frac{2(m+\beta)|b_{-m}|^{2}}{(1-\varepsilon)m^{2}}\frac{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)^{2}}{1-\frac{2(m+2\beta)^{2}}{m^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{1-\frac{2(m+2\beta)^{2}}{m^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}}\frac{|b_{-m}|^{2}}{2\beta}\left((1-\varepsilon)m^{2}-4\beta m-4\beta^{2}-\left(\frac{2(m+2\beta)^{2}}{m^{2}}+2+\frac{4(m-\beta)}{(m-2\beta)^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right),

which leads us to impose the conditions

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\max\left\{\frac{1}{2\beta}\log\left(\frac{2(m+2\beta)}{m}\right),\frac{1}{4\beta}\log\left(\frac{4\left(m^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)^{2}+4m^{2}(m-2\beta)^{2}+8m^{2}(m-\beta)}{\left((1-\varepsilon_{m,\beta})m^{2}-4\beta m-4\beta^{2}\right)m^{2}(m-2\beta)^{2}}\right)\right\},

(2.135)

and gives us with
\varepsilon=\varepsilon_{m,\beta}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\frac{2(m+2\beta)^{2}}{m^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}}\frac{|b_{-m}|^{2}}{2\beta}\left((1-\varepsilon)m^{2}-4\beta m-4\beta^{2}-\left(\frac{2(m+2\beta)^{2}}{m^{2}}+2+\frac{4(m-\beta)}{(m-2\beta)^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{(1-\varepsilon_{m,\beta})m^{2}-4\beta m-4\beta^{2}}{2\beta}\frac{|b_{-m}|^{2}}{2\beta},

and finally,

\displaystyle\frac{\varepsilon\,m^{2}|a_{m}|^{2}}{2\beta(m+2\beta)^{2}}\left(1-\frac{2(m+2\beta)^{2}}{m^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)+\frac{(1-\varepsilon)m^{2}|a_{-m}|^{2}}{2(m+\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{4(m+\beta)}}\left(1-\frac{2(m+2\beta)^{2}}{m^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{|b_{m}|^{2}}{4(m-\beta)^{2}}b^{4(m-\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(m-\beta)}\right)+\frac{|b_{-m}|^{2}}{2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(1-\left(2+\frac{4(m-\beta)}{(m-2\beta)^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{m}\overline{b_{m}}\right)}{m-2\beta}b^{2(m-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2\beta)}\right)+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-m}\overline{b_{-m}}\right)}{m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\varepsilon_{m,\beta}m^{2}|a_{m}|^{2}}{8\beta(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}+\frac{|b_{m}|^{2}}{16(m-\beta)^{2}}b^{4(m-\beta)}+\frac{(1-\varepsilon_{m,\beta})m^{2}-4\beta m-4\beta^{2}}{2(m+\beta)(m+2\beta)^{2}}|a_{-m}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{4(m+\beta)}}

\displaystyle+\frac{(1-\varepsilon_{m,\beta})m^{2}-4\beta m-4\beta^{2}}{2\beta}\frac{|b_{-m}|^{2}}{4\beta}.

The last terms involving
a_{m},a_{-m},b_{m},b_{-m}
are

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{m}\overline{b_{-m}}\right)}{\beta}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)+\frac{\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-m}\overline{b_{m}}\right)}{\beta}\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)

Step 12.
The estimate of the
L^{2}
norm of
u_{0}
. We have

\displaystyle u(z)

\displaystyle=|z|^{1-m}\left(\alpha_{1}\log|z|+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}a_{n}z^{n}\right)\right)+|z|^{m+1}\left(\alpha_{2}\log|z|+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}b_{n}z^{n}\right)\right)

(2.136)

\displaystyle=\alpha_{1}\,r^{1-m}\log(r)+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}a_{n}\,r^{1-m+n}e^{in\theta}\right)+\alpha_{2}r^{m+1}\log(r)+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}b_{n}\,r^{m+1+n}e^{in\theta}\right).

Recall that for all
0<\gamma<1

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\frac{1}{|x|^{2}}\left|\nabla u_{0}+(m-1)\frac{x}{|x|^{2}}u_{0}\right|^{2}|x|^{2\gamma}dx\leq 4\pi\bigg(7\sum_{n=m}^{\infty}\frac{n^{2}}{n-m+\gamma}|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n-m+\gamma)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-m+\gamma)}\right)

\displaystyle+7\sum_{n=1-m,n\neq 0}^{\infty}\frac{n^{2}}{n+m-\gamma}|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m-\gamma)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m-\gamma)}\right)

\displaystyle+2\sum_{n=-m,n\neq 0}^{\infty}\frac{5(2m+n)^{2}+2n^{2}}{n+m+\gamma}|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n+m+\gamma)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m+\gamma)}\right)

\displaystyle+2\sum_{n=m}^{\infty}\frac{5(n-2m)^{2}+2n^{2}}{n-m-\gamma}|b_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-m-\gamma)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-m-\gamma)}\right)\bigg).

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\frac{1}{|x|^{2}}\left|\nabla u_{0}+(m-1)\frac{x}{|x|^{2}}u_{0}\right|^{2}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}dx\leq 28\pi\sum_{n=m}^{\infty}\frac{n^{2}|a_{n}|^{2}}{n-m+\gamma}b^{2(n-m)}

\displaystyle+28\pi\sum_{n=1-m,n\neq 0}\frac{n^{2}|a_{-n}|^{2}}{n+m-\gamma}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m)}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}+4\pi\sum_{n=-m,n\neq 0}^{\infty}\frac{5(2m+n)^{2}+2n^{2}}{n+m+\gamma}|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n+m)}

\displaystyle+4\pi\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}\frac{5(n-2m)^{2}+2n^{2}}{n-m-\gamma}|b_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-m)}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}.

(2.137)

We have

\displaystyle u_{0}

\displaystyle=2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}a_{n}r^{1-m+n}e^{in\theta}\right)+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}b_{n}r^{m+1+n}e^{in\theta}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\left(a_{n}r^{1-m+n}+\overline{a_{-n}}r^{1-m-n}+b_{n}r^{m+1+n}+\overline{b_{-n}}r^{m+1-n}\right)e^{in\theta}.

Parseval identity implies that for all
0<\beta<\dfrac{1}{2}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{\Omega}\frac{u_{0}^{2}}{|x|^{4}}|x|^{4\beta}dx=\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\int_{a}^{b}\left|a_{n}r^{1-m+n}+\overline{a_{-n}}r^{1-m-n}+b_{n}r^{m+1+n}+\overline{b_{-n}}r^{m+1-n}\right|^{2}r^{4\beta-3}dr

\displaystyle=2\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\int_{a}^{b}|a_{n}|^{2}r^{-2m+2n+4\beta-1}dr+2\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\int_{a}^{b}|b_{n}|^{2}r^{2m+2n+4\beta-1}dr

\displaystyle+2\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\int_{a}^{b}\mathrm{Re}\,(a_{n}a_{-n})r^{-2m+4\beta-1}dr+2\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\int_{a}^{b}\mathrm{Re}\,(b_{n}b_{-n})r^{2m-1+4\beta}dr

\displaystyle+4\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)r^{2n+4\beta-1}dr+4\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}b_{-n}\right)r^{4\beta-1}dr

\displaystyle=\sum_{n=m}^{\infty}\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{n-m+2\beta}b^{2(n-m+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-m+2\beta)}\right)+\sum_{n=1-m}^{\infty}\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{n+m-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+m-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=-m,n\neq 0}^{\infty}\frac{|b_{n}|^{2}}{m+n+2\beta}b^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\right)+\sum_{n=1-m}^{\infty}\frac{|b_{-n}|^{2}}{n-m-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-m-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-m-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{m-2\beta}\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}a_{-n}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2(m-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2\beta)}\right)+\frac{2}{m+2\beta}\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}b_{-n}\right)b^{2(m+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+2\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\frac{\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)}{n+2\beta}b^{2(n+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+2\beta)}\right)+2\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\frac{\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-n}\overline{b_{-n}}\right)}{n-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\beta}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}b_{-n}\right)b^{4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right).

On the other hand, we have by (
2.3.2
) and (
2.3.2
)

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{m-1}n^{2}(m+n)|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(m+n)}+\sum_{n=1}^{m-1}n^{2}(m-n)|b_{-n}|^{2}b^{2(m-n)}+8m^{3}|b_{m}|^{2}b^{4m}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4m}\right)

\displaystyle+m^{2}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}(m+n)|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(m+n)}+m^{2}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}(n-m)|b_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-m)}}\leq\frac{1}{8\pi}\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}u\right)^{2}dx.

(2.138)

and

\displaystyle 2\,m^{2}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}(n+m)|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n+m)}+2\,m^{2}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}(n-m)|b_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-m)}}

\displaystyle+\frac{m^{2}}{800}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}n|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n-m)}+\frac{m^{4}(m-1)^{2}}{128(3m-1)^{7}}\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}n|a_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+m)}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}u\right)^{2}dx+\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{\Omega}\left|\mathfrak{L}_{m}u\right|^{2}|dz|^{2}.

(2.139)

Therefore, we need only estimate, using the
L^{2}
norm of
u
, the terms involving
b_{-m}
and
a_{n}
for
1\leq|n|\leq m
. Therefore, we first consider for
1\leq n\leq m-1
the quantity

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{m-n-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{m+n-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{|b_{n}|^{2}}{m+n+2\beta}b^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|b_{-n}|^{2}}{m-n+2\beta}b^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}a_{-n}\right)}{m-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2\beta)}\right)+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,(b_{n}b_{-n})}{m+2\beta}b^{2(m+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)}{n+2\beta}b^{2(n+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+2\beta)}\right)+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-n}\overline{b_{-n}}\right)}{n-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\beta}\left(\mathrm{Re}\,(a_{n}b_{-n})+\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-n}b_{n}\right)\right)b^{4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right).

We start by estimating

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{m-n-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{m+n-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}a_{-n}\right)}{m-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{m-n-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\right)-\frac{(m+n-2\beta)}{(m-2\beta)^{2}}|a_{n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n-2\beta)}}\frac{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2\beta)}\right)^{2}}{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n-2\beta)}\right)}

\displaystyle=\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n-2\beta)}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n-2\beta)}}\left(\frac{1}{m-n-2\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\right)\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n-2\beta)}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\frac{(m+n-2\beta)}{(m-2\beta)^{2}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2\beta)}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n-2\beta)}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n-2\beta)}}\left(\frac{n^{2}}{(m-2\beta)^{2}(m-n-2\beta)}-\frac{2}{m-n-2\beta}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\right).

(2.140)

Likewise, we have

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{m-n-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{m+n-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}a_{-n}\right)}{m-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n-2\beta)}}\left(\frac{n^{2}}{(m-2\beta)^{2}(m+n-2\beta)}-\frac{2}{m+n-2\beta}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\right).

(2.141)

Therefore, we get

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{m-n-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{m+n-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}a_{-n}\right)}{m-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n-2\beta)}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n-2\beta)}}\left(\frac{n^{2}}{2(m-2\beta)^{2}(m-n-2\beta)}-\frac{1}{m-n-2\beta}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n-2\beta)}}\left(\frac{n^{2}}{2(m-2\beta)^{2}(m+n-2\beta)}-\frac{1}{m+n-2\beta}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\right).

(2.142)

Therefore, we assume that the conformal class satisfies the following condition:

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{m-n-2\beta}\log\left(\frac{2(m-2\beta)}{n}\right),

(2.143)

which yields

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{m-n-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{m+n-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}a_{-n}\right)}{m-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n^{2}|a_{n}|^{2}}{4(m-2\beta)^{2}(m-n-2\beta)}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n-2\beta)}}+\frac{n^{2}|a_{-n}|^{2}}{4(m-2\beta)^{2}(m+n-2\beta)}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n-2\beta)}}.

(2.144)

Notice that the same argument also shows that

\displaystyle\frac{|b_{n}|^{2}}{m+n+2\beta}b^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|b_{-n}|^{2}}{m-n+2\beta}b^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,(b_{n}b_{-n})}{m+2\beta}b^{2(m+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n^{2}|b_{n}|^{2}}{4(m-2\beta)^{2}(m+n+2\beta)}b^{2(m+n+2\beta)}+\frac{n^{2}|b_{-n}|^{2}}{4(m-2\beta)^{2}(m-n+2\beta)}b^{2(m-n+2\beta)}.

(2.145)

Then, we estimate

\displaystyle\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)}{n+2\beta}b^{2(n+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+2\beta)}\right)\geq-\frac{16(m-2\beta)^{2}(m+n+2\beta)}{n^{2}(n+2\beta)^{2}}|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(-m-n+2\beta)}

\displaystyle-\frac{n^{2}|b_{n}|^{2}}{16(m-2\beta)^{2}(m+n+2\beta)}b^{2(m+n+2\beta)}

(2.146)

and

\displaystyle\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-n}\overline{b_{-n}}\right)}{n-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-2\beta)}\right)\geq-\frac{n^{2}|a_{-n}|^{2}}{8(m-2\beta)^{2}(m+n-2\beta)}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n-2\beta)}}

\displaystyle-\frac{8(m-2\beta)^{2}(m+n-2\beta)}{n^{2}(n-2\beta)^{2}}|b_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(-m+n-2\beta)}}.

(2.147)

Finally, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}b_{-n}\right)b^{4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)\geq-\frac{16(m-2\beta)^{2}(m-n+2\beta)|a_{n}|^{2}}{\beta^{2}n^{2}}b^{2(n-m+2\beta)}

\displaystyle-\frac{n^{2}|b_{-n}|^{2}}{16(m-2\beta)^{2}(m-n+2\beta)}b^{2(m-n+2\beta)}

(2.148)

and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-n}b_{n}\right)b^{4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)\geq-\frac{16(m-2\beta)^{2}(m+n+2\beta)|a_{-n}|^{2}}{\beta^{2}n^{2}}b^{2(-m-n+2\beta)}

\displaystyle-\frac{n^{2}|b_{n}|^{2}}{16(m-2\beta)^{2}(m+n+2\beta)}b^{2(m+n+2\beta)}.

(2.149)

Finally, we get

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{m-n-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{m+n-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{|b_{n}|^{2}}{m+n+2\beta}b^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|b_{-n}|^{2}}{m-n+2\beta}b^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}a_{-n}\right)}{m-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2\beta)}\right)+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,(b_{n}b_{-n})}{m+2\beta}b^{2(m+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)}{n+2\beta}b^{2(n+2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+2\beta)}\right)+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-n}\overline{b_{-n}}\right)}{n-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\beta}\left(\mathrm{Re}\,(a_{n}b_{-n})+\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-n}b_{n}\right)\right)b^{4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n^{2}|a_{n}|^{2}}{4(m-2\beta)^{2}(m-n-2\beta)}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n-2\beta)}}\left(1-\frac{32(m-2\beta)^{4}\left(m^{2}-(n+2\beta)^{2}\right)}{\beta^{2}n^{4}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{n^{2}|a_{-n}|^{2}}{8(m-2\beta)^{2}(m+n-2\beta)}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n-2\beta)}}\left(1-\frac{64(m-2\beta)^{4}\left((m+n)^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)}{\beta^{2}n^{4}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{n^{2}|b_{n}|^{2}}{8(m-2\beta)^{2}(m+n+2\beta)}b^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\left(1-\frac{64(m-2\beta)^{4}\left((m+n)^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)}{n^{4}(n-2\beta)^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{n^{2}|b_{-n}|^{2}}{8(m-2\beta)^{2}(m-n+2\beta)}b^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\left(1-\frac{64(m-2\beta)^{2}\left(m^{2}-(n-2\beta)^{2}\right)}{n^{4}(n-2\beta)^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\right).

(2.150)

Plus utile probablement

Step 7.
Estimates of the
L^{2}
norm of
u
.

Besides the logarithm components that will require a special (also difficult and lengthy) argument, we will estimate the finitely many additional terms by the weighted
L^{2}
norm of
u
. Recall that
u
is given by

\displaystyle u(r,\theta)

\displaystyle=\alpha_{1}\,r^{1-m}\log(r)+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}a_{n}\,r^{1-m+n}e^{in\theta}\right)+\alpha_{2}\,r^{m+1}\log(r)+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}b_{n}\,r^{m+1+n}e^{in\theta}\right)

\displaystyle=\alpha_{1}\,r^{1-m}\log(r)+\alpha_{2}\,r^{m+1}\log(r)+2\,a_{0}\,r^{1-m}+2\,b_{0}\,r^{m+1}

\displaystyle+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\left(a_{n}\,r^{1-m+n}+\overline{a_{-n}}r^{1-m-n}+b_{n}\,r^{m+1+n}+\overline{b_{-n}}r^{m+1-n}\right)e^{in\theta}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Rad}(u^{2})=\left(\alpha_{1}\,r^{1-m}\log(r)+\alpha_{2}\,r^{m+1}\log(r)+2\,a_{0}\,r^{1-m}+2\,b_{0}\,r^{m+1}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\left|a_{n}\,r^{1-m+n}+\overline{a_{-n}}r^{1-m-n}+b_{n}\,r^{m+1+n}+\overline{b_{-n}}r^{m+1-n}\right|^{2}

\displaystyle=\alpha_{1}^{2}r^{2-2m}\log^{2}(r)+\alpha_{2}^{2}r^{2m+2}\log^{2}(r)+4a_{0}^{2}r^{2-2m}+4b_{0}^{2}r^{2m+2}+2\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}\log^{2}(r)+4\,\alpha_{1}a_{0}r^{2-2m}\log(r)

\displaystyle+4\left(\alpha_{1}b_{0}+\alpha_{2}a_{0}\right)\log(r)+4\,\alpha_{2}b_{0}r^{2m+2}\log(r)+8\,a_{0}b_{0}

\displaystyle+2\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}|a_{n}|^{2}r^{2-2m+2n}+2\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}|b_{n}|^{2}r^{2m+2+2n}+2\,\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}a_{-n}\right)r^{2-2m}+2\,\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}b_{-n}\right)r^{2m+2}

\displaystyle+4\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)r^{2n+2}+4\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}b_{-n}\right)r^{2}.

(2.151)

Then, we have for
\alpha\neq-1

\displaystyle\int r^{\alpha}\log(r)dr

\displaystyle=\frac{r^{\alpha+1}}{\alpha+1}\log(r)-\frac{1}{(\alpha+1)^{2}}r^{\alpha+1}

\displaystyle\int r^{\alpha}\log^{2}(r)dr

\displaystyle=\frac{r^{\alpha+1}}{\alpha+1}\log^{2}(r)-\frac{2}{(\alpha+1)^{2}}r^{\alpha+1}\log(r)+\frac{2}{(\alpha+1)^{3}}r^{\alpha+1},

and for all
\beta>0

\displaystyle\int\frac{\log^{\beta}(r)}{r}dr=\frac{1}{\beta+1}\log^{\beta+1}(r).

This implies that for all
1/2<\beta<\mathrm{min}\left\{1,\frac{m}{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\frac{u^{2}}{|x|^{4}}|x|^{4\beta}dx=2\pi\int_{\Omega}\bigg(\alpha_{1}^{2}r^{-2m-1}\log^{2}(r)+\alpha_{2}^{2}r^{2m-1}\log^{2}(r)+4a_{0}^{2}r^{-2m-1}+4b_{0}^{2}r^{2m-1}+2\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}r^{-3}\log^{2}(r)

\displaystyle+4\,\alpha_{1}a_{0}r^{-2m-1}\log(r)+4\left(\alpha_{1}b_{0}+\alpha_{2}a_{0}\right)r^{-3}\log(r)+4\,\alpha_{2}b_{0}r^{2m-1}\log(r)+8\,a_{0}b_{0}\,r^{-3}

\displaystyle+2\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}|a_{n}|^{2}r^{-2m+2n-1}+2\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}|b_{n}|^{2}r^{2m+2n-1}+2\,\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}a_{-n}\right)r^{-2m-1}+2\,\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}b_{-n}\right)r^{2m-1}

\displaystyle+4\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)r^{2n-1}+4\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}b_{-n}\right)r^{-1}\bigg)r^{4\beta}dr

\displaystyle=2\pi\left(\frac{\alpha_{1}^{2}}{2m-4\beta}\left(\frac{1}{a^{2m-4\beta}}\log^{2}(a)+\frac{1}{m-2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2m-4\beta}}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)+\frac{1}{2(m-2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2m-4\beta}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\left(\frac{1}{b^{2m-4\beta}}\log^{2}(b)+\frac{1}{m-2\beta}\frac{1}{b^{2m}}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)+\frac{1}{2(m-2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{b^{2m-4\beta}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\alpha_{2}^{2}}{2m+4\beta}\left(b^{2m+4\beta}\log^{2}(b)+\frac{1}{m+2\beta}b^{2m+4\beta}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)+\frac{1}{2(m+2\beta)^{2}}b^{2m+4\beta}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left(a^{2m+4\beta}\log^{2}(a)+\frac{1}{m+2\beta}a^{2m+4\beta}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)+\frac{1}{2(m+2\beta)^{2}}a^{2m+4\beta}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{m-2\beta}a_{0}^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2m-4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m-4\beta}\right)+\frac{2}{m+2\beta}b_{0}^{2}b^{2m+4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m+4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}}{2\beta-1}\left(b^{4\beta-2}\log^{2}(b)+\frac{1}{2\beta-1}b^{4\beta-2}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)+\frac{1}{2(2\beta-1)^{2}}b^{4\beta-2}\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\left(a^{4\beta-2}\log^{2}(a)+\frac{1}{2\beta-1}a^{4\beta-2}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)+\frac{1}{2(2\beta-1)^{2}}a^{4\beta-2}\right)\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{2\alpha_{1}a_{0}}{m-2\beta}\left(\frac{1}{a^{2m-4\beta}}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)+\frac{1}{2m-4\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2m-4\beta}}-\left(\frac{1}{b^{2m-4\beta}}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)+\frac{1}{2m-4\beta}\frac{1}{b^{2m-4\beta}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{2(\alpha_{1}b_{0}+\alpha_{2}a_{0})}{2\beta-1}\left(b^{2\beta-1}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)+\frac{1}{4\beta-2}b^{2\beta-1}-\left(a^{2\beta-1}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)+\frac{1}{4\beta-2}a^{2\beta-1}\right)\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{2\alpha_{2}b_{0}}{m+2\beta}\left(b^{2m+4\beta}\log\left(\frac{1}{b}\right)+\frac{1}{2m+4\beta}b^{2m+4\beta}-\left(a^{2m+4\beta}\log\left(\frac{1}{a}\right)+\frac{1}{2m+4\beta}a^{2m+4\beta}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{4a_{0}b_{0}}{2\beta-1}b^{4\beta-2}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta-2}\right)+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{|-m+n+2\beta|}\left|b^{-2m+2n+4\beta}-a^{-2m+2n+4\beta}\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\frac{|b_{n}|^{2}}{|m+n+2\beta|}\left|b^{2m+2n+4\beta}-a^{2m+2n+4\beta}\right|-\frac{1}{m-2\beta}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}a_{-n}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2m-4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m-4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{m+2\beta}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}b_{-n}\right)b^{2m+4\beta}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m+4\beta}\right)+2\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\frac{\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)}{n+2\beta}\left(b^{2n+4\beta}-a^{2n+4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\left.+4\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}b_{-n}\right)\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\right).

(2.152)

Provided that
m>m_{0}=2.03\cdots
, we also get the missing estimate on
a_{m}
,
a_{-m}
, and
b_{m}
(that does not follow from the estimation of the
L^{2}
norm of
u
). Now, we also need to estimate for
0<\beta<\dfrac{1}{2}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{\Omega}\frac{u_{0}^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\frac{dx}{|x|^{4\beta}}=2\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\int_{a}^{b}|a_{n}|^{2}r^{-2m+2n-4\beta-1}dr+2\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}|b_{n}|^{2}r^{2m+2n-4\beta-1}dr

\displaystyle+2\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\int_{a}^{b}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}a_{-n}\right)r^{-2m-4\beta-1}dr+2\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\int_{a}^{b}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}b_{-n}\right)r^{2m-1-4\beta}dr

\displaystyle+4\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)r^{2n-4\beta-1}dr+4\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}b_{-n}\right)r^{-4\beta-1}dr

\displaystyle=\sum_{n=m+1}^{\infty}\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{n-m-2\beta}b^{2(n-m-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-m-2\beta)}\right)+\sum_{n=-m,n\neq 0}^{\infty}\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{m+n+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=1-m,n\neq 0}^{\infty}\frac{|b_{n}|^{2}}{m+n-2\beta}b^{2(m+n-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n-2\beta)}\right)+\sum_{n=m}^{\infty}\frac{|b_{-n}|^{2}}{n-m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-m+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+2\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\frac{\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}a_{-n}\right)}{m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)+2\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\frac{\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}b_{-n}\right)}{m-2\beta}b^{2(m-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+2\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\frac{\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)}{n-2\beta}b^{2(n-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-2\beta)}\right)+2\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\frac{\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-n}\overline{b_{-n}}\right)}{n+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\beta}\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\left(\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}b_{-n}\right)+\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-n}b_{n}\right)\right)\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right).

Consider for
1\leq n\leq m-1
the quantity

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{m-n+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{m+n+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n+2\beta)}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{|b_{-n}|^{2}}{m-n-2\beta}b^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|b_{n}|^{2}}{m+n-2\beta}b^{2(m+n-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}a_{-n}\right)}{m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}b_{-n}\right)}{m-2\beta}b^{2(m-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)}{n-2\beta}b^{2(n-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-2\beta)}\right)+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-n}\overline{b_{-n}}\right)}{n+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\beta}\left(\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}b_{-n}\right)+\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-n}b_{n}\right)\right)\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right).

We first estimate

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{m-n+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{m+n+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n+2\beta)}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}a_{-n}\right)}{m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{m-n+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\right)-\frac{(m+n+2\beta)|a_{n}|^{2}}{(m+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n+2\beta)}}\frac{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n+2\beta)}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n+2\beta)}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n+2\beta)}}\left(\frac{n^{2}}{(m+2\beta)^{2}(m-n+2\beta)}-\frac{2}{m-n+2\beta}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\right).

Therefore, we impose the condition

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{m-n+2\beta}\log\left(\frac{2(m+2\beta)}{n}\right),

to get

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{m-n+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{m+n+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n+2\beta)}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}a_{-n}\right)}{m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)\geq\frac{n^{2}|a_{n}|^{2}}{(m+2\beta)^{2}(m-n+2\beta)}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n+2\beta)}}.

(2.153)

Likewise, if

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{m+n+2\beta}\log\left(\frac{2(m+2\beta)}{n}\right),

we get

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{m-n+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{m+n+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n+2\beta)}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}a_{-n}\right)}{m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)\geq\frac{n^{2}|a_{-n}|^{2}}{2(m+2\beta)^{2}(m+n+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n+2\beta)}}.

(2.154)

Finally, assuming that both conditions on the conformal class hold, we get

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{m-n+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{m+n+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n+2\beta)}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}a_{-n}\right)}{m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n^{2}|a_{n}|^{2}}{4(m+2\beta)^{2}(m-n+2\beta)}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n+2\beta)}}+\frac{n^{2}|a_{-n}|^{2}}{4(m+2\beta)^{2}(m+n+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n+2\beta)}}.

Likewise, we have

\displaystyle\frac{|b_{-n}|^{2}}{m-n-2\beta}b^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|b_{n}|^{2}}{m+n-2\beta}b^{2(m+n-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}b_{-n}\right)}{m-2\beta}b^{2(m-2\beta)}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n^{2}|b_{n}|^{2}}{4(m-2\beta)^{2}(m+n-2\beta)}b^{2(m+n-2\beta)}+\frac{n^{2}|b_{-n}|^{2}}{4(m-2\beta)^{2}(m-n-2\beta)}b^{2(m-n-2\beta)}.

(2.155)

provided that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{m-n-2\beta}\log\left(\frac{2(m-2\beta)}{n}\right).

Now, we have

\displaystyle\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)}{n-2\beta}b^{2(n-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-2\beta)}\right)\geq-\frac{16(m-2\beta)^{2}(m+n-2\beta)|a_{n}|^{2}}{n^{2}(n-2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{b^{2(m+n-2\beta)}}

\displaystyle-\frac{n^{2}|b_{n}|^{2}}{16(m-2\beta)^{2}(m-n-2\beta)}b^{2(m-n-2\beta)}.

Then, we estimate

\displaystyle\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-n}\overline{b_{-n}}\right)}{n+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+2\beta)}\right)\geq-\frac{n^{2}|a_{-n}|^{2}}{8(m+2\beta)^{2}(m+n+2\beta)}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n+2\beta)}}

\displaystyle-\frac{8(m+2\beta)^{2}(m+n+2\beta)|b_{-n}|^{2}}{n^{2}(n+2\beta)^{2}}a^{2(m-n-2\beta)}.

Finally, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}b_{-n}\right)\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)\geq-\frac{n^{2}|a_{n}|^{2}}{8(m+2\beta)^{2}(m-n+2\beta)}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n+2\beta)}}

\displaystyle-\frac{8(m+2\beta)^{2}(m-n+2\beta)|b_{-n}|^{2}}{\beta^{2}n^{2}}a^{2(m-n-2\beta)}

and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\beta}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-n}b_{n}\right)\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)\geq-\frac{16(m-2\beta)^{2}(m-n-2\beta)|a_{-n}|^{2}}{\beta^{2}n^{2}}\frac{1}{b^{2(m+n+2\beta)}}

\displaystyle-\frac{n^{2}|b_{n}|^{2}}{16(m-2\beta)^{2}(m-n-2\beta)}b^{2(m-n-2\beta)}.

Therefore, we get

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{n}|^{2}}{m-n+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|a_{-n}|^{2}}{m+n+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n+2\beta)}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{|b_{-n}|^{2}}{m-n-2\beta}b^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\right)+\frac{|b_{n}|^{2}}{m+n-2\beta}b^{2(m+n-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}a_{-n}\right)}{m+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+2\beta)}\right)+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}b_{-n}\right)}{m-2\beta}b^{2(m-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)}{n-2\beta}b^{2(n-2\beta)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-2\beta)}\right)+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-n}\overline{b_{-n}}\right)}{n+2\beta}\frac{1}{a^{2(n+2\beta)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\beta}\left(\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}b_{-n}\right)+\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-n}b_{n}\right)\right)\frac{1}{a^{4\beta}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n^{2}|a_{n}|^{2}}{4(m+2\beta)^{2}(m-n+2\beta)}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n+2\beta)}}+\frac{n^{2}|a_{-n}|^{2}}{4(m+2\beta)^{2}(m+n+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n+2\beta)}}

\displaystyle+\frac{n^{2}|b_{n}|^{2}}{4(m-2\beta)^{2}(m+n-2\beta)}b^{2(m+n-2\beta)}+\frac{n^{2}|b_{-n}|^{2}}{4(m-2\beta)^{2}(m-n-2\beta)}b^{2(m-n-2\beta)}

\displaystyle-\frac{16(m-2\beta)^{2}(m+n-2\beta)|a_{n}|^{2}}{n^{2}(n-2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{b^{2(m+n-2\beta)}}-\frac{n^{2}|b_{n}|^{2}}{16(m-2\beta)^{2}(m-n-2\beta)}b^{2(m-n-2\beta)}

\displaystyle-\frac{n^{2}|a_{-n}|^{2}}{8(m+2\beta)^{2}(m+n+2\beta)}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n+2\beta)}}-\frac{8(m+2\beta)^{2}(m+n+2\beta)|b_{-n}|^{2}}{n^{2}(n+2\beta)^{2}}a^{2(m-n-2\beta)}

\displaystyle-\frac{n^{2}|a_{n}|^{2}}{8(m+2\beta)^{2}(m-n+2\beta)}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n+2\beta)}}-\frac{8(m+2\beta)^{2}(m-n+2\beta)|b_{-n}|^{2}}{\beta^{2}n^{2}}a^{2(m-n-2\beta)}

\displaystyle-\frac{16(m-2\beta)^{2}(m-n-2\beta)|a_{-n}|^{2}}{\beta^{2}n^{2}}\frac{1}{b^{2(m+n+2\beta)}}-\frac{n^{2}|b_{n}|^{2}}{16(m-2\beta)^{2}(m-n-2\beta)}b^{2(m-n-2\beta)}

\displaystyle=\frac{n^{2}|a_{n}|^{2}}{8(m+2\beta)^{2}(m-n+2\beta)}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n+2\beta)}}\left(1-\frac{128\left(m^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)^{2}\left(m^{2}-(n-2\beta)^{2}\right)}{n^{2}(n-2\beta)^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{n^{2}|a_{-n}|^{2}}{8(m+2\beta)^{2}(m+n+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n+2\beta)}}\left(1-\frac{128\left(m^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)^{2}\left(m^{2}-(n+2\beta)^{2}\right)}{\beta^{2}n^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+n+2\beta)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{n^{2}|b_{n}|^{2}}{8(m-2\beta)^{2}(m-n-2\beta)}b^{2(m-n-2\beta)}

\displaystyle+\frac{n^{2}|b_{-n}|^{2}}{4(m-2\beta)^{2}(m-n-2\beta)}b^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\left(1-\frac{64\left(m^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)^{2}\left(m^{2}-(n+2\beta)^{2}\right)}{n^{2}(n+2\beta)^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\frac{64\left(m^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)^{2}\left(m^{2}-(n-2\beta)^{2}\right)}{\beta^{2}n^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-n-2\beta)}\right).

Therefore, we impose the conditions

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2(m-n+2\beta)}\log\left(\frac{256\left(m^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)^{2}\left(m^{2}-(n-2\beta)^{2}\right)}{n^{2}(n-2\beta)^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2(m+n+2\beta)}\log\left(\frac{256\left(m^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)^{2}\left(m^{2}-(n+2\beta)^{2}\right)}{\beta^{2}n^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2(m-n-2\beta)}\log\left(\frac{256\left(m^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)^{2}\left(m^{2}-(n+2\beta)^{2}\right)}{n^{2}(n+2\beta)^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2(m-n-2\beta)}\log\left(\frac{256\left(m^{2}-4\beta^{2}\right)^{2}\left(m^{2}-(n-2\beta)^{2}\right)}{\beta^{2}n^{2}}\right),

and this finally yields

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{m-1}\left(\frac{n^{2}|a_{n}|^{2}}{16(m+2\beta)^{2}(m-n+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-n)}}+\frac{n^{2}|a_{-n}|^{2}}{16(m+2\beta)^{2}(m+n+2\beta)^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{2(m+n)}}\right)

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}\sum_{n=1}^{m-1}\left(\frac{n^{2}|b_{n}|^{2}}{8(m-2\beta)^{2}(m+n-2\beta)}b^{2(m+n)}+\frac{n^{2}|b_{-n}|^{2}}{8(m-2\beta)^{2}(m-n-2\beta)}b^{2(m-n)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{\Omega}\frac{u_{0}^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{4\beta}dx

which concludes the proof of the lemma in light of the previous estimates.
∎

For technical reasons that only become apparent in the involved argument involving the Bochner identity to show that eigenvalues associated to non-trivial eigenspaces are bounded from below, we will have to refine this estimate so that it involves a weight on the two elliptic operators on the right-hand side of the inequality of Theorem
2.3
.

Theorem 2.7
.

Let
P=3\,X^{4}-8\,X^{3}+10\,X^{2}-16\,X+7\in\mathbb{R}[X]
and let

\displaystyle m_{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{3}+\frac{1}{3}\sqrt{-1-\frac{2^{\frac{2}{3}}}{\sqrt[3]{32+3\sqrt{114}}}+\sqrt[3]{2(32+3\sqrt{114})}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{-\frac{8}{9}+\frac{2^{\frac{8}{3}}}{9\sqrt[3]{32+3\sqrt{114}}}-\frac{4}{9}\sqrt[3]{2(32+3\sqrt{114})}+\frac{88}{9\sqrt{-1-\frac{2^{\frac{2}{3}}}{\sqrt[3]{32+3\sqrt{114}}}+\sqrt[3]{2(32+3\sqrt{114})}}}}

\displaystyle=2.039423\cdots

be its largest real root. Fix
m_{0}<m<\infty
, and let

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}_{m}=\Delta+2(m-1)\frac{x}{|x|^{2}}\cdot\nabla+\frac{(m-1)^{2}}{|x|^{2}}

and

\displaystyle\mathfrak{L}_{m}=\partial_{z}^{2}+\frac{m-1}{z}\partial_{z}+\frac{(m-1)(m-3)}{4z^{2}}.

For all
0<\alpha<2
, for all
0<\beta<1/2
, and for all
0<\gamma<1
, there exists
\Lambda_{\gamma}<\infty
such that for all
0<a<b<\infty
, assuming that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\Lambda_{\gamma},

the following property is verified.
Define
\Omega=B_{b}\setminus\overline{B}_{a}(0)\subset\mathbb{R}^{2}
. There exists constant
C_{m,\alpha,\beta,\gamma}<\infty
such that all function
u\in W^{2,2}(\Omega)
such that
\mathscr{L}_{m}^{\ast}\mathscr{L}_{m}u=0
, the following estimate holds true

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\frac{1}{|x|^{2}}\left|\nabla u+(m-1)\frac{x}{|x|^{2}}u\right|^{2}\left(\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}+\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)dx\leq C_{m,\alpha,\beta,\gamma}\left(\int_{\Omega}\frac{u^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{4\beta}+\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{4\beta}\right)dx\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}u\right)^{2}\left(\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2\alpha}+\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2\alpha}\right)dx+\int_{\Omega}\left|\mathfrak{L}_{m}u\right|^{2}\left(\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2\alpha}+\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2\alpha}\right)dx\right).

(2.156)

However, the estimate for functions in
W^{2,2}_{0}(\Omega)
does not hold true when one adds weights to the
L^{2}
norm of
\mathscr{L}_{m}u
, so we will have to replace the operator
\mathscr{L}_{m}
by a new fourth-order elliptic operator with regular singularities that corresponds to the integral

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega_{k}(\alpha)}|z|^{2-2m}|\partial_{z}^{2}u|^{2}|dz|^{2}

before the change of variable.

3
A New Family of Fourth-Order Operators with Regular Singularities

The main issue in those estimates is that if we want to have a weighted estimate for
u
that involved the weight on the right-hand side, we have to use the
L^{2}
norm of
\mathfrak{L}_{m}
instead of
\mathscr{L}_{m}
. We therefore have to study the (real-valued) solutions to the equation
\overline{\mathfrak{L}_{m}}^{\ast}\mathfrak{L}_{m}u
, or equivalently,
\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\overline{\mathfrak{L}_{m}}^{\ast}\mathfrak{L}_{m}\right)u=0
. Recall that the Cauchy-Riemann operator is defined by

\displaystyle\partial_{z}=\frac{1}{2}\left(\partial_{x}-i\,\partial_{y}\right).

Furthermore, we have

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} \partial_{r}&u=&&\cos(\theta)\partial_{x}u\,&&+&&\sin(\theta)\partial_{y}u\\
\frac{1}{r}\partial_{\theta}u&=-&&\sin(\theta)\partial_{x}u\,&&+&&\cos(\theta)\partial_{y}u,\end{aligned}\right.

which shows that

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}\partial_{x}u\\
\partial_{y}u\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}\cos(\theta)&-\sin(\theta)\\
\sin(\theta)&\cos(\theta)\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}\partial_{r}u\vskip 3.00003pt\\
\dfrac{1}{r}\partial_{\theta}u\end{pmatrix}=\begin{pmatrix}\cos(\theta)\partial_{r}u-\sin(\theta)\dfrac{1}{r}\partial_{\theta}u\vskip 3.00003pt\\
\sin(\theta)\partial_{r}u+\cos(\theta)\dfrac{1}{r}\partial_{\theta}u\end{pmatrix}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\partial_{z}u

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}(\partial_{x}-i\,\partial_{y}u)=\frac{1}{2}\left(\left(\cos(\theta)\partial_{r}u-\sin(\theta)\frac{1}{r}\partial_{\theta}u\right)-i\left(\sin(\theta)\partial_{r}u+\cos(\theta)\frac{1}{r}\partial_{\theta}u\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left(e^{-i\theta}\partial_{r}u-i\,e^{-i\theta}\frac{1}{r}\partial_{\theta}u\right),

(3.1)

which shows that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}\,\left(z\,\partial_{z}u\right)=\frac{1}{2}r\,\partial_{r}u=\frac{1}{2}x\cdot\nabla u.

(3.2)

Likewise, we compute

\displaystyle\partial_{x}^{2}u

\displaystyle=\cos(\theta)\partial_{r}(\partial_{x}u)-\sin(\theta)\frac{1}{r}\partial_{\theta}(\partial_{x}u)

\displaystyle=\cos(\theta)\partial_{r}\left(\cos(\theta)\partial_{r}u-\sin(\theta)\frac{1}{r}\partial_{\theta}u\right)-\sin(\theta)\frac{1}{r}\partial_{\theta}\left(\cos(\theta)\partial_{r}u-\sin(\theta)\frac{1}{r}\partial_{\theta}u\right)

\displaystyle=\cos^{2}(\theta)\partial_{r}^{2}u-\cos(\theta)\sin(\theta)\frac{1}{r}\partial_{r\theta}^{2}u+\cos(\theta)\sin(\theta)\frac{1}{r^{2}}\partial_{\theta}u

\displaystyle-\cos(\theta)\sin(\theta)\frac{1}{r}\partial_{r\theta}^{2}u+\frac{\sin^{2}(\theta)}{r}\partial_{r}u+\frac{\sin^{2}(\theta)}{r^{2}}\partial_{\theta}^{2}+\cos(\theta)\sin(\theta)\frac{1}{r^{2}}\partial_{\theta}u

\displaystyle=\cos^{2}(\theta)\partial_{\theta}^{2}u+\frac{\sin^{2}(\theta)}{r}\partial_{r}u+\frac{\sin^{2}(\theta)}{r^{2}}\partial_{\theta}^{2}-2\cos(\theta)\sin(\theta)\frac{1}{r}\partial_{r\theta}^{2}u+2\cos(\theta)\sin(\theta)\frac{1}{r^{2}}\partial_{\theta}u,

and

\displaystyle\partial_{y}^{2}u

\displaystyle=\sin(\theta)\partial_{r}(\partial_{y}u)+\cos(\theta)\frac{1}{r}\partial_{\theta}(\partial_{y}u)

\displaystyle=\sin(\theta)\partial_{r}\left(\sin(\theta)\partial_{r}u+\cos(\theta)\frac{1}{r}\partial_{\theta}u\right)+\cos(\theta)\frac{1}{r}\partial_{\theta}\left(\sin(\theta)\partial_{r}u+\cos(\theta)\frac{1}{r}\partial_{\theta}u\right)

\displaystyle=\sin^{2}(\theta)\partial_{r}^{2}u+\cos(\theta)\sin(\theta)\frac{1}{r}\partial_{r\theta}^{2}u-\cos(\theta)\sin(\theta)\frac{1}{r^{2}}\partial_{\theta}u

\displaystyle+\cos(\theta)\sin(\theta)\frac{1}{r}\partial_{r\theta}^{2}u+\frac{\cos^{2}(\theta)}{r}\partial_{r}u+\frac{\cos^{2}(\theta)}{r^{2}}\partial_{\theta}^{2}u-\cos(\theta)\sin(\theta)\frac{1}{r^{2}}\partial_{\theta}u

\displaystyle=\sin^{2}(\theta)\partial_{r}^{2}u+\frac{\cos^{2}(\theta)}{r}\partial_{r}u+\frac{\cos^{2}(\theta)}{r^{2}}\partial_{\theta}^{2}u+2\cos(\theta)\sin(\theta)\frac{1}{r}\partial_{r\theta}^{2}u-2\cos(\theta)\sin(\theta)\frac{1}{r^{2}}\partial_{\theta}u.

In particular, we recover the identity

\displaystyle\Delta=\partial_{x}^{2}+\partial_{y}^{2}=\partial_{r}^{2}+\frac{1}{r}\partial_{r}+\frac{1}{r^{2}}\partial_{\theta}^{2}.

(3.3)

Then, we have

\displaystyle\partial_{xy}^{2}u=\cos(\theta)\partial_{r}(\partial_{y}u)-\sin(\theta)\frac{1}{r}\partial_{\theta}(\partial_{y}u)

\displaystyle=\cos(\theta)\partial_{r}\left(\sin(\theta)\partial_{r}u+\cos(\theta)\frac{1}{r}\partial_{\theta}u\right)-\sin(\theta)\frac{1}{r}\partial_{\theta}\left(\sin(\theta)\partial_{r}u+\cos(\theta)\frac{1}{r}\partial_{\theta}u\right)

\displaystyle=\cos(\theta)\sin(\theta)\partial_{r}^{2}u+\frac{\cos^{2}(\theta)}{r}\partial_{r\theta}^{2}u-\frac{\cos^{2}(\theta)}{r^{2}}\partial_{\theta}u

\displaystyle-\frac{\sin^{2}(\theta)}{r}\partial_{r\theta}^{2}u-\cos(\theta)\sin(\theta)\frac{1}{r}\partial_{r}u-\cos(\theta)\sin(\theta)\frac{1}{r^{2}}\partial_{\theta}^{2}+\frac{\sin^{2}(\theta)}{r^{2}}\partial_{\theta}u

\displaystyle=\cos(\theta)\sin(\theta)\partial_{r}^{2}u-\cos(\theta)\sin(\theta)\frac{1}{r}\partial_{r}u-\cos(\theta)\sin(\theta)\frac{1}{r^{2}}\partial_{\theta}^{2}+\frac{\cos^{2}(\theta)-\sin^{2}(\theta)}{r}\partial_{r\theta}^{2}u-\frac{\cos^{2}(\theta)-\sin^{2}(\theta)}{r^{2}}\partial_{\theta}u.

We also compute

\displaystyle\partial_{yx}^{2}u=\sin(\theta)\partial_{r}(\partial_{x}u)+\cos(\theta)\frac{1}{r}\partial_{\theta}\left(\partial_{x}u\right)

\displaystyle=\sin(\theta)\partial_{r}\left(\cos(\theta)\partial_{r}u-\sin(\theta)\frac{1}{r}\partial_{\theta}u\right)+\cos(\theta)\frac{1}{r}\partial_{\theta}\left(\cos(\theta)\partial_{r}u-\sin(\theta)\frac{1}{r}\partial_{\theta}u\right)

\displaystyle=\cos(\theta)\sin(\theta)\partial_{r}^{2}u-\frac{\sin^{2}(\theta)}{r}\partial_{r\theta}^{2}u+\frac{\sin^{2}(\theta)}{r}\partial_{\theta}u

\displaystyle+\frac{\cos^{2}(\theta)}{r}\partial_{r\theta}^{2}u-\frac{\cos(\theta)\sin(\theta)}{r}\partial_{r}u-\frac{\cos(\theta)\sin(\theta)}{r^{2}}\partial_{\theta}^{2}u-\frac{\cos^{2}(\theta)}{r^{2}}\partial_{\theta}u

\displaystyle=\cos(\theta)\sin(\theta)\partial_{r}^{2}u-\frac{\cos(\theta)\sin(\theta)}{r}\partial_{r}u-\frac{\cos(\theta)\sin(\theta)}{r^{2}}\partial_{\theta}^{2}u+\frac{\cos^{2}(\theta)-\sin^{2}(\theta)}{r}\partial_{r\theta}^{2}u-\frac{\cos^{2}(\theta)-\sin^{2}(\theta)}{r^{2}}\partial_{\theta}u.

Then, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}\,\left(z^{2}\,\partial_{z}^{2}\right)=\mathrm{Re}\,\left((x+i\,y)^{2}\left(\frac{1}{2}\left(\partial_{x}-i\,\partial_{y}\right)\right)^{2}\right)=\frac{1}{4}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\left(x^{2}-y^{2}+2i\,xy\right)\left(\partial_{x}^{2}-\partial_{y}^{2}-2i\,\partial_{xy}^{2}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4}\Big((x^{2}-y^{2})\left(\partial_{x}^{2}-\partial_{y}^{2}\right)+4\,xy\,\partial_{xy}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle=\frac{r^{2}}{4}\left(\left(\cos^{2}(\theta)-\sin^{2}(\theta)\right)\left(\left(\cos^{2}(\theta)-\sin^{2}(\theta)\right)\partial_{r}^{2}-\frac{\cos^{2}(\theta)-\sin^{2}(\theta)}{r}\partial_{r}-\frac{\cos^{2}(\theta)-\sin^{2}(\theta)}{r^{2}}\partial_{\theta}^{2}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.-\frac{4\cos(\theta)\sin(\theta)}{r}\partial_{r\theta}^{2}+\frac{4\cos(\theta)\sin(\theta)}{r^{2}}\partial_{\theta}\right)+4\cos(\theta)\sin(\theta)\left(\cos(\theta)\sin(\theta)\partial_{r}^{2}-\frac{\cos(\theta)\sin(\theta)}{r}\partial_{r}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.-\frac{\cos(\theta)\sin(\theta)}{r^{2}}\partial_{\theta}^{2}+\frac{\cos^{2}(\theta)-\sin^{2}(\theta)}{r}\partial_{r\theta}^{2}-\frac{\cos^{2}(\theta)-\sin^{2}(\theta)}{r^{2}}\partial_{\theta}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{r^{2}}{4}\left(\partial_{r}^{2}-\frac{1}{r}\partial_{r}-\frac{1}{r^{2}}\partial_{\theta}^{2}\right)=\frac{r^{2}}{4}\left(2\,\partial_{r}^{2}-\Delta\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{|x|^{2}}{4}\left(2\left(\frac{x}{|x|}\right)\cdot\nabla^{2}(\,\cdot\,)\cdot\left(\frac{x}{|x|}\right)-\Delta\right)=\frac{|x|^{4}}{2}\left(\frac{x}{|x|^{2}}\right)\cdot\nabla^{2}(\,\cdot\,)\cdot\left(\frac{x}{|x|^{2}}\right)-\frac{|x|^{2}}{4}\Delta.

(3.4)

Now, notice that for all
u,v\in C^{\infty}_{c}(\Omega)
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}v\frac{1}{z}\partial_{z}u|dz|^{2}=-\int_{\Omega}u\,\partial_{z}\left(\frac{1}{z}v\right)|dz|^{2}=-\int_{\Omega}u\left(\frac{1}{z}\partial_{z}v-\frac{1}{z^{2}}v\right)|dz|^{2}.

Therefore, we deduce that

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{z}\partial_{z}\right)^{\ast}=-\left(\frac{1}{z}\partial_{z}-\frac{1}{z^{2}}\right).

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\mathfrak{L}_{m}^{\ast}

\displaystyle=\left(\partial_{z}^{2}+\frac{(m-1)}{z}\partial_{z}+\frac{(m-1)(m-3)}{4z^{2}}\right)^{\ast}=\left(\partial_{z}^{2}-\frac{(m-1)}{z}\partial_{z}+\frac{(m-1)(m-3)}{4z^{2}}+\frac{(m-1)}{z^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\partial_{z}^{2}-\frac{(m-1)}{z}\partial_{z}+\frac{(m-1)^{2}}{z^{2}}+\frac{(m^{2}-1)}{4z^{2}}\right).

Now, we have

\displaystyle\overline{\mathfrak{L}_{m}}^{\ast}\mathfrak{L}_{m}=\left(\partial_{\overline{z}}^{2}-\frac{(m-1)}{\overline{z}}\partial_{\overline{z}}+\frac{(m-1)(m-3)}{4\overline{z}^{2}}+\frac{(m-1)}{\overline{z}^{2}}\right)\left(\partial_{z}^{2}+\frac{(m-1)}{z}\partial_{z}+\frac{(m-1)(m-3)}{4z^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{16}\Delta^{2}+\frac{(m-1)}{4z}\partial_{\overline{z}}\Delta+\frac{(m-1)(m-3)}{4|z|^{4}}\overline{z}^{2}\partial_{\overline{z}}^{2}-\frac{(m-1)}{4\overline{z}}\partial_{z}\Delta-\frac{(m-1)^{2}}{4|z|^{2}}\Delta-\frac{(m-1)^{2}(m-3)}{4|z|^{4}}\overline{z}\,\partial_{\overline{z}}

\displaystyle+\frac{(m-1)(m-3)}{4|z|^{4}}z^{2}\partial_{z}^{2}+\frac{(m-1)^{2}(m-3)}{4|z|^{4}}z\,\partial_{z}+\frac{(m-1)^{2}(m-3)^{2}}{16|z|^{4}}

\displaystyle+\frac{(m-1)}{|z|^{4}}z^{2}\partial_{z}^{2}+\frac{(m-1)^{2}}{|z|^{4}}z\,\partial_{z}+\frac{(m-1)^{2}(m-3)}{4|z|^{4}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{16}\Delta^{2}-\frac{i}{2}\frac{(m-1)}{|z|^{2}}\mathrm{Im}\,\left(z\,\partial_{z}\Delta\right)+\frac{i}{2}\frac{(m-1)^{2}(m-3)}{4|z|^{4}}\mathrm{Im}\,\left(z\,\partial_{z}\right)-\frac{(m-1)^{2}}{4|z|^{2}}\Delta

\displaystyle+\frac{(m-1)(m-3)}{2|z|^{4}}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(z^{2}\partial_{z}^{2}\right)+\frac{(m-1)}{|z|^{4}}z^{2}\partial_{z}^{2}+\frac{(m-1)^{2}}{|z|^{4}}z\,\partial_{z}+\frac{(m+1)(m-1)^{2}(m-3)}{16|z|^{2}}.

Therefore, we deduce that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\overline{\mathfrak{L}_{m}}^{\ast}\mathfrak{L}_{m}\right)=\frac{1}{16}\Delta^{2}-\frac{(m-1)^{2}}{4|z|^{2}}\Delta+\frac{(m-1)^{2}}{2|z|^{4}}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(z^{2}\partial_{z}^{2}\right)+\frac{(m-1)^{2}}{|z|^{4}}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(z\,\partial_{z}\right)+\frac{(m+1)(m-1)^{2}(m-3)}{16|z|^{4}}.

Using (
3.2
) and (
3
), we deduce that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\overline{\mathfrak{L}_{m}}^{\ast}\mathfrak{L}_{m}\right)=\frac{1}{16}\Delta^{2}-\frac{(m-1)^{2}}{4|x|^{2}}\Delta+\frac{(m-1)^{2}}{2}\left(\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{x}{|x|^{2}}\right)\cdot\nabla^{2}(\,\cdot\,)\cdot\left(\frac{x}{|x|^{2}}\right)-\frac{1}{4|x|^{2}}\Delta\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{(m-1)^{2}}{2|x|^{2}}\frac{x}{|x|^{2}}\cdot\nabla u+\frac{(m+1)(m-1)^{2}(m-3)}{16|x|^{4}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{16}\Delta^{2}-\frac{3(m-1)^{2}}{8|x|^{2}}\Delta+\frac{(m-1)^{2}}{4}\left(\frac{x}{|x|^{2}}\right)\cdot\nabla^{2}(\,\cdot\,)\cdot\left(\frac{x}{|x|^{2}}\right)+\frac{(m-1)^{2}}{2|x|^{2}}\frac{x}{|x|^{2}}\cdot\nabla u+\frac{(m+1)(m-1)^{2}(m-3)}{16|x|^{4}}.

And finally, we have

\displaystyle\mathfrak{D}_{m}

\displaystyle=16\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\overline{\mathfrak{L}_{m}}^{\ast}\mathfrak{L}_{m}\right)=\Delta^{2}-\frac{6(m-1)^{2}}{|x|^{2}}\Delta+4(m-1)^{2}\left(\frac{x}{|x|^{2}}\right)\cdot\nabla^{2}(\,\cdot\,)\cdot\left(\frac{x}{|x|^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{8(m-1)^{2}}{|x|^{2}}\frac{x}{|x|^{2}}\cdot\nabla u+\frac{(m+1)(m-1)^{2}(m-3)}{|x|^{4}}

Recall that in comparison, we have

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}_{m}^{\ast}\mathscr{L}_{m}=\Delta^{2}+\frac{2(m^{2}-1)}{|x|^{2}}\Delta-4(m^{2}-1)\left(\frac{x}{|x|^{2}}\right)^{t}\cdot\nabla^{2}(\,\cdot\,)\cdot\left(\frac{x}{|x|^{2}}\right)+\frac{(m^{2}-1)^{2}}{|x|^{4}}.

Let us now find a basis of solutions of
\mathfrak{D}_{m}u=0
. We have (classically or by the above computation (
3.3
))

\displaystyle\Delta=\partial_{r}^{2}+\frac{1}{r}\partial_{r}+\frac{1}{r^{2}}\partial_{\theta}^{2}.

Therefore, we get

\displaystyle\partial_{r}\Delta

\displaystyle=\partial_{r}^{3}+\frac{1}{r}\partial_{r}^{2}-\frac{1}{r^{2}}\partial_{r}+\frac{1}{r^{2}}\partial_{r}\partial_{\theta}^{2}-\frac{2}{r^{3}}\partial_{\theta}^{2}

\displaystyle\partial_{r}^{2}\Delta

\displaystyle=\partial_{r}^{4}+\frac{1}{r}\partial_{r}^{3}-\frac{2}{r^{2}}\partial_{r}^{2}+\frac{2}{r^{3}}\partial_{r}+\frac{1}{r^{2}}\partial_{r}^{2}\partial_{\theta}^{2}-\frac{4}{r^{3}}\partial_{r}\partial_{\theta}^{2}+\frac{6}{r^{4}}\partial_{\theta}^{2}.

Therefore, we find that

\displaystyle\Delta^{2}

\displaystyle=\partial_{r}^{4}+\frac{1}{r}\partial_{r}^{3}-\frac{2}{r^{2}}\partial_{r}^{2}+\frac{2}{r^{3}}\partial_{r}+\frac{1}{r^{2}}\partial_{r}^{2}\partial_{\theta}^{2}-\frac{4}{r^{3}}\partial_{r}\partial_{\theta}^{2}+\frac{6}{r^{4}}\partial_{\theta}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{r}\partial_{r}^{3}+\frac{1}{r}\partial_{r}^{2}-\frac{1}{r^{3}}\partial_{r}+\frac{1}{r^{3}}\partial_{r}\partial_{\theta}^{2}-\frac{2}{r^{4}}\partial_{\theta}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{r^{2}}\partial_{r}^{2}\partial_{\theta}^{2}+\frac{1}{r^{3}}\partial_{r}\partial_{\theta}^{2}+\frac{1}{r^{4}}\partial_{\theta}^{4}

\displaystyle=\partial_{r}^{4}+\frac{2}{r}\partial_{r}^{3}-\frac{1}{r}\partial_{r}^{2}+\frac{1}{r}\partial_{r}+\frac{2}{r^{2}}\partial_{r}^{2}\partial_{\theta}^{2}-\frac{2}{r^{3}}\partial_{r}\partial_{\theta}^{2}+\frac{4}{r^{4}}\partial_{\theta}^{2}+\frac{1}{r^{4}}\partial_{\theta}^{2}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\mathfrak{D}_{m}=\partial_{r}^{4}+\frac{2}{r}\partial_{r}^{3}-\frac{1}{r}\partial_{r}^{2}+\frac{1}{r}\partial_{r}+\frac{2}{r^{2}}\partial_{r}^{2}\partial_{\theta}^{2}-\frac{2}{r^{3}}\partial_{r}\partial_{\theta}^{2}+\frac{4}{r^{4}}\partial_{\theta}^{2}+\frac{1}{r^{4}}\partial_{\theta}^{4}

\displaystyle-6(m-1)^{2}\left(\partial_{r}^{2}+\frac{1}{r}\partial_{r}+\frac{1}{r^{2}}\partial_{\theta}^{2}\right)+4(m-1)^{2}\partial_{r}^{2}+\frac{8(m-1)^{2}}{r^{3}}\partial_{r}+\frac{(m+1)(m-1)^{2}(m-3)}{r^{4}}

\displaystyle=\partial_{r}^{4}+\frac{2}{r}\partial_{r}^{3}-\frac{2(m-1)^{2}+1}{r^{2}}\partial_{r}^{2}+\frac{2(m-1)^{2}+1}{r}\partial_{r}+\frac{2}{r^{2}}\partial_{r}^{2}\partial_{\theta}^{2}-\frac{2}{r^{3}}\partial_{r}\partial_{\theta}^{2}+\frac{4-6(m-1)^{2}}{r^{4}}\partial_{\theta}^{2}+\frac{1}{r^{4}}\partial_{\theta}^{4}

\displaystyle+\frac{(m+1)(m-1)^{2}(m-3)}{r^{4}}.

Therefore, we deduce that

\displaystyle\pi_{n^{2}}(\mathfrak{D}_{m})

\displaystyle=\partial_{r}^{4}+\frac{2}{r}\partial_{r}^{3}-\frac{2(m-1)^{2}+2n^{2}+1}{r^{2}}\partial_{r}^{2}+\frac{2(m-1)^{2}+2n^{2}+1}{r^{3}}\partial_{r}

\displaystyle+\frac{n^{4}+n^{2}(6(m-1)^{2}-4)+(m+1)(m-1)^{2}(m-3)}{r^{4}}.

Make the change of variable
u(r)=Y(\log(r))
. It yields

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} \partial_{r}u&=\frac{1}{r}Y^{\prime}\\
\partial_{r}^{2}u&=\frac{1}{r^{2}}(Y^{\prime\prime}-Y^{\prime})\\
\partial_{r}^{3}u&=\frac{1}{r^{3}}(Y^{\prime\prime\prime}-Y^{\prime\prime}-2(Y^{\prime\prime}-Y^{\prime}))=\frac{1}{r^{3}}(Y^{\prime\prime\prime}-3Y^{\prime\prime}+2Y^{\prime})\\
\partial_{r}^{4}u&=\frac{1}{r^{4}}(Y^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}-3Y^{\prime\prime\prime}+2Y^{\prime\prime}-3(Y^{\prime\prime\prime}-3Y^{\prime\prime}+2Y^{\prime\prime}))=\frac{1}{r^{4}}(Y^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}-6Y^{\prime\prime\prime}+11Y^{\prime\prime}-6Y^{\prime}).\end{aligned}\right.

Therefore, we get

\displaystyle r^{4}\Pi_{n^{2}}(\mathfrak{D}_{m})u

\displaystyle=Y^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}-6Y^{\prime\prime\prime}+11Y^{\prime\prime}-6Y^{\prime}+2\left(Y^{\prime\prime\prime}-3Y^{\prime\prime}+2Y^{\prime}\right)-(2(m-1)^{2}+2n^{2}+1)\left(Y^{\prime\prime}-Y^{\prime}\right)

\displaystyle+(2(m-1)^{2}+2n^{2}+1)Y^{\prime}+\left(n^{4}+n^{2}(6(m-1)^{2}-4)+(m+1)(m-1)^{2}(m-3)\right)Y

\displaystyle=Y^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}-4Y^{\prime\prime\prime}-\left(2(m-1)^{2}+2n^{2}-4\right)Y^{\prime\prime}+(4(m-1)^{2}+4n^{2})Y^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\left(n^{4}+n^{2}(6(m-1)^{2}-4)+(m+1)(m-1)^{2}(m-3)\right)Y.

Write for simplicity
\lambda_{m,n}=n^{4}+n^{2}(6(m-1)^{2}-4)+(m+1)(m-1)^{2}(m-3)
. The characteristic polynomial of this ordinary differential equation is given by

\displaystyle P(X)=X^{4}-4X^{3}-(2(m-1)^{2}+2n^{2}-4)X^{2}+(4(m-1)^{2}+4n^{2})X+\lambda_{m,n}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle Q(X)

\displaystyle=P(X+1)=X^{4}+4X^{3}+6X^{2}+4X+1-4(X^{3}+3X^{2}+3X+1)-(2(m-1)^{2}+2n^{2}-4)(X^{2}+2X+1)

\displaystyle+(4(m-1)^{2}+4n^{2})(X+1)+\lambda_{m,n}

\displaystyle=X^{4}-2((m-1)^{2}+n^{2}+1)X^{2}+2(m-1)^{2}+2n^{2}+1+\lambda_{m,n},

which is a biquadratic equation. The discriminant of the associated quadratic polynomial is equal to

\displaystyle D_{m,n}=16((m-1)^{2}-((m-1)^{2}-1)n^{2}).

Therefore, for
n=0
, we get
D_{m,0}=16(m-1)^{2}
, which shows that the roots of
Q(\sqrt{X})
are equal to

\displaystyle r_{0,1}=(m-1)^{2}+1+2(m-1)=m^{2}\quad r_{0,2}=(m-1)^{2}+1-2(m-1)=(m-2)^{2},

which shows that a basis of solutions of
\Pi_{0}(\mathscr{D}_{m})u=0
is given by (assuming that
m>2
)

\displaystyle r^{m+1},r^{1-m},r^{m-1},r^{3-m}.

For
n=\pm 1
, we have
D_{m,\pm 1}=16
, which yields the roots

\displaystyle r_{1,1}=(m-1)^{2}+2+2=(m-1)^{2}+4\qquad r_{1,2}=(m-1)^{2}+2-2=(m-1)^{2}.

As
m>2
, a basis of solutions of
\Pi_{\pm 1}(\mathfrak{D}_{m})u=0
is given by

\displaystyle r^{1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}},r^{1-\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}},r^{m},r^{2-m}.

Notice that a real-valued function
v
satisfies
\partial_{z}^{2}v=0
if and only if
v
belongs to the
4
-dimensional linear space spanned by

\displaystyle 1,r\cos(\theta),r\sin(\theta),r^{2}.

Indeed, since
\partial_{z}v
is anti-holomorphic, there exists
\left\{a_{n}\right\}_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}
such that

\displaystyle\partial_{z}v=\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}a_{n}\overline{z}^{n}.

Therefore, we deduce that there exists
\left\{b_{n}\right\}_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}
such that

\displaystyle v(z)=\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}a_{n}z\overline{z}^{n}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}b_{n}\overline{z}^{n}.

Since
v
must be real valued, we deduce that
b_{0}\in\mathbb{R}
, that
b_{1}=\overline{a_{0}}
, and that
a_{1}\in\mathbb{R}
. All other coefficients must vanish, which shows in the end that

\displaystyle v(z)=b_{0}+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{0}z\right)+a_{1}|z|^{2}.

Now, if
v=|z|^{m-1}u
, this implies that

\displaystyle u(z)

\displaystyle=b_{0}|z|^{1-m}+2\,|z|^{1-m}\,\mathrm{Re}\,(a_{0}z)+a_{1}|z|^{3-m}

\displaystyle=b_{0}\,r^{1-m}+a_{1}r^{3-m}+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,(a_{0})r^{2-m}\cos(\theta)-2\,\mathrm{Im}\,(a_{0})r^{2-m}\sin(\theta),

which shows that the Kernel of
\mathfrak{L}_{m}
is spanned by the functions

\displaystyle r^{1-m},r^{3-m},r^{2-m}\cos(\theta),r^{2-m}\sin(\theta).

The result is coherent with the roots found above since we have
r^{1-m},r^{3-m}\in\mathrm{Ker}(\Pi_{0}(\mathfrak{D}_{m}))
and
r^{2-m}\cos(\theta),r^{2-m}\sin(\theta)\in\mathrm{Ker}(\Pi_{\pm 1}(\mathfrak{D}_{m}))
. Indeed, as those functions belong to
\mathrm{Ker}(\Pi_{0}(\mathfrak{L}_{m})
and
\mathrm{Ker}(\Pi_{\pm 1}(\mathfrak{L}_{m}))
, they must
a fortiori
belong to
\mathrm{Ker}(\Pi_{0}(\mathrm{Re}\,(\overline{\mathfrak{L}_{m}}^{\ast}\mathfrak{L}_{m})))
and
\mathrm{Ker}(\Pi_{\pm 1}(\mathrm{Re}\,(\overline{\mathfrak{L}_{m}}^{\ast}\mathfrak{L}_{m})))
.

Now, if
n\geq 2
, we have

\displaystyle((m-1)^{2}-1)n^{2}-(m-1)^{2}\geq 3(m-1)^{2}-4>0\Longleftrightarrow m>1+\frac{2}{\sqrt{3}}=2.154700\cdots.

Therefore, assuming that
m>1+\frac{2}{\sqrt{3}}
, we have
D_{m,n}<0
for all
|n|\geq 2
. This implies that the roots of
Q(\sqrt{X})
are given by

\displaystyle r_{n,1}

\displaystyle=(m-1)^{2}+n^{2}+1+2i\sqrt{((m-1)^{2}-1)n^{2}-(m-1)^{2}}

\displaystyle r_{n,2}

\displaystyle=(m-1)^{2}+n^{2}+1-2i\sqrt{((m-1)^{2}-1)n^{2}-(m-1)^{2}}.

Recall that if
x>0
, then for all
y>0
, the roots of
x+i\,y
are given by
\pm(a+i\,b)
, where

\displaystyle a=\sqrt{\frac{\sqrt{x^{2}+y^{2}}+x}{2}}\quad b=\sqrt{\frac{\sqrt{x^{2}+y^{2}}-x}{2}},

while for
y<0
, we have

\displaystyle a=\sqrt{\frac{\sqrt{x^{2}+y^{2}}+x}{2}}\quad b=-\sqrt{\frac{\sqrt{x^{2}+y^{2}}-x}{2}}

Here, we have
x=(m-1)^{2}+n^{2}+1
and
y=\pm 2\sqrt{((m-1)^{2}-1)n^{2}-(m-1)^{2}}
.

Since the real part of the roots is positive, we deduce that the roots of
Q
are given by

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} r_{m,n,1}&=\sqrt{\frac{\sqrt{m^{2}(m-2)^{2}+n^{2}(n^{2}+6m(m-2)+4)}+(m-1)^{2}+n^{2}+1}{2}}\\
&+i\sqrt{\frac{\sqrt{m^{2}(m-2)^{2}+n^{2}(n^{2}+6m(m-2)+4)}-((m-1)^{2}+n^{2}+1)}{2}}\\
r_{m,n,2}&=-\sqrt{\frac{\sqrt{m^{2}(m-2)^{2}+n^{2}(n^{2}+6m(m-2)+4)}+(m-1)^{2}+n^{2}+1}{2}}\\
&+i\sqrt{\frac{\sqrt{m^{2}(m-2)^{2}+n^{2}(n^{2}+6m(m-2)+4)}-((m-1)^{2}+n^{2}+1)}{2}}\\
r_{m,n,3}&=\sqrt{\frac{\sqrt{m^{2}(m-2)^{2}+n^{2}(n^{2}+6m(m-2)+4)}+(m-1)^{2}+n^{2}+1}{2}}\\
&-i\sqrt{\frac{\sqrt{m^{2}(m-2)^{2}+n^{2}(n^{2}+6m(m-2)+4)}-((m-1)^{2}+n^{2}+1)}{2}}\\
r_{m,n,4}&=-\sqrt{\frac{\sqrt{m^{2}(m-2)^{2}+n^{2}(n^{2}+6m(m-2)+4)}+(m-1)^{2}+n^{2}+1}{2}}\\
&-i\sqrt{\frac{\sqrt{m^{2}(m-2)^{2}+n^{2}(n^{2}+6m(m-2)+4)}-((m-1)^{2}+n^{2}+1)}{2}}.\end{aligned}\right.

Finally, the roots of
P
are given by

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} \lambda_{m,n,1}&=1+\sqrt{\frac{\sqrt{m^{2}(m-2)^{2}+n^{2}(n^{2}+6m(m-2)+4)}+(m-1)^{2}+n^{2}+1}{2}}\\
&+i\sqrt{\frac{\sqrt{m^{2}(m-2)^{2}+n^{2}(n^{2}+6m(m-2)+4)}-((m-1)^{2}+n^{2}+1)}{2}}\\
\lambda_{m,n,2}&=1-\sqrt{\frac{\sqrt{m^{2}(m-2)^{2}+n^{2}(n^{2}+6m(m-2)+4)}+(m-1)^{2}+n^{2}+1}{2}}\\
&+i\sqrt{\frac{\sqrt{m^{2}(m-2)^{2}+n^{2}(n^{2}+6m(m-2)+4)}-((m-1)^{2}+n^{2}+1)}{2}}\\
\lambda_{m,n,3}&=1+\sqrt{\frac{\sqrt{m^{2}(m-2)^{2}+n^{2}(n^{2}+6m(m-2)+4)}+(m-1)^{2}+n^{2}+1}{2}}\\
&-i\sqrt{\frac{\sqrt{m^{2}(m-2)^{2}+n^{2}(n^{2}+6m(m-2)+4)}-((m-1)^{2}+n^{2}+1)}{2}}\\
\lambda_{m,n,4}&=1-\sqrt{\frac{\sqrt{m^{2}(m-2)^{2}+n^{2}(n^{2}+6m(m-2)+4)}+(m-1)^{2}+n^{2}+1}{2}}\\
&-i\sqrt{\frac{\sqrt{m^{2}(m-2)^{2}+n^{2}(n^{2}+6m(m-2)+4)}-((m-1)^{2}+n^{2}+1)}{2}}.\end{aligned}\right.

Noting for simplicity
\mu_{m,n}=\sqrt{m^{2}(m-2)^{2}+n^{2}(n^{2}+6m(m-2)+4)}
, we deduce that a basis of real solutions of the equation
\Pi_{n^{2}}(\mathfrak{D}_{m})u=0
for
|n|\geq 2
is given by

\displaystyle r^{1+\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{m,n}+(m-1)^{2}+n^{2}+1}{2}}}\cos\left(\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{m,n}-((m-1)^{2}+n^{2}+1)}{2}}r\right)

\displaystyle r^{1+\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{m,n}+(m-1)^{2}+n^{2}+1}{2}}}\sin\left(\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{m,n}-((m-1)^{2}+n^{2}+1)}{2}}r\right)

\displaystyle r^{1-\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{m,n}+(m-1)^{2}+n^{2}+1}{2}}}\cos\left(\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{m,n}-((m-1)^{2}+n^{2}+1)}{2}}r\right)

\displaystyle r^{1-\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{m,n}+(m-1)^{2}+n^{2}+1}{2}}}\cos\left(\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{m,n}-((m-1)^{2}+n^{2}+1)}{2}}r\right).

To simplify further the notations, write

\displaystyle\alpha_{m,n}=\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{m,n}+(m-1)^{2}+n^{2}+1}{2}}

\displaystyle\beta_{m,n}=\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{m,n}-((m-1)^{2}+n^{2}+1)}{2}}.

Then the basis of solutions is simply written as

\displaystyle r^{1+\alpha_{m,n}}\cos(\beta_{m,n}r),r^{1+\alpha_{m,n}}\sin(\beta_{m,n}r),r^{1-\alpha_{m,n}}\cos(\beta_{m,n}r),r^{1-\alpha_{m,n}}\sin(\beta_{m,n}r).

Notice that we obviously have
\alpha_{m,-n}=\alpha_{m,n}
and
\beta_{m,n}=\beta_{m,-n}
for all
n\in\mathbb{Z}\setminus\left\{-1,0,1\right\}
. Finally, if
u
solves the equation
\mathfrak{D}_{m}u=0
, it admits an expansion of the form (where
\alpha_{0},\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2},\alpha_{3}\in\mathbb{R}
and
\left\{a_{n}\right\}_{n\in\mathbb{Z}},\left\{b_{n}\right\}_{n\in\mathbb{Z}},\left\{c_{n}\right\},\left\{d_{n}\right\}\in\mathbb{C}
)

\displaystyle u(r,\theta)=\alpha_{0}\,r^{m+1}+\alpha_{1}r^{m-1}+\alpha_{2}r^{1-m}+\alpha_{3}r^{3-m}

\displaystyle+r^{1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}\mathrm{Re}\,(a_{1}e^{i\theta})+r^{1-\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}\mathrm{Re}\,(b_{1}e^{i\theta})+r^{m}\mathrm{Re}\,(c_{1}e^{i\theta})+r^{2-m}\mathrm{Re}\,(d_{1}e^{i\theta})

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}r^{1+\alpha_{m,n}}\cos(\beta_{m,n}r)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}e^{in\theta}\right)+\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}r^{1+\alpha_{m,n}}\sin(\beta_{m,n}r)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}e^{in\theta}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}r^{1-\alpha_{m,n}}\cos(\beta_{m,n}r)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(c_{n}e^{in\theta}\right)+\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}r^{1-\alpha_{m,n}}\sin(\beta_{m,n}r)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(d_{n}e^{in\theta}\right).

Now, the first issue is that for all
\alpha\in\mathbb{C}\setminus\left\{0\right\}
, the function
r^{\alpha}\cos(r)
does not admit a primitive expressible with standard functions,
Notice that

\displaystyle\alpha_{m,n}

\displaystyle\underset{|n|\rightarrow\infty}{\sim}|n|

\displaystyle\beta_{m,n}

\displaystyle=\frac{4(((m-1)^{2}-1)n^{2}-(m-1)^{2})}{\sqrt{m^{2}(m-2)^{2}+n^{2}(n^{2}+6m(m-2)+4)}+(m-1)^{2}+n^{2}+1}\underset{|n|\rightarrow\infty}{\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\meno}$}}}\mathrel{\mkern-3.0mu}\rightarrow}2((m-1)^{2}-1)=2m(m-2).

Now, consider for
a,b,c>0
the function

\displaystyle f(x)=\sqrt{x^{2}+ax+b}-(x+c).

We have

\displaystyle f^{\prime}(x)=\frac{2x+a}{\sqrt{x^{2}+ax+b}}-1\geq 0\Longleftrightarrow(2x+a)^{2}-(x^{2}+ax+b)\geq 0\Longleftrightarrow 3x^{2}+ax+a^{2}-b\geq 0.

The discriminant of
3X^{2}+aX+a^{2}-b
is equal to
D=-11a^{2}+12b
. In our case of interest, we have
a=6m(m-2)+4
,
b=m^{2}(m-2)^{2}
and
c=(m-1)^{2}+1
, which yields

\displaystyle D

\displaystyle=-11\left(6m(m-2)+4\right)^{2}+12m^{2}(m-2)^{2}=-16(24m^{2}(m-2)^{2}+33m(m-2)+11)<0

for all
m\geq 2
. Therefore,
f
is increasing and

\displaystyle f(x)\geq f(1)=\sqrt{4+2a+b}-(c+2)\quad\text{for all}\;\,x\geq 2.

and we have

\displaystyle 4+2a+b-(c+2)^{2}=m^{4}-4\,m^{3}+15\,m^{2}-22\,m+8>0

for all

\displaystyle m>1+\sqrt{\frac{\sqrt{89}-9}{2}}=1.465822\cdots.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\sqrt{m^{2}(m-2)^{2}+12m(m-2)+12}-((m-1)^{2}+2)=\frac{m^{4}-4\,m^{3}+15\,m^{2}-22\,m}{\sqrt{m^{2}(m-2)^{2}+12m(m-2)+12}+((m-1)^{2}+2)}

\displaystyle\leq\beta_{m,n}\leq 2m(m-2)

for all
|n|\geq 2
. By the elementary inequality
\sin(x)\leq x
for all
x\geq 0
, we deduce that

\displaystyle\cos(x)-1+\frac{x^{2}}{2}\geq 0\qquad\text{for all}\;\,x\geq 0,

which shows that

\displaystyle\sin(x)\geq x-\dfrac{x^{3}}{6}\quad\text{for all}\;\,x\geq 0.

Therefore, for all
0\leq x\leq 1
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\leq\cos(x)\leq 1

\displaystyle\frac{x}{2}\leq\sin(x)\leq x.

Then, we need a more precise expansion of
\alpha_{m,n}
. We have

\displaystyle\mu_{m,n}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{m^{2}(m-2)^{2}+n^{2}(n^{2}+6m(m-2)+4)}=n^{2}\sqrt{1+\frac{2(3m(m-2)+2)}{n^{2}}+\frac{m^{2}(m-2)^{2}}{n^{4}}}

\displaystyle=n^{2}+3m(m-2)+2+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right),

and this implies that

\displaystyle\alpha_{m,n}=\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{m,n}+(m-1)^{2}+n^{2}+1}{2}}=\sqrt{n^{2}+2m(m-2)+2+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)}=|n|+m(m-2)+1+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right).

This means that there exists
0<\gamma_{m}<\infty
such that
