0\leq r\leq\frac{1}{2m(m-2)}

\displaystyle r^{2+m(m-2)+n-\frac{\gamma_{m}}{n^{2}}}\leq r^{1+\alpha_{m,n}}\cos(\beta_{m,n}r)\leq r^{2+m(m-2)+n+\frac{\gamma_{m}}{n^{2}}}

\displaystyle r^{-m(m-2)+n-\frac{\gamma_{m}}{n^{2}}}\leq r^{1-\alpha_{m,n}}\cos(\beta_{m,n}r)\leq r^{-m(m-2)+n+\frac{\gamma_{m}}{n^{2}}}

\displaystyle\beta_{m,2}r^{3+m(m-2)+n-\frac{\gamma_{m}}{n^{2}}}\leq r^{1+\alpha_{m,n}}\sin(\beta_{m,n}r)\leq 2m(m-2)r^{3+m(m-2)+n+\frac{\gamma_{m}}{n^{2}}}

\displaystyle\beta_{m,2}r^{1-m(m-2)+n-\frac{\gamma_{m}}{n^{2}}}\leq r^{1-\alpha_{m,n}}\sin(\beta_{m,n}r)\leq\beta_{m}r^{1-m(m-2)+n+\frac{\gamma_{m}}{n^{2}}}.

It means that when we integrate
u
and its derivatives on
\Omega=B_{b}\setminus\overline{B}_{a}(0)
(provided that
b\leq\dfrac{1}{2m(m-2)}
),
u
behaves like the function

\displaystyle v(z)

\displaystyle=\alpha_{0}|z|^{m+1}+\alpha_{1}|z|^{m-1}+\alpha_{2}|z|^{1-m}+\alpha_{3}|z|^{3-m}

\displaystyle+r^{\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}\mathrm{Re}\,(a_{1}z)+r^{-\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}\mathrm{Re}\,(b_{1}z)+r^{m-1}\mathrm{Re}\,(c_{1}z)+r^{1-m}\mathrm{Re}\,(d_{1}z)

\displaystyle+|z|^{m(m-2)+2}\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\mathrm{Re}\,(a_{n}z^{n})+|z|^{m(m-2)+3}\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\mathrm{Re}\,(b_{n}z^{n})

\displaystyle+|z|^{-m(m-2)}\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\frac{c_{n}}{z^{n}}\right)+|z|^{1-m(m-2)}\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\frac{d_{n}}{z^{n}}\right)

which is much more reminiscent than the expression for solutions of
\mathscr{L}^{\ast}_{m}\mathscr{L}_{m}w=0
, whose solutions can be expanded products by harmonic functions multiplied by a fixed radial function of
m
:

\displaystyle w(z)=|z|^{1-m}\left(\alpha_{0}\log|z|+\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}a_{n}z^{n}\right)\right)+|z|^{m+1}\left(\alpha_{1}\log|z|+\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}b_{n}z^{n}\right)\right).

Now, let us go back to the expression

\displaystyle u(r,\theta)=\alpha_{0}\,r^{m+1}+\alpha_{1}r^{m-1}+\alpha_{2}r^{1-m}+\alpha_{3}r^{3-m}

\displaystyle+r^{1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}\mathrm{Re}\,(a_{1}e^{i\theta})+r^{1-\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}\mathrm{Re}\,(b_{1}e^{i\theta})+r^{m}\mathrm{Re}\,(c_{1}e^{i\theta})+r^{2-m}\mathrm{Re}\,(d_{1}e^{i\theta})

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}r^{1+\alpha_{m,n}}\cos(\beta_{m,n}r)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}e^{in\theta}\right)+\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}r^{1+\alpha_{m,n}}\sin(\beta_{m,n}r)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}e^{in\theta}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}r^{1-\alpha_{m,n}}\cos(\beta_{m,n}r)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(c_{n}e^{in\theta}\right)+\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}r^{1-\alpha_{m,n}}\sin(\beta_{m,n}r)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(d_{n}e^{in\theta}\right).

We can now state the main theorem of this section.

Theorem 3.1
.

Let
3\leq m<\infty
and define

\displaystyle\mathfrak{L}_{m}=\partial_{z}^{2}+\frac{m-1}{z}\partial_{z}+\frac{(m-1)(m-3)}{4z^{2}}.

For all
0<\gamma<1
, for all
0<\beta<1
, and for all
0<\alpha<2
, there exists a universal constant
\Gamma_{m,\alpha,\beta,\gamma}<\infty
and
C_{\alpha,\beta,\gamma}
with the following property.
Let
0<a<b<\infty
and
\Omega=B_{b}\setminus\overline{B}_{a}(0)
. Then, provided that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\Gamma_{m,\alpha,\beta,\gamma},

(3.5)

for all
u\in W^{2,2}(\Omega)
such that
\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\overline{\mathfrak{L}}_{m}^{\ast}\mathfrak{L}_{m}\right)u=0
, or equivalently

\displaystyle\Delta^{2}u-\frac{6(m^{2}-1)}{|x|^{2}}\Delta u+4(m^{2}-1)\left(\frac{x}{|x|^{2}}\right)^{t}\cdot\nabla^{2}u\cdot\left(\frac{x}{|x|^{2}}\right)+\frac{8(m-1)^{2}}{|x|^{2}}\frac{x}{|x|^{2}}\cdot\nabla u

\displaystyle+\frac{(m+1)(m-1)^{2}(m-3)}{|x|^{4}}u=0,

we have

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\left|\nabla u+(m-1)\frac{x}{|x|^{2}}u\right|^{2}\left(\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}+\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)dx\leq C_{\alpha,\beta,\gamma}\left(\int_{\Omega}\frac{u^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{4\beta}+\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{4\beta}\right)dx\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}u\right)^{2}\left(\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2\alpha}+\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2\alpha}\right)dx+\int_{\Omega}\left|\mathfrak{L}_{m}u\right|^{2}\left(\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2\alpha}+\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2\alpha}\right)dx\right).

Remark 3.2
.

To compare to the previous theorem, recall that the solutions of
\mathscr{L}_{m}^{\ast}\mathscr{L}_{m}u=0
satisfy the equation

\displaystyle\Delta^{2}u+\frac{2(m^{2}-1)}{|x|^{2}}\Delta u-4(m^{2}-1)\left(\frac{x}{|x|^{2}}\right)^{t}\cdot\nabla^{2}u\cdot\left(\frac{x}{|x|^{2}}\right)+\frac{(m-1)^{2}}{|x|^{4}}u=0.

Although formally similar, the Taylor expansion of the solution of the former system are significantly more involved.

Proof.

Step 1.
Expansion and first integral.

Recall that
u
admits the following expansion

\displaystyle u(r,\theta)=\alpha_{0}\,r^{m+1}+\alpha_{1}\,r^{m-1}+\alpha_{2}\,r^{1-m}+\alpha_{3}\,r^{3-m}

\displaystyle+r^{1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}\,\mathrm{Re}\,(a_{1}e^{i\theta})+r^{1-\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}\,\mathrm{Re}\,(b_{1}e^{i\theta})+r^{m}\,\mathrm{Re}\,(c_{1}e^{i\theta})+r^{2-m}\,\mathrm{Re}\,(d_{1}e^{i\theta})

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}r^{1+\alpha_{m,n}}\cos(\beta_{m,n}r)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}e^{in\theta}\right)+\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}r^{1+\alpha_{m,n}}\sin(\beta_{m,n}r)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}e^{in\theta}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}r^{1-\alpha_{m,n}}\cos(\beta_{m,n}r)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(c_{n}e^{in\theta}\right)+\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}r^{1-\alpha_{m,n}}\sin(\beta_{m,n}r)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(d_{n}e^{in\theta}\right).

Furthermore, the following estimates hold true, provided that
0\leq r\leq b\leq\dfrac{1}{2m(m-2)}
:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\leq\cos(\beta_{m,n}r)\leq 1

\displaystyle\frac{\beta_{m,n}r}{2}\leq\sin(\beta_{m,n}r)\leq\beta_{m,n}r.

We now compute

\displaystyle\partial_{r}u=(m+1)\alpha_{0}\,r^{m}+(m-1)\alpha_{1}\,r^{m-2}+(1-m)\alpha_{2}\,r^{-m}+(3-m)\alpha_{3}\,r^{2-m}

\displaystyle+\left(1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)r^{\sqrt{(m-1)^{4}+4}}\,\mathrm{Re}\,(a_{1}e^{i\theta})+\left(1-\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)r^{-\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{1}e^{i\theta}\right)

\displaystyle+m\,r^{m-1}\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(c_{1}e^{i\theta}\right)+(2-m)r^{1-m}\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(d_{1}e^{i\theta}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\left((1+\alpha_{m,n})r^{\alpha_{m,n}}\cos(\beta_{m,n}r)-\beta_{m,n}r^{1+\alpha_{m,n}}\sin(\beta_{m,n}r)\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}e^{in\theta}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\left((1+\alpha_{m,n})r^{\alpha_{m,n}}\sin(\beta_{m,n}r)+\beta_{m,n}r^{1+\alpha_{m,n}}\cos(\beta_{m,n}r)\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}e^{in\theta}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\left((1-\alpha_{m,n})r^{-\alpha_{m,n}}\cos(\beta_{m,n}r)-\beta_{m,n}r^{1-\alpha_{m,n}}\sin(\beta_{m,n}r)\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(c_{n}e^{in\theta}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\left((1-\alpha_{m,n})r^{-\alpha_{m,n}}\sin(\beta_{m,n}r)+\beta_{m,n}r^{1-\alpha_{m,n}}\cos(\beta_{m,n}r)\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(d_{n}e^{in\theta}\right).

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\partial_{r}u+\frac{m-1}{r}u=2m\,\alpha_{0}r^{m}+2(m-1)\alpha_{1}r^{m-2}+2\,\alpha_{3}r^{2-m}

\displaystyle+\left(m+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)r^{\sqrt{(m-1)^{4}+4}}\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{1}e^{i\theta}\right)+\left(m-\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)r^{-\sqrt{(m-1)^{4}+4}}\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{1}e^{i\theta}\right)

\displaystyle+(2m-1)r^{m-1}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(c_{1}e^{i\theta}\right)+r^{1-m}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(d_{1}e^{i\theta}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\left((m+\alpha_{m,n})r^{\alpha_{m,n}}\cos(\beta_{m,n}r)-\beta_{m,n}r^{1+\alpha_{m,n}}\sin(\beta_{m,n}r)\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}e^{in\theta}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\left((m+\alpha_{m,n})r^{\alpha_{m,n}}\sin(\beta_{m,n}r)+\beta_{m,n}r^{1+\alpha_{m,n}}\cos(\beta_{m,n}r)\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}e^{in\theta}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\left((m-\alpha_{m,n})r^{-\alpha_{m,n}}\cos(\beta_{m,n}r)-\beta_{m,n}r^{1-\alpha_{m,n}}\sin(\beta_{m,n}r)\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(c_{n}e^{in\theta}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\left((m-\alpha_{m,n})r^{-\alpha_{m,n}}\sin(\beta_{m,n}r)+\beta_{m,n}r^{1-\alpha_{m,n}}\cos(\beta_{m,n}r)\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(d_{n}e^{in\theta}\right).

Notice that for
m=\dfrac{5}{2}
,
m-\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}=0
, so we will not have to estimate this term in this specific case. Thanks to the elementary estimate

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p}a_{i}\right)^{2}\leq p\sum_{i=1}^{p}a_{i}^{2}\qquad\text{for all}\;\,(a_{1},\cdots,a_{p})\in\mathbb{R}^{p},

and Fourier series, we deduce that

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\frac{1}{|x|^{2}}\left|\partial_{r}u+\frac{m-1}{r}u\right|^{2}|x|^{2\gamma}dx\leq 6\pi\left(4m^{2}\alpha_{0}^{2}\int_{a}^{b}r^{2m-1+2\gamma}dr+4(m-1)^{2}\alpha_{1}^{2}\int_{a}^{b}r^{2m-5+2\gamma}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+4\alpha_{3}^{2}\int_{a}^{b}r^{3-2m+2\gamma}dr\right)

\displaystyle+4\pi\left(\left(m+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)^{2}|a_{1}|^{2}\int_{a}^{b}r^{2\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-1+2\gamma}dr\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\left(m-\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)^{2}|b_{1}|^{2}\int_{a}^{b}r^{-1-2\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}+2\gamma}dr\right.

\displaystyle\left.+(2m-1)^{2}|c_{1}|^{2}\int_{a}^{b}r^{2m-3+2\gamma}dr+|d_{1}|^{2}\int_{a}^{b}r^{1-2m+2\gamma}dr\right)

\displaystyle+8\pi\left(\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}|a_{n}|^{2}\int_{a}^{b}\left((m+\alpha_{m,n})^{2}r^{2\alpha_{m,n}-1+2\gamma}\cos^{2}(\beta_{m,n}r)+\beta_{m,n}^{2}r^{2\alpha_{m,n}+1+2\gamma}\sin^{2}(\beta_{m,n}r)\right)dr\right.

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}|b_{n}|^{2}\int_{a}^{b}\left((m+\alpha_{m,n})^{2}r^{2\alpha_{m,n}-1+2\gamma}\sin^{2}(\beta_{m,n}r)+\beta_{m,n}^{2}r^{2\alpha_{m,n}+1+2\gamma}\cos^{2}(\beta_{m,n}r)\right)dr

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}|c_{n}|^{2}\int_{a}^{b}\left((m+\alpha_{m,n})^{2}r^{-2\alpha_{m,n}-1+2\gamma}\cos^{2}(\beta_{m,n}r)+\beta_{m,n}^{2}r^{-2\alpha_{m,n}+1+2\gamma}\sin^{2}(\beta_{m,n}r)\right)dr

\displaystyle\left.+\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}|d_{n}|^{2}\int_{a}^{b}\left((m+\alpha_{m,n})^{2}r^{-2\alpha_{m,n}-1+2\gamma}\sin^{2}(\beta_{m,n}r)+\beta_{m,n}^{2}r^{-2\alpha_{m,n}+1+2\gamma}\cos^{2}(\beta_{m,n}r)\right)dr\right)

\displaystyle\leq 6\pi\left(\frac{2m^{2}\alpha_{0}^{2}}{m+\gamma}b^{2(m+\gamma)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+\gamma)}\right)+\frac{2(m-1)^{2}\alpha_{1}^{2}}{m-2+\gamma}b^{2(m-2+\gamma)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2+\gamma)}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{2\alpha_{3}^{2}}{m-2-\gamma}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-2-\gamma)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2-\gamma)}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+2\pi\left(\frac{\left(m+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)^{2}}{\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}+\gamma}|a_{1}|^{2}b^{2\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}+\gamma\right)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}+\gamma\right)}\right)\right.

\displaystyle+\frac{\left(m-\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)^{2}}{\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-\gamma}|b_{1}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-\gamma\right)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-\gamma\right)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{(2m-1)^{2}}{m-1+\gamma}|c_{1}|^{2}b^{2(m-1+\gamma)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-1+\gamma)}\right)+\frac{|d_{1}|^{2}}{m-1-\gamma}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-1-\gamma)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-1-\gamma)}\right)

\displaystyle+4\pi\left(\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}|a_{n}|^{2}\left(\frac{(m+\alpha_{m,n})^{2}}{\alpha_{m,n}+\gamma}b^{2(\alpha_{m,n}+\gamma)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha_{m,n}+\gamma)}\right)+\frac{\beta_{m,n}^{4}}{\alpha_{m,n}+1+\gamma}b^{2(\alpha_{m,n}+1+\gamma)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha_{m,n}+1+\gamma)}\right)\right)\right.

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\frac{(m+\alpha_{m,n})^{2}+\beta_{m,n}^{4}}{\alpha_{m,n}+1+\gamma}|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(\alpha_{m,n}+1+\gamma)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha_{m,n}+1+\gamma)}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}|c_{n}|^{2}\left(\frac{(m+\alpha_{m,n})^{2}}{\alpha_{m,n}-\gamma}\frac{1}{a^{2(\alpha_{m,n}-\gamma)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha_{m,n}-\gamma)}\right)+\frac{\beta_{m,n}^{4}}{\alpha_{m,n}-2-\gamma}\frac{1}{a^{2(\alpha_{m,n}-2-\gamma)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha_{m,n}-2-\gamma)}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\left.+\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\frac{(m+\alpha_{m,n})^{2}+\beta_{m,n}^{4}}{\alpha_{m,n}-2-\gamma}|d_{n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2\left(\alpha_{m,n}-2-\gamma\right)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha_{m,n}-2-\gamma)}\right)\right).

(3.6)

Step 2.
The radial component.

Now, make the decomposition
u=\mathrm{Rad}(u)+u_{0}
, where
\mathrm{Rad}(u)
is the radial component.
We have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Rad}(u)=\alpha_{0}r^{m+1}+\alpha_{1}r^{m-1}+\alpha_{2}r^{1-m}+\alpha_{3}r^{3-m}.

We have

\displaystyle\partial_{r}\mathrm{Rad}(u)=(m+1)\alpha_{0}r^{m}+(m-1)\alpha_{1}r^{m-2}+(1-m)\alpha_{2}r^{-m}+(3-m)\alpha_{3}r^{2-m}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{r}\left(\partial_{r}\mathrm{Rad}(u)+\frac{(m-1)}{r}u\right)=2\left(m\,\alpha_{0}r^{m-1}+(m-1)\alpha_{1}r^{m-3}+\alpha_{3}r^{1-m}\right).

On the other hand, we have for all
\alpha\in\mathbb{R}

\displaystyle\partial_{z}r^{\alpha}

\displaystyle=\partial_{r}(z\overline{z})^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}=\frac{\alpha}{2}\overline{z}|z|^{\alpha-2}=\frac{\alpha}{2z}r^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\partial_{z}^{2}r^{\alpha}

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha(\alpha-2)}{4}\overline{z}^{2}|z|^{\alpha-4}=\frac{\alpha(\alpha-2)}{4z^{2}}r^{\alpha}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\mathfrak{L}_{m}r^{\alpha}

\displaystyle=\left(\partial_{z}^{2}+\frac{m-1}{z}\partial_{z}+\frac{(m-1)(m-3)}{4z^{2}}\right)=\left(\frac{\alpha(\alpha-2)}{4}+\frac{(m-1)\alpha}{2}+\frac{(m-1)(m-3)}{4}\right)\frac{r^{\alpha}}{z^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4z^{2}}\left(\alpha+m-1\right)\left(\alpha+m-3\right)r^{\alpha},

which yields

\displaystyle\dfrac{4z^{2}}{|z|^{2}}\mathfrak{L}_{m}r^{\alpha}=(\alpha+m-1)(\alpha+m-3)r^{\alpha-2}.

Therefore,
r^{1-m},r^{3-m}\in\mathrm{Ker}(\mathfrak{L}_{m})
and we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{z^{2}}{|z|^{2}}\mathfrak{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)=(m-1)\left(m\,\alpha_{0}r^{m-1}+(m-2)\alpha_{1}r^{m-3}\right).

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}_{m}=\Delta+2(m-1)\frac{x}{|x|^{2}}\cdot\nabla+\frac{(m-1)^{2}}{|x|^{2}}=\partial_{r}^{2}+\frac{(2m-1)}{r}\partial_{r}+\frac{(m-1)^{2}}{r^{2}}+\frac{1}{r^{2}}\partial_{\theta}^{2}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}_{m}r^{\alpha}=\left(\alpha(\alpha-1)+(2m-1)\alpha+(m-1)\right)^{2}r^{\alpha-2}=\left(\alpha^{2}+2(m-1)\alpha+(m-1)^{2}\right)r^{\alpha-2}=(\alpha+m-1)^{2}r^{\alpha-2}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)=4\left(m^{2}\alpha_{0}r^{m-1}+(m-1)^{2}\alpha_{1}r^{m-3}+\alpha_{3}r^{1-m}\right).

First, we estimate

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\frac{1}{|x|^{2}}\left|\partial_{r}\mathrm{Rad}(u)+\frac{m-1}{r}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right|^{2}dx\leq 24\pi\int_{a}^{b}\left(m^{2}\alpha_{0}^{2}r^{2m-2}+(m-1)^{2}\alpha_{1}^{2}r^{2m-6}+\alpha_{3}^{2}r^{2-2m}\right)r\,dr

\displaystyle=12\pi\left(m\alpha_{0}^{2}b^{2m}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)+(m-1)\alpha_{1}^{2}b^{2(m-2)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2)}\right)+\alpha_{3}^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-2)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2)}\right)\right).

(3.7)

Now, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\left|\mathfrak{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right|^{2}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2\alpha}dx=2\pi(m-1)^{2}\int_{a}^{b}\left(m^{2}\alpha_{0}^{2}r^{2m-2}+(m-2)^{2}\alpha_{1}^{2}r^{2m-6}+2m(m-2)\alpha_{0}\alpha_{1}r^{2m-4}\right)r^{1+2\alpha}\frac{dx}{b^{2\alpha}}

\displaystyle=\pi(m-1)^{2}\left(\frac{m^{2}}{m+\alpha}\alpha_{0}^{2}b^{2m}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+\alpha)}\right)+\frac{(m-2)^{2}}{m-2+\alpha}\alpha_{1}^{2}b^{2(m-2)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2+\alpha)}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{2m(m-2)}{m-1+\alpha}\alpha_{0}\alpha_{1}b^{2(m-1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-1+\alpha)}\right)\right).

We estimate

\displaystyle\frac{m^{2}}{m+\alpha}\alpha_{0}^{2}b^{2m}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+\alpha)}\right)+\frac{(m-2)^{2}}{m-2+\alpha}\alpha_{1}^{2}b^{2(m-2)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2+\alpha)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2m(m-2)}{m-1+\alpha}\alpha_{0}\alpha_{1}b^{2(m-1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-1+\alpha)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{m^{2}}{m+\alpha}\alpha_{0}^{2}b^{2m}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+\alpha)}\right)-\frac{m^{2}(m-2)(m-2+\alpha)}{(m-1+\alpha)^{2}}\alpha_{0}^{2}b^{2m}\frac{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-1+\alpha)}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2+\alpha)}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2+\alpha)}}\frac{\alpha_{0}^{2}}{(m+\alpha)(m-1+\alpha)^{2}}b^{2m}\left(m^{2}-2m^{2}(m-1+\alpha)^{2}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-1+\alpha)}\right).

Therefore, we impose the following constraint on the conformal class:

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{m-1+\alpha}\log\left(\frac{m-1+\alpha}{2}\right),

which yields

\displaystyle\frac{m^{2}}{m+\alpha}\alpha_{0}^{2}b^{2m}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+\alpha)}\right)+\frac{(m-2)^{2}}{m-2+\alpha}\alpha_{1}^{2}b^{2(m-2)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2+\alpha)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2m(m-2)}{m-1+\alpha}\alpha_{0}\alpha_{1}b^{2(m-1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-1+\alpha)}\right)\geq\frac{m^{2}\alpha_{0}^{2}}{2(m+\alpha)(m-1+\alpha)^{2}}b^{2m}.

Likewise, if

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{m-1+\alpha}\log\left(\frac{m-2+\alpha}{2}\right),

we get

\displaystyle\frac{m^{2}}{m+\alpha}\alpha_{0}^{2}b^{2m}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m+\alpha)}\right)+\frac{(m-2)^{2}}{m-2+\alpha}\alpha_{1}^{2}b^{2(m-2)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2+\alpha)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2m(m-2)}{m-1+\alpha}\alpha_{0}\alpha_{1}b^{2(m-1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-1+\alpha)}\right)\geq\frac{(m-2)^{2}\alpha_{1}^{2}}{2(m-2+\alpha)(m-1+\alpha)^{2}}b^{2(m-2)}

and finally

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\left|\mathfrak{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right|^{2}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2\alpha}dx\geq\frac{\pi\,m^{2}(m-1)^{2}\alpha_{0}^{2}}{4(m+\alpha)(m-1+\alpha)^{2}}b^{2m}+\frac{\pi(m-1)^{2}(m-2)^{2}\alpha_{1}^{2}}{4(m-2+\alpha)(m-1+\alpha)^{2}}b^{2(m-2)}.

(3.8)

Now, using the elementary inequality

\displaystyle 2ab\leq\varepsilon\,a^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}b^{2}\qquad\forall\;\,a,b\in\mathbb{R}\,\;\forall\varepsilon>0,

we deduce that
(a+b)^{2}\geq\dfrac{1}{2}a^{2}-b^{2}
, which yields

\displaystyle\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right)^{2}\geq 16\left(\frac{1}{2}\alpha_{3}^{2}r^{2-2m}-2m^{4}\alpha_{0}^{2}r^{2m-2}-2(m-1)^{4}\alpha_{1}^{2}r^{2m-6}\right),

and we get

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right)^{2}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2\alpha}dx\geq 16\pi\,a^{2\alpha}\int_{a}^{b}\alpha_{3}^{2}r^{3-2m-2\alpha}dr-64\pi\int_{a}^{b}\left(m^{4}\alpha_{0}^{2}r^{2m-1}+(m-1)^{4}\alpha_{1}^{2}r^{2m-5}\right)dr

\displaystyle=\frac{8\pi\alpha_{3}^{2}}{m-2+\alpha}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-2)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2+\alpha)}\right)-32\pi\,m^{3}\alpha_{0}^{2}b^{2m}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2m}\right)

\displaystyle-32\pi(m-1)^{3}\alpha_{1}^{2}b^{2(m-2)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{8\pi\alpha_{3}^{2}}{m-2+\alpha}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-2)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-2+\alpha)}\right)

\displaystyle-128(m-1+\alpha)^{2}\left(\frac{m(m+\alpha)}{(m-1)^{2}}+\frac{(m-1)(m-2+\alpha)}{(m-2)^{2}}\right)\int_{\Omega}\left|\mathfrak{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right|^{2}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2\alpha}dx,

(3.9)

where we used (
3.8
). Therefore, we impose

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{\log(2)}{2(m-2+\alpha)}

to get

\displaystyle\frac{4\pi\alpha_{3}^{2}}{m-2+\alpha}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-2)}}\leq\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right)^{2}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2\alpha}dx

\displaystyle+128(m-1+\alpha)^{2}\left(\frac{m(m+\alpha)}{(m-1)^{2}}+\frac{(m-1)(m-2+\alpha)}{(m-2)^{2}}\right)\int_{\Omega}\left|\mathfrak{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right|^{2}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2\alpha}dx.

(3.10)

Therefore, we deduce by (
3
), (
3.8
), and (
3
) that

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\frac{1}{|x|^{2}}\left|\partial_{r}\mathrm{Rad}(u)+\frac{m-1}{r}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right|^{2}dx\leq 3(m-2+\alpha)\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right)^{2}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2\alpha}dx

\displaystyle+384(m-2+\alpha)(m-1+\alpha)^{2}\left(\frac{m(m+\alpha)}{(m-1)^{2}}+\frac{(m-1)(m-2+\alpha)}{(m-2)^{2}}\right)\int_{\Omega}\left|\mathfrak{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right|^{2}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2\alpha}dx

\displaystyle+48(m-1+\alpha)^{2}\left(\frac{m+\alpha}{m(m-1)^{2}}+\frac{m-2+\alpha}{(m-1)(m-2)^{2}}\right)\int_{\Omega}\left|\mathfrak{L}_{m}\mathrm{Rad}(u)\right|^{2}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2\alpha}dx.

(3.11)

Step 3.
The first Fourier mode.

Now, let us estimate the first frequency since it also has a special status. The difficulty here is that
r^{2-m}\cos(\theta),r^{2-m}\sin(\theta)\in\mathrm{Ker}(\mathscr{L}_{m})\cap\mathrm{Ker}(\mathfrak{L}_{m})
, so we will also need the weighted
L^{2}
norm of
u
to control all components.

Now, recalling (
3
), we have

\displaystyle\partial_{z}=\frac{1}{2}\left(e^{-i\theta}\partial_{r}-i\,e^{-i\theta}\frac{1}{r}\partial_{\theta}\right).

Therefore, for all
z\in\mathbb{Z}
, we have

\displaystyle\partial_{z}\left(r^{\alpha}e^{in\theta}\right)=\frac{\alpha+n}{2}r^{\alpha-1}e^{i(n-1)\theta}

\displaystyle\partial_{z}^{2}\left(r^{\alpha}e^{in\theta}\right)=\frac{(\alpha+n)(\alpha-2+n)}{4}r^{\alpha-2}e^{i(n-2)\theta},

which yields

\displaystyle\mathfrak{L}_{m}\left(r^{\alpha}e^{in\theta}\right)=\left(\partial_{z}^{2}+\frac{m-1}{z}\partial_{z}+\frac{(m-1)(m-3)}{4z^{2}}\right)\left(r^{\alpha}e^{in\theta}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{(\alpha+n)(\alpha-2+n)}{4}r^{\alpha-2}e^{i(n-2)\theta}+\frac{(m-1)(\alpha+n)}{2}r^{\alpha-2}e^{i(n-2)\theta}+\frac{(m-1)(m-3)}{4}r^{\alpha-2}e^{i(n-2)\theta}

\displaystyle=\frac{(\alpha+m+n-1)(\alpha+m+n-3)}{4}r^{\alpha-2}e^{i(n-2)\theta},

where we used that

\displaystyle(\alpha+n)(\alpha+n-2)+2(m-1)(\alpha+n)+(m-1)(m-3)

\displaystyle=(\alpha+n)(\alpha+n-2+(m-1))+(m-1)((\alpha+n)+(m-3))=(\alpha+m+n-1)(\alpha+m+n-3).

For Fourier consideration, it is useful to consider instead
e^{2i\theta}\mathfrak{L}_{m}
, and we compute

\displaystyle 8\frac{z^{2}}{|z|^{2}}\mathfrak{L}_{m}\left(r^{\alpha}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a\,e^{in\theta}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=(\alpha+m+n-1)(\alpha+m+n-3)a\,r^{\alpha-2}e^{in\theta}

\displaystyle+(\alpha+m-n-1)(\alpha+m-n-3)\overline{a}\,r^{\alpha-2}e^{-in\theta}.

We see in particular that no element belongs to the Kernel provided that
|n|\geq 2
. Write
u_{1}
the function associated with the
1
st Fourier frequency. Explicitly, we have

\displaystyle u_{1}(r,\theta)=r^{1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{1}\,e^{i\theta}\right)+r^{1-\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{1}\,e^{i\theta}\right)+r^{m}\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(c_{1}\,e^{i\theta}\right)+r^{2-m}\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(d_{1}\,e^{i\theta}\right).

Recall that

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}_{m}

\displaystyle=\partial_{r}^{2}+\frac{(2m-1)}{r}\partial_{r}+\frac{(m-1)^{2}}{r^{2}}+\frac{1}{r^{2}}\partial_{\theta}^{2}

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}_{m}\,r^{\alpha}

\displaystyle=(\alpha+m-1)^{2}\,r^{\alpha-2}.

Therefore, we get

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}_{m}\left(r^{\alpha}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a\,e^{in\theta}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=(\alpha+m-1)^{2}\,r^{\alpha-2}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a\,e^{in\theta}\right)-n^{2}\,r^{\alpha-2}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a\,e^{in\theta}\right)

\displaystyle=(\alpha+(m-1)+n)(\alpha+(m-1)-n)r^{\alpha-2}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a\,e^{in\theta}\right).

We deduce that

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}_{m}u_{1}

\displaystyle=\left(m+1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)\left(m-1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)r^{\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-1}\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{1}\,e^{i\theta}\right)

\displaystyle+\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-(m+1)\right)\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-(m-1)\right)r^{-1-\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{1}\,e^{i\theta}\right)

\displaystyle+(m^{2}-1)r^{m-2}\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(c_{1}\,e^{i\theta}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(m+1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)\left(m-1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)r^{\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-1}\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{1}\,e^{i\theta}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{16(m-1)}{\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}+(m-1)^{2}\right)\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}+(m+1)^{2}\right)}r^{-1-\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{1}\,e^{i\theta}\right)

\displaystyle+(m^{2}-1)\,r^{m-2}\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(c_{1}\,e^{i\theta}\right).

We deduce that

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}u_{1}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2\alpha}dx

\displaystyle=\pi\left(m+1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)^{2}\left(m-1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)^{2}|a_{1}|^{2}\int_{a}^{b}r^{2\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-1+2\alpha}\frac{dr}{b^{2\alpha}}

\displaystyle+\frac{256\pi(m-1)^{2}|b_{1}|^{2}}{\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}+(m-1)^{2}\right)^{2}\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}+(m+1)^{2}\right)^{2}}\int_{a}^{b}r^{-1-2\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}+2\alpha}\frac{dr}{b^{2\alpha}}

\displaystyle+\pi(m^{2}-1)^{2}|c_{1}|^{2}\int_{a}^{b}r^{2m-3+2\alpha}\frac{dr}{b^{2\alpha}}

\displaystyle-32\pi\frac{(m-1)\left(m+1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)\left(m-1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)}{\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}+(m-1)^{2}\right)\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}+(m+1)^{2}\right)}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{1}\overline{b_{1}}\right)\int_{a}^{b}r^{-1+2\alpha}\frac{dr}{b^{2\alpha}}

\displaystyle+2\pi(m^{2}-1)\left(m+1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)\left(m-1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{1}\overline{c_{1}}\right)\int_{a}^{b}r^{m-2+2\alpha+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}\frac{dr}{b^{2\alpha}}

\displaystyle-\frac{32\pi(m-1)(m^{2}-1)}{\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}+(m-1)^{2}\right)\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}+(m+1)^{2}\right)}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{1}\overline{c_{1}}\right)\int_{a}^{b}r^{m-2+2\alpha-\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}\frac{dr}{b^{2\alpha}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\pi\left(m+1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)^{2}\left(m-1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)^{2}}{2\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}+\alpha\right)}|a_{1}|^{2}b^{2\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}+\alpha\right)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{128\pi(m-1)^{2}|b_{1}|^{2}}{\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}+(m-1)^{2}\right)^{2}\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}+(m+1)^{2}\right)^{2}\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-\alpha\right)}

\displaystyle\times\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\alpha}\frac{1}{a^{2\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-\alpha\right)}\right)+\frac{\pi(m^{2}-1)^{2}}{2(m-1+\alpha)}|c_{1}|^{2}b^{2(m-1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-1+\alpha)}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{16\pi}{\alpha}\frac{(m-1)\left(m+1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)\left(m-1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)}{\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}+(m-1)^{2}\right)\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}+(m+1)^{2}\right)}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{1}\overline{b_{1}}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\alpha}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\alpha}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\pi\left(m^{2}-1\right)\left(m+1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)\left(m-1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)}{m-1+2\alpha+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{1}\overline{c_{1}}\right)b^{m-1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}

\displaystyle\times\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{m+2\alpha+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{32\pi(m-1)\left(m^{2}-1\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{1}\overline{c_{1}}\right)}{\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}+(m-1)^{2}\right)\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}+(m+1)^{2}\right)\left((m-1+2\alpha)-\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)}

\displaystyle\times\frac{1}{b^{\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-(m-1)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\alpha}\right),

where we used that
\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}<m-1+2\alpha
for all

\displaystyle\alpha>f(m)=\frac{2}{\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}+(m-1)^{2}}.

Since this latter function
f
of
m
is strictly decreasing (and converges to
0
as
m\rightarrow\infty
), we can always find a value
0<\alpha<1
for which the estimate will be satisfied since

\displaystyle f(3)=\frac{1}{2+\sqrt{2}}=0.2928932\cdots<1.

Now, we consider the following quantity

\displaystyle\frac{\left(m+1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)^{2}\left(m-1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)^{2}}{2\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}+\alpha\right)}|a_{1}|^{2}b^{2\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}+\alpha\right)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{(m^{2}-1)}{2(m-1+\alpha)}|c_{1}|^{2}b^{2(m-1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-1+\alpha)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\left(m^{2}-1\right)\left(m+1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)\left(m-1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)}{m-1+2\alpha+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{1}\overline{c_{1}}\right)b^{m-1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}

\displaystyle\times\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{m+2\alpha+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}\right).

In fact, to avoid redoing multiple times the same estimate, let
\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}>-1
,
a_{1},a_{2}\in\mathbb{C}
, and

n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}
, and define

\displaystyle f(r,\theta)

\displaystyle=r^{\alpha_{1}}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\mathfrak{a}_{1}e^{in\theta}\right)+r^{\alpha_{2}}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\mathfrak{a}_{2}\,e^{in\theta}\right)=\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\left(\mathfrak{a}_{1}r^{\alpha_{1}}+\mathfrak{a}_{2}r^{\alpha_{2}}\right)e^{in\theta}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left(\mathfrak{a}_{1}r^{\alpha_{1}}+\mathfrak{a}_{2}r^{\alpha_{2}}\right)e^{in\theta}+\frac{1}{2}\left(\overline{\mathfrak{a}_{1}}r^{\alpha_{1}}+\overline{\mathfrak{a}_{2}}r^{\alpha_{2}}\right)e^{-in\theta}.

We have

\displaystyle|f(r,\theta)|^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}|\mathfrak{a}_{1}r^{\alpha_{1}}+\mathfrak{a}_{2}r^{\alpha_{2}}|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\left(\mathfrak{a}_{1}r^{\alpha_{1}}+\mathfrak{a}_{2}r^{\alpha_{2}}\right)e^{2in\theta}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left(|\mathfrak{a}_{1}|^{2}r^{2\alpha_{1}}+|\mathfrak{a}_{2}|^{2}r^{2\alpha_{2}}+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\mathfrak{a}_{1}\overline{\mathfrak{a}_{2}}\right)r^{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}}\right)+\frac{1}{2}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\left(\mathfrak{a}_{1}r^{\alpha_{1}}+\mathfrak{a}_{2}r^{\alpha_{2}}\right)e^{2in\theta}\right).

As
n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}|f(x)|^{2}dx=\pi\int_{a}^{b}\left(|\mathfrak{a}_{1}|^{2}r^{2\alpha_{1}+1}+|\mathfrak{a}_{2}|^{2}r^{2\alpha_{2}+1}+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\mathfrak{a}_{1}\overline{\mathfrak{a}_{2}}\right)r^{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+1}\right)dr

\displaystyle=\pi\left(\frac{|\mathfrak{a}_{1}|^{2}}{2(\alpha_{1}+1)}b^{2(\alpha_{1}+1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha_{1}+1)}\right)+\frac{|\mathfrak{a}_{2}|^{2}}{2(\alpha_{2}+1)}b^{2(\alpha_{2}+1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha_{2}+1)}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\mathfrak{a}_{1}\overline{\mathfrak{a}_{2}}\right)}{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+2}b^{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+2}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+2}\right)\right).

Using the elementary inequality

\displaystyle 2ab\geq-\dfrac{1}{2}a^{2}-2b^{2},

we deduce that

\displaystyle\frac{|\mathfrak{a}_{1}|^{2}}{2(\alpha_{1}+1)}b^{2(\alpha_{1}+1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha_{1}+1)}\right)+\frac{|\mathfrak{a}_{2}|^{2}}{2(\alpha_{2}+1)}b^{2(\alpha_{2}+1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha_{2}+1)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\mathfrak{a}_{1}\overline{\mathfrak{a}_{2}}\right)}{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+2}b^{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+2}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|\mathfrak{a}_{1}|^{2}}{2(\alpha_{1}+1)}b^{2(\alpha_{1}+1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha_{1}+1)}\right)-\frac{2(\alpha_{2}+1)}{(\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+2)^{2}}|\mathfrak{a}_{1}|^{2}b^{2(\alpha_{1}+1)}\frac{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+2}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha_{2}+1)}}.

Now, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2(\alpha_{1}+1)}-\frac{2(\alpha_{2}+1)}{(\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+2)}=\frac{(\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+2)^{2}-4(\alpha_{1}+1)(\alpha_{2}+1)}{2(\alpha_{1}+1)(\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+2)}=\frac{(\alpha_{1}-\alpha_{2})^{2}}{2(\alpha_{1}+1)(\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+1)^{2}}.

Therefore, we get

\displaystyle\frac{|\mathfrak{a}_{1}|^{2}}{2(\alpha_{1}+1)}b^{2(\alpha_{1}+1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha_{1}+1)}\right)-\frac{2(\alpha_{2}+1)}{(\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+2)^{2}}|\mathfrak{a}_{1}|^{2}b^{2(\alpha_{1}+1)}\frac{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+2}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha_{2}+1)}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha_{2}+1)}}\frac{|\mathfrak{a}_{1}|^{2}}{2(\alpha_{1}+1)}b^{2(\alpha_{1}+1)}\left(\frac{(\alpha_{1}-\alpha_{2})^{2}}{(\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+2)^{2}}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha_{1}+1)}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha_{2}+1)}\right).

Assuming that
\alpha_{1}\neq\alpha_{2}
(in which case the estimate is trivial since
\mathrm{Re}\,(\mathfrak{a}_{1}\overline{\mathfrak{a}_{2}})=|\mathfrak{a}_{1}|^{2}\geq 0
) and that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{\min\left\{\alpha_{1}+1,\alpha_{2}+1\right\}}\log\left(\frac{2(\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+2)}{|\alpha_{1}-\alpha_{2}|}\right),

(3.12)

we get

\displaystyle\frac{|\mathfrak{a}_{1}|^{2}}{2(\alpha_{1}+1)}b^{2(\alpha_{1}+1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha_{1}+1)}\right)+\frac{|\mathfrak{a}_{2}|^{2}}{2(\alpha_{2}+1)}b^{2(\alpha_{2}+1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha_{2}+1)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\mathfrak{a}_{1}\overline{\mathfrak{a}_{2}}\right)}{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+2}b^{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+2}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{(\alpha_{1}-\alpha_{2})^{2}}{4(\alpha_{1}+1)(\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+2)^{2}}|\mathfrak{a}_{1}|^{2}b^{2(\alpha_{1}+1)}.

Therefore, using a symmetrical estimate for
\mathfrak{a}_{2}
, we deduce that provided that provided that (
3.12
) holds, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}|f(x)|^{2}dx\geq\frac{\pi(\alpha_{1}-\alpha_{2})^{2}}{8(\alpha_{1}+1)(\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+2)^{2}}|\mathfrak{a}_{1}|^{2}b^{2(\alpha_{1}+1)}+\frac{\pi(\alpha_{1}-\alpha_{2})^{2}}{8(\alpha_{2}+1)(\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+2)^{2}}|\mathfrak{a}_{2}|^{2}b^{2(\alpha_{2}+1)}.

(3.13)

Applying this estimate to

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &\alpha_{1}=\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-1+\alpha\qquad&&\mathfrak{a}_{1}=\left(m+1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)\left(m-1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)a_{1}\\
&\alpha_{2}=m-2+\alpha\qquad&&\mathfrak{a_{2}}=(m^{2}-1)c_{1}\end{aligned}\right.

(3.14)

and
n=1
, we deduce that

\displaystyle\frac{\left(m+1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)^{2}\left(m-1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)^{2}}{2\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}+\alpha\right)}|a_{1}|^{2}b^{2\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}+\alpha\right)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{(m^{2}-1)}{2(m-1+\alpha)}|c_{1}|^{2}b^{2(m-1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-1+\alpha)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\left(m^{2}-1\right)\left(m+1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)\left(m-1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)}{m-1+2\alpha+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{1}\overline{c_{1}}\right)b^{m-1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}

\displaystyle\times\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{m+2\alpha+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-(m-1)\right)^{2}\left(m+1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)\left(m-1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)}{8\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}+\alpha\right)(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}+(m-1)+2\alpha)}|a_{1}|^{2}b^{2\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}

\displaystyle+\frac{(m^{2}-1)\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-(m-1)\right)^{2}}{8(m-1+\alpha)\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}+(m-1)+2\alpha\right)}|c_{1}|^{2}b^{2(m-1)}.

(3.15)

The other crossed terms are easily estimated. Define

\displaystyle\alpha_{3}=-1-\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}+\alpha\qquad\mathfrak{a}_{3}=\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-(m+1)\right)\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}}-(m-1)\right)b_{1}.

(3.16)

We estimate

\displaystyle\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\mathfrak{a}_{1}\mathfrak{a}_{3}\right)}{\alpha}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\alpha}\geq-\frac{|\mathfrak{a}_{1}|^{2}}{\delta\alpha^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\alpha}a^{2(-\alpha_{3}-1)}-\delta|\mathfrak{a}_{3}|^{2}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\alpha}\frac{1}{a^{2(-\alpha_{3}-1)}},

while

\displaystyle\frac{2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\mathfrak{a}_{2}\mathfrak{a}_{3}\right)}{(m-1+2\alpha)-\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}b^{m-1}\frac{1}{b^{\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}}

\displaystyle\geq-\varepsilon|\mathfrak{a}_{2}|^{2}b^{2(m-1)}-\frac{|\mathfrak{a}_{3}|^{2}}{\varepsilon\left(m-1+2\alpha-\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)^{2}}\frac{1}{b^{2\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}}.

We can take

\displaystyle\varepsilon=\frac{(\alpha_{1}-\alpha_{2})^{2}}{16(\alpha_{2}+1)(\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+2)},

and then we simply estimate

\displaystyle\frac{|\mathfrak{a}_{3}|^{2}}{2\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-\alpha\right)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\alpha}\frac{1}{a^{2(-\alpha_{3}-1)}}-\delta|\mathfrak{a}_{3}|^{2}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\alpha}\frac{1}{a^{2(-\alpha_{3}-1)}}

\displaystyle-\frac{|\mathfrak{a}_{3}|^{2}}{\varepsilon\left(m-1+2\alpha-\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)^{2}}\frac{1}{b^{2\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|a_{3}|^{2}}{4\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-\alpha\right)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\alpha}\frac{1}{a^{2(-\alpha_{3}-1)}}\left(1-4\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-\alpha\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(-\alpha_{3}-1-\alpha)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-(m+1)\right)^{2}\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-(m-1)\right)^{2}}{8\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-\alpha\right)}|b_{1}|^{2}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\alpha}\frac{1}{a^{2\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}}

if we take

\displaystyle\delta=\frac{1}{4\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-\alpha\right)},

and impose

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-\alpha\right)}\log\left(8\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-\alpha\right)\right).

Notice that this imposes the condition
\alpha<\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}
, which is satisfied since
m\geq 3
provided that
\alpha<2\sqrt{2}
. Finally we estimate

\displaystyle\frac{(\alpha_{1}-\alpha_{2})^{2}}{8(\alpha_{1}+1)(\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+2)^{2}}|\mathfrak{a}_{1}|^{2}b^{2(\alpha_{1}+1)}-\frac{|\mathfrak{a}_{1}|^{2}}{\delta\alpha^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\alpha}a^{2(\alpha_{1}+1)}\geq\frac{(\alpha_{1}-\alpha_{2})^{2}}{16(\alpha_{1}+1)(\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+2)^{2}}|\mathfrak{a}_{1}|^{2}b^{2(\alpha_{1}+1)}

provided that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}\log\left(\frac{8(\alpha_{1}+1)(\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+2)^{2}}{\delta\alpha^{2}(\alpha_{1}-\alpha_{2})^{2}}\right).

Finally, we deduce that (assuming that all previous conditions on the conformal class are satisfied)

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}u_{1}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)dx

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\pi\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-(m-1)\right)^{2}\left(m+1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)\left(m-1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)}{8\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}+\alpha\right)(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}+(m-1)+2\alpha)}|a_{1}|^{2}b^{2\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\pi\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-(m+1)\right)^{2}\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-(m-1)\right)^{2}}{8\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-\alpha\right)}|b_{1}|^{2}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\alpha}\frac{1}{a^{2\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\pi(m^{2}-1)\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-(m-1)\right)^{2}}{8(m-1+\alpha)\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}+(m-1)+2\alpha\right)}|c_{1}|^{2}b^{2(m-1)}.

(3.17)

Writing to simplify

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}_{m}u_{1}=r^{\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-1}\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\mathfrak{a}_{1}e^{i\theta}\right)+r^{-1-\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\mathfrak{a}_{3}e^{i\theta}\right)+r^{m-2}\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\mathfrak{a}_{2}e^{i\theta}\right).

Therefore, we get

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}u_{1}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2\alpha}dx\geq\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Omega}\left(r^{-1-\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\mathfrak{a}_{3}e^{i\theta}\right)\right)^{2}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2\alpha}dx

\displaystyle-4\int_{\Omega}\left(r^{\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-1}\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\mathfrak{a}_{1}e^{i\theta}\right)\right)^{2}dx-4\int_{\Omega}\left(r^{m-2}\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\mathfrak{a}_{2}e^{i\theta}\right)\right)^{2}dx

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\pi|\mathfrak{a}_{3}|^{2}}{4\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-\alpha\right)}\frac{1}{a^{2\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-\alpha\right)}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{2\pi|\mathfrak{a}_{1}|^{2}}{\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}b^{2\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}}}-\frac{2\pi|\mathfrak{a}_{2}|^{2}}{m-1}b^{2(m-1)}.

Therefore, using the estimate of (
3
), we deduce that there exists a constant
\Gamma_{1,m}<\infty
such that

\displaystyle\frac{\pi\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-(m-1)\right)^{2}\left(m+1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)\left(m-1+\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}\right)}{8\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}+\alpha\right)(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}+(m-1)+2\alpha)}|a_{1}|^{2}b^{2\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\pi\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-(m+1)\right)^{2}\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-(m-1)\right)^{2}}{8\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-\alpha\right)}|b_{1}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\pi(m^{2}-1)\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}-(m-1)\right)^{2}}{8(m-1+\alpha)\left(\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}+(m-1)+2\alpha\right)}|c_{1}|^{2}b^{2(m-1)}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}u_{1}\right)^{2}\left(\Gamma_{1,m}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2\alpha}+\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2\alpha}\right)dx.

(3.18)

Then, we have for all
\beta>0
the estimate

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\frac{1}{|x|^{4}}\left(r^{2-m}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(d_{1}e^{i\theta}\right)\right)^{2}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{4\beta}dx=\frac{\pi}{2(m-1+2\beta)}|d_{1}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(m-1)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(m-1+2\beta)}\right)dx.

Therefore, using the previous estimate (
3
), we deduce that there exists
\Gamma_{2,m}<\infty
such that for all
0<\alpha<\sqrt{(m-1)^{2}+4}
and
\beta>0

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\frac{1}{|x|^{2}}\left|\partial_{r}u_{1}+\frac{m-1}{r}u_{1}\right|^{2}dx\leq\Gamma_{2,m}\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}u_{1}\right)^{2}\left(\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2\alpha}+\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2\alpha}\right)dx+\int_{\Omega}\frac{u_{1}^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{4\beta}dx.

Step 4.
The remaining Fourier modes for
|n|\geq 2
.
For all
n\geq 2
, we have

\displaystyle u_{n}=r^{1+\alpha_{m,n}}\cos\left(\beta_{m,n}r\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}e^{in\theta}\right)+r^{1+\alpha_{m,n}}\sin\left(\beta_{m,n}r\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}e^{in\theta}\right)

\displaystyle+r^{1-\alpha_{m,n}}\cos\left(\beta_{m,n}r\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(c_{n}e^{in\theta}\right)+r^{1-\alpha_{m,n}}\sin\left(\beta_{m,n}r\right)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(d_{n}e^{in\theta}\right).

(3.19)

We deduce that

\displaystyle 2\,\mathrm{Rad}(u_{n}^{2})=r^{2+2\alpha_{m,n}}\cos^{2}(\beta_{m,n}r)|a_{n}|^{2}+r^{2+2\alpha_{m,n}}\sin^{2}(\beta_{m,n}r)|b_{n}|^{2}

\displaystyle+r^{2-2\alpha_{m,n}}\cos^{2}(\beta_{m,n}r)|c_{n}|^{2}+r^{2-2\alpha_{m,n}}\sin^{2}(\beta_{m,n}r)|d_{n}|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\,r^{2+2\alpha_{m,n}}\cos(\beta_{m,n}r)\sin(\beta_{m,n}r)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)+2\,r^{2}\cos^{2}(\beta_{m,n}r)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{c_{n}}\right)+2\,r^{2}\cos(\beta_{m,n}r)\sin(\beta_{m,n}r)

\displaystyle+2\,r^{2}\cos(\beta_{m,n}r)\sin(\beta_{m,n}r)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}\overline{c_{n}}\right)+2\,r^{2}\sin^{2}(\beta_{m,n}r)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}\overline{d_{n}}\right)

\displaystyle+r^{2-2\alpha_{m,n}}\cos(\beta_{m,n}r)\sin(\beta_{m,n}r)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(c_{n}\overline{d_{n}}\right).

(3.20)

Therefore, it comes down to estimating the
L^{2}
norm of the following function

\displaystyle f(r,\theta)=r^{\alpha}\left(\mathfrak{a}_{1}\cos(\beta r)+\mathfrak{a}_{2}\sin(\beta r)\right)

which is given by

\displaystyle|f(r,\theta)|^{2}

\displaystyle=r^{2\alpha}\left|\mathfrak{a}_{1}\cos(\beta r)+\mathfrak{a}_{2}\sin(\beta r)\right|^{2}

\displaystyle=r^{2\alpha}\left(|\mathfrak{a}_{1}|^{2}\cos^{2}(\beta r)+|\mathfrak{a}_{2}|^{2}\sin^{2}(\beta r)+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\mathfrak{a}_{1}\overline{\mathfrak{a}_{2}}\right)\cos(\beta r)\sin(\beta r)\right)

on
\Omega=B_{b}\setminus\overline{B}_{a}(0)
, where we can take
b
as small as we want. In such a way that

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{2}\leq\cos(\beta r)\leq 1

\displaystyle\dfrac{\beta r}{2}\leq\sin(\beta r)\leq\beta r.

The difficulty is that the functions
r^{2\alpha}\cos(\beta r),r^{2\alpha}\sin(\beta r)
do not possess primitives in terms of elementary functions. Now, we will use ideas coming from the estimates involving solutions of
\mathscr{L}_{m}^{\ast}\mathscr{L}_{m}u=0
. Assuming without loss of generality that
\mathfrak{a_{1}}\mathfrak{a}_{2}\neq 0
, write

\displaystyle f_{1}(r,\theta)=\frac{\mathfrak{a_{1}}}{|\mathfrak{a}_{1}|}r^{\alpha}\cos(\beta\,r)

\displaystyle f_{2}(r,\theta)=\frac{\mathfrak{a_{2}}}{|\mathfrak{a}_{2}|}r^{\alpha}\sin(\beta\,r).

We deduce by Lemma
2.5
that

\displaystyle\frac{|\mathfrak{a}_{1}||\mathfrak{a}_{2}|}{2}\int_{\Omega\times\Omega}\left|\det\begin{pmatrix}f_{1}(x)&f_{2}(x)\\
f_{1}(y)&f_{2}(y)\end{pmatrix}\right|^{2}dx\,dy\leq\left\|f_{1}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\left\|f_{2}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\int_{\Omega}||\mathfrak{a}_{1}|f_{1}+|\mathfrak{a}_{2}|f_{2}|^{2}dx

\displaystyle=\left\|f_{1}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\left\|f_{2}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\int_{\Omega}|f|^{2}dx.

If
\alpha>-1
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|f_{1}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}=2\pi\int_{a}^{b}r^{2\alpha+1}\cos^{2}(\beta r)dr\leq\frac{\pi}{\alpha+1}b^{2(\alpha+1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha+1)}\right).

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle\left\|f_{2}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}=2\pi\int_{a}^{b}r^{2\alpha+1}\sin^{2}(\beta r)dr\leq\frac{\pi\beta^{2}}{\alpha+2}b^{2(\alpha+2)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha+2)}\right).

Therefore, we get

\displaystyle\left\|f_{1}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\left\|f_{2}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\leq\frac{\pi\,\beta}{\sqrt{(\alpha+1)(\alpha+2)}}b^{2\alpha+3}.

Then, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega\times\Omega}\left|\det\begin{pmatrix}f_{1}(x)&f_{2}(x)\\
f_{1}(y)&f_{2}(y)\end{pmatrix}\right|^{2}dx\,dy

\displaystyle=4\pi^{2}\int_{a}^{b}\int_{a}^{b}\left(r^{\alpha}\cos(\beta r)s^{\alpha}\sin(\beta s)-s^{\alpha}\cos(\beta s)r^{\alpha}\sin(\beta r)\right)^{2}rs\,dr\,ds

\displaystyle=4\pi^{2}\int_{a}^{b}\int_{a}^{b}\left(\cos(\beta r)\sin(\beta s)-\cos(\beta s)\sin(\beta r)\right)^{2}r^{2\alpha+1}s^{2\alpha+1}dr\,ds

\displaystyle=4\pi^{2}\int_{a}^{b}\int_{a}^{b}\sin^{2}\left(\beta(r-s)\right)r^{2\alpha+1}s^{2\alpha+1}dr\,ds

\displaystyle\geq\pi^{2}\beta^{2}\int_{a}^{b}\int_{a}^{b}(r-s)^{2}r^{2\alpha+1}s^{2\alpha+1}dr\,ds.

(3.21)

We have by Fubini’s theorem

\displaystyle\int_{a}^{b}\int_{a}^{b}(r-s)^{2}r^{2\alpha+1}s^{2\alpha+1}dr\,ds=2\left(\int_{a}^{b}r^{2\alpha+3}dr\right)\left(\int_{a}^{b}s^{2\alpha+1}ds\right)-2\left(\int_{a}^{b}r^{2\alpha+2}dr\right)\left(\int_{a}^{b}s^{2\alpha+2}ds\right)

\displaystyle=2\left(\frac{b^{2(\alpha+2)}}{2(\alpha+2)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha+2)}\right)\right)\left(\frac{b^{2(\alpha+1)}}{2(\alpha+1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha+1)}\right)\right)-2\left(\frac{b^{2\alpha+3}}{2\alpha+3}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\alpha+3}\right)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2(\alpha+1)(\alpha+2)(2\alpha+3)}b^{2(2\alpha+3)}\left(1-2(2\alpha+3)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha+1)}\right).

Assuming that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2(\alpha+1)}\log\left(4(2\alpha+3)\right),

we deduce that

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega\times\Omega}\left|\det\begin{pmatrix}f_{1}(x)&f_{2}(x)\\
f_{1}(y)&f_{2}(y)\end{pmatrix}\right|^{2}dx\,dy\geq\frac{\pi^{2}\beta^{2}}{4(\alpha+1)(\alpha+2)(2\alpha+3)}b^{2(2\alpha+3)}.

In the end, we obtain the estimate

\displaystyle\frac{|\mathfrak{a_{1}}||\mathfrak{a}_{2}|}{2}\frac{\pi^{2}\beta^{2}}{4(\alpha+1)(\alpha+2)(2\alpha+3)}b^{2(2\alpha+3)}\leq\frac{\pi\beta}{\sqrt{(\alpha+1)(\alpha+2)}}b^{2\alpha+3}\int_{\Omega}|f|^{2}dx,

which finally yields

\displaystyle|\mathfrak{a}_{1}||\mathfrak{a}_{2}|b^{2\alpha+3}\leq\frac{8\sqrt{(\alpha+1)(\alpha+2)}(2\alpha+3)}{\pi\beta}\int_{\Omega}|f|^{2}dx.

(3.22)

Then, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}|f|^{2}dx=2\pi\int_{a}^{b}\left(|\mathfrak{a}_{1}|^{2}r^{2\alpha+1}\cos^{2}(\beta r)+|\mathfrak{a}_{2}|^{2}r^{2\alpha+1}\sin^{2}(\beta r)+2\,\mathrm{Re}\,(\mathfrak{a}_{1}\overline{\mathfrak{a}_{2}})r^{2\alpha+1}\cos(\beta r)\sin(\beta r)\right)dr

\displaystyle\geq\pi\int_{a}^{b}|\mathfrak{a}_{1}|^{2}r^{2\alpha+1}dr+2\pi\beta^{2}\int_{a}^{b}|\mathfrak{a}_{2}|^{2}r^{2\alpha+3}dr-2\left|\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\mathfrak{a}_{1}\overline{\mathfrak{a}_{2}}\right)\right|\int_{a}^{b}r^{2\alpha+1}\cos(\beta r)\sin(\beta r)dr

\displaystyle\geq\pi\left(\int_{a}^{b}|\mathfrak{a}_{1}|^{2}r^{2\alpha+1}dr+2\beta^{2}\int_{a}^{b}|\mathfrak{a}_{2}|^{2}r^{2\alpha+3}dr-2\beta\left|\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\mathfrak{a}_{1}\overline{\mathfrak{a}_{2}}\right)\right|\int_{a}^{b}r^{2\alpha+2}dr\right)

\displaystyle=\pi\left(\frac{1}{2(\alpha+1)}|\mathfrak{a}_{1}|^{2}b^{2(\alpha+1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha+1)}\right)+\frac{\beta^{2}}{\alpha+2}|\mathfrak{a}_{2}|^{2}b^{2(\alpha+2)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha+2)}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\frac{2\beta\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\mathfrak{a}_{1}\overline{\mathfrak{a}_{2}}\right)}{2\alpha+3}b^{2\alpha+3}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\alpha+3}\right)\right).

Therefore, (
3.22
) implies that

\displaystyle\frac{\pi}{2(\alpha+1)}|\mathfrak{a}_{1}|^{2}b^{2(\alpha+1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha+1)}\right)+\frac{\pi\beta^{2}}{\alpha+2}|\mathfrak{a}_{2}|^{2}b^{2(\alpha+2)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha+2)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\int_{\Omega}|f|^{2}dx+\frac{2\pi\beta\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\mathfrak{a}_{1}\overline{\mathfrak{a}_{2}}\right)}{2\alpha+3}b^{2\alpha+3}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\alpha+3}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\int_{\Omega}|f|^{2}dx+\frac{2\pi\beta|\mathfrak{a}_{1}||\mathfrak{a}_{2}|}{2\alpha+3}b^{2\alpha+3}\leq\left(1+16\sqrt{(\alpha+1)(\alpha+2)}\right)\int_{\Omega}|f|^{2}dx.

(3.23)

Now, for
\alpha<-2
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{a}^{b}\int_{a}^{b}(r-s)^{2}r^{-2\alpha+1}s^{-2\alpha+1}drds=2\left(\frac{1}{2(\alpha-2)}\frac{1}{a^{2(\alpha-2)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha-2)}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\times\left(\frac{1}{2(\alpha-1)}\frac{1}{a^{2(\alpha-1)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha-1)}\right)\right)-2\left(\frac{1}{2\alpha-3}\frac{1}{a^{2\alpha-3}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\alpha-3}\right)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2(\alpha-1)(\alpha-2)(2\alpha-3)}\frac{1}{a^{2(2\alpha-3)}}\left(1-2(2\alpha-3)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha-2)}\right),

and this implies that if

\displaystyle g(r,\theta)

\displaystyle=r^{-\alpha}\left(\mathfrak{a}_{1}\cos(\beta r)+\mathfrak{a}_{2}\sin(\beta r)\right)

\displaystyle=|\mathfrak{a}_{1}|g_{1}(r,\theta)+|\mathfrak{a}_{2}|g_{2}(r,\theta),

then

\displaystyle\left\|g_{1}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}=2\pi\int_{a}^{b}r^{-2\alpha+1}\cos^{2}(\beta r)dr\leq\frac{\pi}{\alpha-1}\frac{1}{a^{2(\alpha-1)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha-1)}\right)

and

\displaystyle\left\|g_{2}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}\leq\frac{\pi\beta^{2}}{\alpha-2}\frac{1}{a^{2(\alpha-2)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha-1)}\right)

and we get

\displaystyle|\mathfrak{a}_{1}||\mathfrak{a}_{2}|\frac{1}{a^{2\alpha-3}}\leq\frac{8\sqrt{(\alpha-1)(\alpha-2)}(2\alpha-3)}{\pi\beta}\int_{\Omega}|g|^{2}dx.

(3.24)

Therefore, we deduce by the same argument as in (
3
)

\displaystyle\frac{\pi}{2(\alpha-1)}|\mathfrak{a}_{1}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(\alpha-1)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha-1)}\right)+\frac{\pi\beta^{2}}{\alpha-2}|\mathfrak{a}_{2}|^{2}b^{2(\alpha-2)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha-2)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+16\sqrt{(\alpha-1)(\alpha-2)}\right)\int_{\Omega}|g|^{2}dx.

(3.25)

The argument allows us to overcome the fact that
r^{\alpha}\cos(\beta r)
has no primitive expressible in terms of elementary functions for all
\alpha\neq 0
. Notice that a trivial argument using the inequality
\cos^{2}(\beta r)\geq 1-\beta^{2}r^{2}
would not be of much help since we would get a factor of the form
1-\beta^{2}b^{2}
which would make the direct application of Cauchy’s inequality fail.
Now, we rewrite identity (
3
) as

\displaystyle 2\,\mathrm{Rad}(u_{n}^{2})

\displaystyle=\left|r^{1+\alpha_{m,n}}\left(a_{n}\cos(\beta_{m,n}r)+b_{n}\sin(\beta_{m,n}r)\right)+r^{1-\alpha_{m,n}}\left(c_{n}\,\cos(\beta_{m,n}r)+d_{n}\sin(\beta_{m,n}r)\right)\right|^{2}

\displaystyle=\left|r^{1+\alpha_{m,n}}\left(a_{n}\cos(\beta_{m,n}r)+b_{n}\sin(\beta_{m,n}r)\right)\right|^{2}+\left|r^{1-\alpha_{m,n}}\left(c_{n}\,\cos(\beta_{m,n}r)+d_{n}\sin(\beta_{m,n}r)\right)\right|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\,r^{2}\cos^{2}(\beta_{m,n}r)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{c_{n}}\right)+2\,r^{2}\cos(\beta_{m,n}r)\sin(\beta_{m,n}r)

\displaystyle+2\,r^{2}\cos(\beta_{m,n}r)\sin(\beta_{m,n}r)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}\overline{c_{n}}\right)+2\,r^{2}\sin^{2}(\beta_{m,n}r)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}\overline{d_{n}}\right).

Recall that

\displaystyle\alpha_{m,n}=\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{m,n}+(m-1)^{2}+n^{2}+1}{2}},

where

\displaystyle\mu_{m,n}=\sqrt{n^{4}+\left(6m(m-2)+4\right)n^{2}+m^{2}(m-2)^{2}}>\sqrt{n^{4}+2m(m-2)n^{2}+m^{2}(m-2)^{2}}=n^{2}+m(m-2).

Therefore, we get

\displaystyle\alpha_{m,n}>\sqrt{\frac{n^{2}+m(m-2)+(m-1)^{2}+n^{2}+1}{2}}=\sqrt{n^{2}+(m-1)^{2}}.

We have (replacing respectively

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\frac{u_{n}^{2}}{|x|^{4}}|x|^{2\alpha}dx=2\pi\int_{a}^{b}\bigg\{\left|r^{\alpha_{m,n}-1+\alpha}\left(a_{n}\cos(\beta_{m,n}r)+b_{n}\sin(\beta_{m,n}r)\right)\right|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left|r^{\alpha_{m,n}-1+\alpha}\left(c_{n}\,\cos(\beta_{m,n}r)+d_{n}\sin(\beta_{m,n}r)\right)\right|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\,r^{-2+2\alpha}\cos^{2}(\beta_{m,n}r)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{c_{n}}\right)+2\,r^{-2+2\alpha}\cos(\beta_{m,n}r)\sin(\beta_{m,n}r)

\displaystyle+2\,r^{-2+2\alpha}\cos(\beta_{m,n}r)\sin(\beta_{m,n}r)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}\overline{c_{n}}\right)+2\,r^{-2+2\alpha}\sin^{2}(\beta_{m,n}r)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(b_{n}\overline{d_{n}}\right)\bigg\}r\,dr

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{1+16\sqrt{(\alpha_{m,n}+\alpha)(\alpha_{m,n}+1+\alpha)}}\left(\frac{\pi}{2(\alpha_{m,n}+\alpha)}b^{2(\alpha_{m,n}+\alpha)}|a_{n}|^{2}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha_{m,n}+\alpha)}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{\pi\beta_{m,n}^{2}}{\alpha_{m,n}+1+\alpha}|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(\alpha_{m,n}+1+\alpha)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha_{m,n}+1+\alpha)}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{1+16\sqrt{(\alpha_{m,n}-\alpha)}(\alpha_{m,n}-1-\alpha)}\left(\frac{\pi}{2(\alpha_{m,n}-\alpha)}|c_{n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(\alpha_{m,n}-\alpha)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha_{m,n}-\alpha)}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{\pi\beta_{m,n}^{2}}{\alpha_{m,n}-1-\alpha}|d_{n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(\alpha_{m,n}-1-\alpha)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha_{m,n}-1-\alpha)}\right)\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{2\pi}{|\alpha|}\left(\left|\mathrm{Re}(a_{n}\overline{c_{n}})\right|+\left|\mathrm{Re}(a_{n}\overline{d_{n}})\right|+\left|\mathrm{Re}(b_{n}\overline{c_{n}})\right|+\left|\mathrm{Re}(b_{n}\overline{d_{n}})\right|\right)\left|b^{2\alpha}-a^{2\alpha}\right|.

The crossed terms are easily estimated by Cauchy’s inequality as in the proof of Theorem REF. Let us only treat the case
\alpha>0
. Then, we have

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\alpha}\left|\mathrm{Re}(a_{n}\overline{c_{n}})\right|b^{2\alpha}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\alpha}\right)\leq\varepsilon|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(\alpha_{m,n}+\alpha)}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon\alpha^{2}}|c_{n}|^{2}\frac{1}{b^{2(\alpha_{m,n}-\alpha)}}

so we see that for all

and

, there exists

(independent of

) such that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq M(\varepsilon,\alpha)

then

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1+16\sqrt{(\alpha_{m,n}+\alpha)(\alpha_{m,n}+1+\alpha)}}\frac{\pi}{2(\alpha_{m,n}+\alpha)}b^{2(\alpha_{m,n}+\alpha)}|a_{n}|^{2}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha_{m,n}+\alpha)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{1+16\sqrt{(\alpha_{m,n}-\alpha)}(\alpha_{m,n}-1-\alpha)}\frac{\pi}{2(\alpha_{m,n}-\alpha)}|c_{n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(\alpha_{m,n}-\alpha)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha_{m,n}-\alpha)}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{\alpha}\left|\mathrm{Re}(a_{n}\overline{c_{n}})\right|b^{2\alpha}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\alpha}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\left(\frac{1}{1+16\sqrt{(\alpha_{m,n}+\alpha)(\alpha_{m,n}+1+\alpha)}}\frac{\pi}{2(\alpha_{m,n}+\alpha)}-\varepsilon\right)b^{2(\alpha_{m,n}+\alpha)}|a_{n}|^{2}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha_{m,n}+\alpha)}\right)

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{1}{1+16\sqrt{(\alpha_{m,n}-\alpha)}(\alpha_{m,n}-1-\alpha)}-\delta\right)\frac{\pi}{2(\alpha_{m,n}-\alpha)}|c_{n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(\alpha_{m,n}-\alpha)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha_{m,n}-\alpha)}\right)

Applying the same argument to the three remaining terms, we finally deduce that there exists
A=A(\alpha)
such that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq A(\alpha),

then

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\frac{u_{n}^{2}}{|x|^{4}}|x|^{2\alpha}dx\geq\frac{1}{1+16\sqrt{(\alpha_{m,n}+\alpha)(\alpha_{m,n}+1+\alpha)}}\left(\frac{\pi}{2(\alpha_{m,n}+\alpha)}b^{2(\alpha_{m,n}+\alpha)}|a_{n}|^{2}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha_{m,n}+\alpha)}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{\pi\beta_{m,n}^{2}}{\alpha_{m,n}+1+\alpha}|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(\alpha_{m,n}+1+\alpha)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha_{m,n}+1+\alpha)}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{1+16\sqrt{(\alpha_{m,n}-\alpha)}(\alpha_{m,n}-1-\alpha)}\left(\frac{\pi}{2(\alpha_{m,n}-\alpha)}|c_{n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(\alpha_{m,n}-\alpha)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha_{m,n}-\alpha)}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{\pi\beta_{m,n}^{2}}{\alpha_{m,n}-1-\alpha}|d_{n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(\alpha_{m,n}-1-\alpha)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\alpha_{m,n}-1-\alpha)}\right)\right).

(3.26)

Therefore, we need only estimate the weighted

\mathscr{L}_{m}u
or
\mathfrak{L}_{m}u
for

large enough since we need to capture the linear growth in
